===== 3. CHANGE PASSWORD ENDPOINT =====
     1	<?php
     2	require_once __DIR__ . '/../php-func/user.valid.php';
     3	/*
     4	Document : php-crud/change.passCRD.php
     5	Description : Change Password Handler - Fixed
     6	*/
     7	if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_NONE) { session_start(); }
     8	include_once "../php-class/crudClass.php";
     9	include_once "../php-func/function.all.php";
    10	
    11	// Validasi session
    12	if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || empty($_SESSION['user_id'])) {
    13	    echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Session expired.']);
    14	    exit;
    15	}
    16	
    17	// Endpoint ubah password hanya boleh menyentuh akun milik sesi aktif.
    18	$userId = (int)$_SESSION['user_id'];
    19	$task = $_POST['task'] ?? '';
    20	
    21	switch ($task) {
    22	    case 'read_edit':
    23	        try {
    24	            $db = new crud();
    25	            $field = "
    26	                s.id,s.login_name,s.employee_id,s.active,'' as password,'' as confirm_password,e.employee_name,e.gender,e.from_date,
    27					d.department_name,d.status_name
    28	            ";
    29	            $table = "m_user_system s
    30	                LEFT JOIN m_employee e ON e.id = s.employee_id
    31	                LEFT JOIN view_employee_depart_status d ON COALESCE(d.id, 0) = COALESCE(e.id, 0)";
    32	            $where = "s.id = " . $userId;
    33	            
    34	            $db->select($table, $field, $where);
    35	            echo $db->getResult();
    36	        } catch (Exception $e) {
    37	            error_log("[ChangePass] read_edit error: " . $e->getMessage());
    38	            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Gagal memuat data.']);
    39	        }
    40	        break;
    41	
    42	    case 'update':
    43	        try {
    44	            $db = new crud();
    45	            
    46	            $newPassword = trim($_POST['password'] ?? '');
    47	            $confirmPassword = trim($_POST['confirm_password'] ?? '');
    48	            
    49	            // Validasi
    50	            if (empty($newPassword) || empty($confirmPassword)) {
    51	                echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Password wajib diisi.']);
    52	                exit;
    53	            }
    54	            
    55	            if ($newPassword !== $confirmPassword) {
    56	                echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Password tidak cocok.']);
    57	                exit;
    58	            }
    59	            
    60	            if (strlen($newPassword) < 6) {
    61	                echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Password minimal 6 karakter.']);
    62	                exit;
    63	            }
    64	            
    65	            if (strlen($newPassword) > 50) {
    66	                echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Password maksimal 50 karakter.']);
    67	                exit;
    68	            }
    69	            
    70	            // [FIX] Hash dengan bcrypt (lebih aman dari BASE64)
    71	            $hashedPassword = password_hash($newPassword, PASSWORD_DEFAULT, ['cost' => 12]);
    72	            
    73	            // [FIX] Update by user_id, bukan employee_id
    74	            $updateStmt = $db->prepare("
    75	                UPDATE m_user_system 
    76	                SET password = :password
    77	                WHERE id = :user_id
    78	            ");
    79	            
    80	            $updateStmt->execute([
    81	                ':password' => $hashedPassword,
    82	                ':user_id' => $userId
    83	            ]);
    84	            
    85	            if ($updateStmt->rowCount() === 0) {
    86	                throw new Exception('User tidak ditemukan.');
    87	            }
    88	            
    89	            echo json_encode([
    90	                'success' => true,
    91	                'message' => 'Password berhasil diubah. Silakan login ulang.'
    92	            ]);
    93	            
    94	        } catch (Exception $e) {
    95	            error_log("[ChangePass] update error: " . $e->getMessage());
    96	            echo json_encode([
    97	                'success' => false,
    98	                'message' => 'Gagal mengubah password: ' . $e->getMessage()
    99	            ]);
   100	        }
   101	        break;
   102	
   103	    default:
   104	        echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Task tidak dikenali.']);
   105	        break;
   106	}
   107	?>

===== 4. MATERIAL MASTER ENDPOINT =====
     1	<?php
     2	require_once __DIR__ . '/../php-func/user.valid.php';
     3	/**
     4	 * m.materialsCRD.php
     5	 * Backend Controller CRUD Master Material (materials) PostgreSQL
     6	 */
     7	
     8	require_once __DIR__ . '/../php-func/function.all.php';
     9	require_once __DIR__ . '/../php-class/crudClass.php';
    10	
    11	if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_NONE) {
    12	    session_start();
    13	}
    14	header('Content-Type: application/json');
    15	
    16	$task = $_POST['task'] ?? $_GET['task'] ?? '';
    17	
    18	if (in_array($task, ['create', 'update', 'delete'], true)
    19	    && strtoupper($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] ?? 'GET') !== 'POST') {
    20	    http_response_code(405);
    21	    header('Allow: GET, POST');
    22	    echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Operasi perubahan data wajib menggunakan POST.']);
    23	    exit;
    24	}
    25	
    26	// Otorisasi backend mandiri: login wajib dan hak operasi mengikuti FormAccess menu.
    27	if (empty($_SESSION['user_id']) || !is_numeric($_SESSION['user_id']) || (int)$_SESSION['user_id'] <= 0) {
    28	    http_response_code(401);
    29	    echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Sesi berakhir, silakan login ulang.']);
    30	    exit;
    31	}
    32	$requiredAccess = 1;
    33	if (in_array($task, ['create', 'add', 'save'], true)) $requiredAccess = 2;
    34	if (in_array($task, ['update', 'edit'], true)) $requiredAccess = 3;
    35	if (in_array($task, ['delete'], true)) $requiredAccess = 4;
    36	if ((int)($_SESSION['FormAccess'] ?? 0) < $requiredAccess) {
    37	    http_response_code(403);
    38	    echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Hak akses tidak mencukupi untuk operasi ini.']);
    39	    exit;
    40	}
    41	
    42	// Helper error handling PostgreSQL
    43	function PgOrFail($result, $context = '') {
    44	    if ($result === false) {
    45	        throw new Exception(($context ? "[{$context}] " : '') . pg_last_error());
    46	    }
    47	    return $result;
    48	}
    49	
    50	try {
    51	    switch ($task) {
    52	
    53	        // ==========================================================
    54	        // 1. LIST -- Load Data Grid
    55	        // ==========================================================
    56	        case 'list':
    57	            $search = trim($_REQUEST['query'] ?? '');
    58	            $where  = '1=1';
    59	            $params = [];
    60	
    61	            if ($search !== '') {
    62	                $where .= " AND (material_code ILIKE $1 OR material_name ILIKE $1)";
    63	                $params[] = '%' . $search . '%';
    64	            }
    65	
    66	            $sql = "SELECT id, material_code, material_name, unit, estimated_price, active, created_at
    67	                    FROM materials
    68	                    WHERE {$where}
    69	                    ORDER BY material_name ASC";
    70	                    
    71	            $result = PgOrFail(pg_query_params($sql, $params), 'list');
    72	            $rows = pg_fetch_all($result) ?: [];
    73	
    74	            foreach ($rows as &$r) {
    75	                $r['active'] = ($r['active'] === 't' || $r['active'] === 'true' || $r['active'] === true);
    76	            }
    77	
    78	            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => $rows]);
    79	            break;
    80	
    81	        // ==========================================================
    82	        // 2. READ EDIT -- Load Single Record
    83	        // ==========================================================
    84	        case 'read_edit':
    85	            $id = (int)($_REQUEST['id'] ?? 0);
    86	            $result = PgOrFail(pg_query_params(
    87	                "SELECT id, material_code, material_name, unit, estimated_price, active
    88	                 FROM materials WHERE id = $1",
    89	                [$id]
    90	            ), 'read_edit');
    91	            
    92	            $row = pg_fetch_assoc($result);
    93	            if (!$row) throw new Exception('Data material tidak ditemukan');
    94	
    95	            $row['active'] = ($row['active'] === 't' || $row['active'] === 'true' || $row['active'] === true);
    96	
    97	            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => $row]);
    98	            break;
    99	
   100	        // ==========================================================
   101	        // 3. CREATE -- Insert Material Baru
   102	        // ==========================================================
   103	        case 'create':
   104	            $code  = trim($_POST['material_code'] ?? '');
   105	            $name  = trim($_POST['material_name'] ?? '');
   106	            $unit  = trim($_POST['unit'] ?? '');
   107	            $price = (float)($_POST['estimated_price'] ?? 0);
   108	
   109	            // Parsing status active
   110	            $rawActive = $_POST['is_active'] ?? 'false';
   111	            $active = ($rawActive === 'true' || $rawActive === 't' || $rawActive === '1' || $rawActive === true || $rawActive === 'on') ? 't' : 'f';
   112	
   113	            if ($code === '') throw new Exception('Kode Material wajib diisi');
   114	            if ($name === '') throw new Exception('Nama Material wajib diisi');
   115	            if ($unit === '') throw new Exception('Satuan wajib diisi');
   116	
   117	            pg_query('BEGIN');
   118	            try {
   119	                $result = PgOrFail(pg_query_params(
   120	                    "INSERT INTO materials (material_code, material_name, unit, estimated_price, active)
   121	                     VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5) RETURNING id",
   122	                    [$code, $name, $unit, $price, $active]
   123	                ), 'insert materials');
   124	                
   125	                $newId = pg_fetch_result($result, 0, 'id');
   126	                pg_query('COMMIT');
   127	            } catch (Exception $e) {
   128	                @pg_query('ROLLBACK');
   129	                if (stripos($e->getMessage(), 'duplicate') !== false || stripos($e->getMessage(), 'unique') !== false) {
   130	                    throw new Exception("Kode Material '{$code}' sudah dipakai. Gunakan kode lain.");
   131	                }
   132	                throw $e;
   133	            }
   134	
   135	            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'id' => $newId, 'material_code' => $code]);
   136	            break;
   137	
   138	        // ==========================================================
   139	        // 4. UPDATE -- Perbarui Data Material
   140	        // ==========================================================
   141	        case 'update':
   142	            $id    = (int)($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
   143	            $code  = trim($_POST['material_code'] ?? '');
   144	            $name  = trim($_POST['material_name'] ?? '');
   145	            $unit  = trim($_POST['unit'] ?? '');
   146	            $price = (float)($_POST['estimated_price'] ?? 0);
   147	
   148	            // Parsing status active
   149	            $rawActive = $_POST['is_active'] ?? 'false';
   150	            $active = ($rawActive === 'true' || $rawActive === 't' || $rawActive === '1' || $rawActive === true || $rawActive === 'on') ? 't' : 'f';
   151	
   152	            if ($id <= 0) throw new Exception('ID Material tidak valid');
   153	            if ($code === '') throw new Exception('Kode Material wajib diisi');
   154	            if ($name === '') throw new Exception('Nama Material wajib diisi');
   155	            if ($unit === '') throw new Exception('Satuan wajib diisi');
   156	
   157	            pg_query('BEGIN');
   158	            try {
   159	                PgOrFail(pg_query_params(
   160	                    "UPDATE materials
   161	                     SET material_code = $1, material_name = $2, unit = $3, estimated_price = $4, active = $5
   162	                     WHERE id = $6",
   163	                    [$code, $name, $unit, $price, $active, $id]
   164	                ), 'update materials');
   165	                
   166	                pg_query('COMMIT');
   167	            } catch (Exception $e) {
   168	                @pg_query('ROLLBACK');
   169	                if (stripos($e->getMessage(), 'duplicate') !== false || stripos($e->getMessage(), 'unique') !== false) {
   170	                    throw new Exception("Kode Material '{$code}' sudah dipakai oleh material lain.");
   171	                }
   172	                throw $e;
   173	            }
   174	
   175	            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'id' => $id]);
   176	            break;
   177	
   178	        // ==========================================================
   179	        // 5. DELETE -- Soft Delete
   180	        // ==========================================================

===== 5. PURCHASE ORDER ENDPOINT =====

     6	 */

     8	ob_start();
     9	require_once __DIR__ . '/../php-func/function.all.php';
    10	require_once __DIR__ . '/../php-class/crudClass.php';
    11	if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_NONE) { session_start(); }
    12	header('Content-Type: application/json');
    13	$db = new crud();
    14	const PO_EPS = 0.0001;
    15	const PO_VEPS = 0.01;
    16	const PPN_DEFAULT_RATE = 11.0;
    17	const PPN_MODES = ['NONE', 'EXCL', 'INCL'];
    18	const PO_TRANSITIONS = [
    19	    'Draft'      => ['Diajukan', 'Dibatalkan'],
    20	    'Diajukan'   => ['Disetujui', 'Draft', 'Dibatalkan'],
    21	    'Disetujui'  => ['Selesai', 'Dibatalkan'],
    22	    'Selesai'    => [],
    23	    'Dibatalkan' => [],
    24	];
    25	class PoBusinessError extends Exception {}
    26	function sendJsonResponse($success, $data = [], $message = '', $extra = []) {
    27	    $response = array_merge(['success' => $success, 'message' => $message], $extra);
    28	    if ($success) {
    29	        if (is_array($data) && isset($data['data'])) {
    30	            $response['data'] = $data['data'];
    31	            if (isset($data['totalCount'])) $response['totalCount'] = $data['totalCount'];
    32	        } else { $response['data'] = $data; }
    33	    }
    34	    if (ob_get_length()) { ob_clean(); }
    35	    echo json_encode($response, JSON_INVALID_UTF8_SUBSTITUTE);
    36	    exit;
    37	}
    38	function po_fmt($n): string { return rtrim(rtrim(number_format((float)$n, 3, '.', ''), '0'), '.'); }
    39	function po_ppn_mode($m): string { $m = strtoupper(trim((string)$m)); return in_array($m, PPN_MODES, true) ? $m : 'NONE'; }
    40	function po_ppn_calc(float $subtotal, string $mode, float $rate): array {
    41	    $subtotal = round($subtotal, 2); $mode = po_ppn_mode($mode);
    42	    if ($mode === 'NONE' || $rate <= 0) return ['subtotal'=>$subtotal,'dpp'=>$subtotal,'ppn'=>0.0,'total'=>$subtotal];
    43	    if ($mode === 'INCL') { $dpp = round($subtotal/(1+$rate/100),2); return ['subtotal'=>$subtotal,'dpp'=>$dpp,'ppn'=>round($subtotal-$dpp,2),'total'=>$subtotal]; }
    44	    $ppn = round($subtotal*$rate/100,2); return ['subtotal'=>$subtotal,'dpp'=>$subtotal,'ppn'=>$ppn,'total'=>round($subtotal+$ppn,2)];
    45	}
    46	function po_line_dpp(float $qty, float $price, string $mode, float $rate): float {
    47	    $sub = round($qty*$price,2);
    48	    if ($mode === 'INCL' && $rate > 0) return round($sub/(1+$rate/100),2);
    49	    return $sub;
    50	}
    51	function po_base_currency($db): array {
    52	    $r = $db->query("SELECT fs.base_currency_id AS id, c.init FROM public.finance_settings fs JOIN public.m_currency c ON c.id = fs.base_currency_id ORDER BY fs.id LIMIT 1")->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
    53	    if (!$r) $r = $db->query("SELECT id, init FROM public.m_currency WHERE UPPER(TRIM(init))='IDR' ORDER BY id LIMIT 1")->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
    54	    if (!$r) throw new Exception('Mata uang dasar belum dikonfigurasi (finance_settings.base_currency_id).');
    55	    return ['id'=>(int)$r['id'], 'code'=>strtoupper(trim((string)$r['init']))];
    56	}
    57	function po_currency_code($db, int $id): string {
    58	    static $cache = [];
    59	    if (isset($cache[$id])) return $cache[$id];
    60	    $st = $db->prepare("SELECT UPPER(TRIM(init)) FROM public.m_currency WHERE id = :id"); $st->execute([':id'=>$id]);
    61	    $c = (string)$st->fetchColumn(); $cache[$id] = $c !== '' ? $c : 'IDR'; return $cache[$id];
    62	}
    63	function po_sql_line_dpp(string $i, string $po): string {
    64	    return "ROUND(CASE WHEN {$po}.ppn_mode='INCL' AND {$po}.ppn_rate>0 THEN ROUND({$i}.qty*{$i}.unit_price,2)/(1+{$po}.ppn_rate/100.0) ELSE ROUND({$i}.qty*{$i}.unit_price,2) END,2)";
    65	}
    66	function po_sql_budget_factor(int $baseId, string $po, string $p): string {
    67	    $effCur = "COALESCE({$po}.currency_id, {$baseId})";
    68	    $effBud = "COALESCE({$po}.budget_currency_id, {$p}.budget_currency_id, {$baseId})";
    69	    $rate = "COALESCE(NULLIF({$po}.exch_rate,0),1)";
    70	    $budR = "COALESCE(NULLIF({$po}.budget_exch_rate,0), NULLIF({$p}.budget_exch_rate,0),1)";
    71	    return "CASE WHEN {$effCur}={$effBud} THEN 1.0 WHEN {$effBud}={$baseId} THEN {$rate} ELSE {$rate}/{$budR} END";
    72	}
    73	function po_ordered_map($db, int $projectId, int $exclPoId = 0): array {
    74	    $st = $db->prepare("SELECT poi.budget_item_id, SUM(poi.qty) AS q FROM po_items poi JOIN purchase_orders po ON po.id=poi.po_id WHERE po.project_id=:p AND po.status<>'Dibatalkan' AND po.id<>:x AND poi.budget_item_id IS NOT NULL GROUP BY poi.budget_item_id");
    75	    $st->execute([':p'=>$projectId, ':x'=>$exclPoId]);
    76	    $map = []; foreach ($st->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC) as $r) $map[(int)$r['budget_item_id']] = (float)$r['q']; return $map;
    77	}
    78	function po_committed_map($db, int $projectId, int $exclPoId, int $baseId): array {
    79	    $dpp = po_sql_line_dpp('poi','po'); $fac = po_sql_budget_factor($baseId,'po','p');
    80	    $st = $db->prepare("SELECT poi.budget_item_id, SUM({$dpp}*{$fac}) AS v FROM po_items poi JOIN purchase_orders po ON po.id=poi.po_id JOIN public.projects p ON p.id=po.project_id WHERE po.project_id=:p AND po.status<>'Dibatalkan' AND po.id<>:x GROUP BY poi.budget_item_id");
    81	    $st->execute([':p'=>$projectId, ':x'=>$exclPoId]);
    82	    $map = []; $total = 0.0;
    83	    foreach ($st->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC) as $r) { $v = round((float)$r['v'],2); $total += $v; if ($r['budget_item_id'] !== null) $map[(int)$r['budget_item_id']] = $v; }
    84	    return ['map'=>$map, 'total'=>round($total,2)];
    85	}
    86	function po_project_budget($db, int $projectId, array $base): array {
    87	    $st = $db->prepare("SELECT p.budget_currency_id, p.budget_exch_rate, p.budget, COALESCE(UPPER(TRIM(c.init)),'') AS budget_code FROM public.projects p LEFT JOIN public.m_currency c ON c.id=p.budget_currency_id WHERE p.id=:p");
    88	    $st->execute([':p'=>$projectId]); $r = $st->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
    89	    if (!$r) throw new PoBusinessError('Proyek tidak ditemukan.');
    90	    $curId = (int)$r['budget_currency_id'] > 0 ? (int)$r['budget_currency_id'] : $base['id'];
    91	    $code = $curId === $base['id'] ? $base['code'] : ($r['budget_code'] !== '' ? $r['budget_code'] : po_currency_code($db,$curId));
    92	    $st = $db->prepare("SELECT COALESCE(SUM(ROUND(ROUND(qty_plan*COALESCE(unit_price_plan,0),2)*(1+COALESCE(tolerance_pct,0)/100.0),2)),0) FROM public.budget_items WHERE project_id=:p AND category='Material' AND COALESCE(qty_plan,0)>0");
    93	    $st->execute([':p'=>$projectId]);
    94	    $ceil = (float)$r['budget'];
    95	    return ['currency_id'=>$curId,'code'=>$code,'is_base'=>$curId===$base['id'],'proj_rate'=>(float)$r['budget_exch_rate'],'ceiling'=>$ceil>0?round($ceil,2):null,'total_limit'=>round((float)$st->fetchColumn(),2)];
    96	}
    97	function po_received_total($db, int $poId): float {
    98	    $st = $db->prepare("SELECT COALESCE(SUM(d.qty_received),0) FROM grn_dtl d JOIN grn_hdr h ON h.id=d.grn_hdr_id WHERE h.po_id=:id AND COALESCE(LOWER(h.status),'') NOT IN ('cancelled','canceled','dibatalkan','void','reversed')");
    99	    $st->execute([':id'=>$poId]); return (float)$st->fetchColumn();
   100	}

   102	$task = $_REQUEST['task'] ?? '';
   103	$poAccess = (int)($_SESSION['FormAccess'] ?? 0);

   107	if ($task === 'delete_po') $poRequired = 4;

   109	if (in_array($task, ['save_po','change_status','delete_po'], true) && ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] ?? '') !== 'POST') { http_response_code(405); sendJsonResponse(false, [], 'Metode harus POST untuk perubahan purchase order.'); }

   115	            foreach ($rows as &$r) { $r['id']=(int)$r['id']; $r['budget_currency_id']=(int)$r['budget_currency_id']; $r['budget_is_base']=in_array($r['budget_is_base'],[true,'t','true',1,'1'],true); } unset($r);
   116	            sendJsonResponse(true, $rows); break;

   125	            sendJsonResponse(true, $rows); break;
   126	        case 'get_exchange_rate':

   145	            $st->execute([':p'=>$prj]);

   152	            }

   154	            sendJsonResponse(true, $data, '', ['summary'=>$summary]); break;
   155	        case 'list_pos':
   156	            $base = po_base_currency($db);
   157	            $start=(int)($_REQUEST['start']??0); $limit=(int)($_REQUEST['limit']??250); $query=trim($_REQUEST['query']??'');
   158	            $where=''; $params=[];
   159	            if ($query !== '') { $where="WHERE (po.po_number ILIKE :query OR p.name ILIKE :query OR s.name ILIKE :query)"; $params[':query']='%'.$query.'%'; }
   160	            $st = $db->prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM purchase_orders po JOIN public.projects p ON p.id=po.project_id LEFT JOIN public.m_suppliers s ON s.id=po.supplier_id {$where}"); $st->execute($params); $totalCount=(int)$st->fetchColumn();
   161	            $dpp2 = po_sql_line_dpp('i2','po'); $fac2 = po_sql_budget_factor($base['id'],'po','p');
   162	            $st = $db->prepare("SELECT po.id, po.po_number, po.po_date, po.project_id, p.name AS project_name, po.supplier_id, s.name AS supplier_name, po.status, po.notes, po.currency_id, c.init AS currency_code, po.exch_rate, po.ppn_mode, po.ppn_rate, COALESCE(po.budget_currency_id,p.budget_currency_id,{$base['id']}) AS budget_currency_id, bc.init AS budget_code, COUNT(i.id) AS item_count, COALESCE(SUM(i.qty*i.unit_price),0) AS total_amount, COALESCE(SUM(ROUND(i.qty*i.unit_price,2)),0) AS subtotal_r, COALESCE((SELECT SUM({$dpp2}*{$fac2}) FROM po_items i2 WHERE i2.po_id=po.id),0) AS total_budget FROM purchase_orders po JOIN public.projects p ON p.id=po.project_id LEFT JOIN public.m_suppliers s ON s.id=po.supplier_id LEFT JOIN public.m_currency c ON c.id=po.currency_id LEFT JOIN public.m_currency bc ON bc.id=COALESCE(po.budget_currency_id,p.budget_currency_id,{$base['id']}) LEFT JOIN po_items i ON i.po_id=po.id {$where} GROUP BY po.id, p.name, s.name, c.init, bc.init, p.budget_currency_id, p.budget_exch_rate ORDER BY po.po_date DESC, po.id DESC LIMIT :limit OFFSET :start");
   163	            foreach ($params as $k=>$v) $st->bindValue($k,$v);
   164	            $st->bindValue(':limit',$limit,PDO::PARAM_INT); $st->bindValue(':start',$start,PDO::PARAM_INT); $st->execute();
   165	            $rows = $st->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
   166	            foreach ($rows as &$r) {
   167	                $r['id']=(int)$r['id']; $r['project_id']=(int)$r['project_id']; $r['supplier_id']=$r['supplier_id']!==null?(int)$r['supplier_id']:null; $r['currency_id']=$r['currency_id']!==null?(int)$r['currency_id']:null; $r['budget_currency_id']=(int)$r['budget_currency_id']; $r['exch_rate']=(float)$r['exch_rate']; $r['item_count']=(int)$r['item_count']; $r['total_amount']=(float)$r['total_amount']; $r['total_budget']=round((float)$r['total_budget'],2);
   168	                $r['ppn_mode']=po_ppn_mode($r['ppn_mode']); $r['ppn_rate']=(float)$r['ppn_rate'];
   169	                $calc=po_ppn_calc((float)$r['subtotal_r'],$r['ppn_mode'],$r['ppn_rate']); unset($r['subtotal_r']);
   170	                $r['dpp']=$calc['dpp']; $r['ppn_amount']=$calc['ppn']; $r['grand_total']=$calc['total']; $r['ppn_idr']=round($calc['ppn']*$r['exch_rate'],2);
   171	            } unset($r);
   172	            sendJsonResponse(true, ['data'=>$rows,'totalCount'=>$totalCount]); break;
   173	        case 'load_po':
   174	            $base = po_base_currency($db); $id=(int)($_REQUEST['id']??0);
   175	            $st = $db->prepare("SELECT po.id, po.po_number, po.po_date, po.project_id, p.name AS project_name, po.supplier_id, s.name AS supplier_name, po.status, po.notes, po.currency_id, c.init AS currency_code, po.exch_rate, po.ppn_mode, po.ppn_rate, po.budget_currency_id, po.budget_exch_rate, p.budget_currency_id AS prj_budget_currency_id, p.budget_exch_rate AS prj_budget_exch_rate, bc.init AS budget_code FROM purchase_orders po JOIN public.projects p ON p.id=po.project_id LEFT JOIN public.m_suppliers s ON s.id=po.supplier_id LEFT JOIN public.m_currency c ON c.id=po.currency_id LEFT JOIN public.m_currency bc ON bc.id=COALESCE(po.budget_currency_id,p.budget_currency_id,{$base['id']}) WHERE po.id=:id");
   176	            $st->execute([':id'=>$id]); $hdr=$st->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
   177	            if (!$hdr) sendJsonResponse(false, [], 'PO tidak ditemukan.');
   178	            $hdr['exch_rate']=(float)$hdr['exch_rate'];
   179	            $curId=(int)$hdr['currency_id']>0?(int)$hdr['currency_id']:$base['id'];
   180	            $budId=(int)$hdr['budget_currency_id']>0?(int)$hdr['budget_currency_id']:((int)$hdr['prj_budget_currency_id']>0?(int)$hdr['prj_budget_currency_id']:$base['id']);
   181	            if ($budId===$base['id']) $budRate=1.0; elseif ($budId===$curId) $budRate=$hdr['exch_rate'];
   182	            else $budRate=(float)$hdr['budget_exch_rate']>0?(float)$hdr['budget_exch_rate']:((float)$hdr['prj_budget_exch_rate']>0?(float)$hdr['prj_budget_exch_rate']:1.0);
   183	            $factor=($curId===$budId)?1.0:($budRate>0?$hdr['exch_rate']/$budRate:0.0);
   184	            $hdr['ppn_mode']=po_ppn_mode($hdr['ppn_mode']); $hdr['ppn_rate']=(float)$hdr['ppn_rate'];
   185	            $st = $db->prepare("SELECT i.id, i.budget_item_id, i.material_id, m.material_code, i.description, i.unit, i.qty, i.unit_price, i.currency_id, COALESCE(UPPER(TRIM(ic.init)),'IDR') AS item_currency_code, COALESCE(NULLIF(i.exch_rate,0),1) AS exch_rate, COALESCE(g.q,0) AS qty_received FROM po_items i LEFT JOIN public.materials m ON m.id=i.material_id LEFT JOIN public.m_currency ic ON ic.id=i.currency_id LEFT JOIN (SELECT d.po_item_id, SUM(d.qty_received) AS q FROM grn_dtl d JOIN grn_hdr h ON h.id=d.grn_hdr_id WHERE COALESCE(LOWER(h.status),'') NOT IN ('cancelled','canceled','dibatalkan','void','reversed') GROUP BY d.po_item_id) g ON g.po_item_id=i.id WHERE i.po_id=:id ORDER BY i.id");
   186	            $st->execute([':id'=>$id]); $items=$st->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
   187	            $received=0.0; $subItems=0.0; $totBud=0.0; $totBase=0.0;
   188	            foreach ($items as &$it) {
   189	                $it['id']=(int)$it['id']; $it['budget_item_id']=$it['budget_item_id']!==null?(int)$it['budget_item_id']:null; $it['material_id']=$it['material_id']!==null?(int)$it['material_id']:null; $it['currency_id']=$it['currency_id']!==null?(int)$it['currency_id']:null; $it['qty']=(float)$it['qty']; $it['unit_price']=(float)$it['unit_price']; $it['exch_rate']=(float)$it['exch_rate']; $it['qty_received']=(float)$it['qty_received']; $received+=$it['qty_received'];
   190	                $lineDpp=po_line_dpp($it['qty'],$it['unit_price'],$hdr['ppn_mode'],$hdr['ppn_rate']);
   191	                $it['amount_budget']=round($lineDpp*$factor,2); $it['amount_base']=round($lineDpp*$hdr['exch_rate'],2);
   192	                $totBud+=$it['amount_budget']; $totBase+=$it['amount_base']; $subItems+=round($it['qty']*$it['unit_price'],2);
   193	            } unset($it);
   194	            $calc=po_ppn_calc($subItems,$hdr['ppn_mode'],$hdr['ppn_rate']);
   195	            $hdr['subtotal']=$calc['subtotal']; $hdr['dpp']=$calc['dpp']; $hdr['ppn_amount']=$calc['ppn']; $hdr['grand_total']=$calc['total']; $hdr['ppn_idr']=round($calc['ppn']*$hdr['exch_rate'],2);
   196	            $hdr['currency_id']=$curId; $hdr['budget_currency_id']=$budId; $hdr['budget_code']=$hdr['budget_code']!==null?strtoupper(trim((string)$hdr['budget_code'])):po_currency_code($db,$budId); $hdr['budget_is_base']=$budId===$base['id']; $hdr['budget_exch_rate']=$budRate; $hdr['total_budget']=round($totBud,2); $hdr['total_base']=round($totBase,2);
   197	            unset($hdr['prj_budget_currency_id'],$hdr['prj_budget_exch_rate']);
   198	            $allowed=PO_TRANSITIONS[$hdr['status']]??[];
   199	            if ($received>PO_EPS) $allowed=array_values(array_diff($allowed,['Dibatalkan','Draft']));
   200	            sendJsonResponse(true, ['header'=>$hdr,'items'=>$items,'allowed_actions'=>$allowed]); break;
   201	        case 'save_po':
   202	            $id=(int)($_POST['id']??0);
   203	            if ($id>0 && $poAccess<3) { http_response_code(403); sendJsonResponse(false,[],'Hak ubah Purchase Order tidak tersedia.'); }
   204	            $base=po_base_currency($db);
   205	            $prj=(int)($_POST['project_id']??0); $sup=(int)($_POST['supplier_id']??0)?:null; $cur=(int)($_POST['currency_id']??0)?:$base['id'];
   206	            $rate=(float)($_POST['exch_rate']??1); $budRateIn=(float)($_POST['budget_exch_rate']??0);
   207	            $date=(string)($_POST['po_date']??''); $notes=trim((string)($_POST['notes']??''))?:null;
   208	            $items=json_decode($_POST['items']??'[]',true);
   209	            if ($rate<=0) $rate=1;
   210	            $ppnMode=po_ppn_mode($_POST['ppn_mode']??'NONE'); $ppnRate=round((float)($_POST['ppn_rate']??PPN_DEFAULT_RATE),2);
   211	            try {
   212	                $d=DateTime::createFromFormat('Y-m-d',$date);
   213	                if (!$d || $d->format('Y-m-d')!==$date) throw new PoBusinessError('Tanggal PO tidak valid.');
   214	                if ($prj<=0) throw new PoBusinessError('Proyek wajib dipilih.');
   215	                if (!is_array($items)||!count($items)) throw new PoBusinessError('Minimal satu material harus diisi.');
   216	                if ($ppnMode==='NONE') $ppnRate=0.0; elseif ($ppnRate<=0||$ppnRate>100) throw new PoBusinessError('Tarif PPN harus lebih dari 0 dan maksimal 100%.');
   217	                $pb=po_project_budget($db,$prj,$base);
   218	                $st=$db->prepare("SELECT UPPER(TRIM(init)) FROM public.m_currency WHERE id=:id"); $st->execute([':id'=>$cur]); $curCode=$st->fetchColumn();
   219	                if (!$curCode) throw new PoBusinessError('Mata uang PO tidak ditemukan.');
   220	                if ($cur===$base['id']) $rate=1.0;

===== 6. SPK ENDPOINT =====
     1	<?php
     2	/**
     3	 * spkCRD.php — SPK (Surat Perintah Kerja)
     4	 *
     5	 * SPK = izin mengeluarkan material gudang untuk satu proyek. Hanya bisa DITERBITKAN setelah proyek menerima pembayaran
     6	 * termin, dan qty per material dibatasi: kuota kumulatif = qty RAB x (% kontrak yang sudah dibayar x 2), maks 100%
     7	 * (lihat php-func/function.spk.php). Warehouse Issuing proyek wajib memilih SPK dan qty issue dibatasi sisa jatah SPK.
     8	 *
     9	 * Status : Draft -> Diterbitkan -> Selesai ; Dibatalkan (dari Draft / Diterbitkan yang belum dipakai issue)
    10	 * Action : list | detail | lookup_projects | lookup_tasks | lookup_subcontractors | lookup_materials | quota | save | issue | close | cancel | delete
    11	 *
    12	 * [2026-11-11] SPK INTERNAL atau SUBCON (database/subcontract_management_ddl.sql, Langkah 1 SUBCONTRACT_MANAGEMENT_DESIGN.md)
    13	 *   - spk_type INTERNAL (default, perilaku lama tidak berubah) atau SUBCON (execution_subcontractor_id wajib).
    14	 *   - SPK SUBCON boleh tanpa baris material (pekerjaan murni). Kalau ada material, aturan kuota + stok tetap sama.
    15	 *   - Syarat 'harus ada pembayaran klien' tetap untuk semua SPK yang melepas material; SPK SUBCON tanpa material
    16	 *     tidak dikenai syarat itu (ubah lewat SPK_SUBCON_REQUIRE_CLIENT_PAYMENT).
    17	 *   - Tipe SPK tidak bisa diubah setelah disimpan. Kontrak subkon (subcon_contracts.spk_id) menempel ke SPK SUBCON.
    18	 *   - Kode tetap jalan di database yang belum menjalankan DDL tersebut (kolom baru dicek dulu).
    19	 * Akses  : buat / ubah >= 2 (ubah form tersimpan perlu 3) ; terbitkan / tutup / batalkan >= 3 ; hapus >= 4
    20	 */
    21	ob_start();
    22	ini_set('display_errors', '0');
    23	require_once __DIR__ . '/../php-func/user.valid.php';
    24	require_once '../php-func/function.all.php';
    25	require_once '../php-class/crudClass.php';
    26	require_once '../php-func/function.stock.php';
    27	require_once '../php-func/function.spk.php';
    28	
    29	define('SPK_ACCESS_EDIT', 2);
    30	define('SPK_ACCESS_ACTION', 3);
    31	define('SPK_ACCESS_DELETE', 4);
    32	// SPK SUBCON tanpa material gudang: apakah tetap wajib menunggu pembayaran klien sebelum diterbitkan?
    33	define('SPK_SUBCON_REQUIRE_CLIENT_PAYMENT', false);
    34	// SPK SUBCON hanya boleh diterbitkan bila kontrak subkon yang terhubung berstatus Disetujui (set false untuk mematikan aturan ini)
    35	define('SPK_SUBCON_REQUIRE_CONTRACT_ON_ISSUE', true);
    36	
    37	if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_NONE) { session_start(); }
    38	$spkUserId = (int)($_SESSION['user_id'] ?? 0);
    39	if ($spkUserId <= 0) { http_response_code(401); WhFail('Sesi berakhir, silakan login ulang.'); }
    40	if ((int)($_SESSION['FormAccess'] ?? 0) < 1) { http_response_code(403); WhFail('Anda tidak memiliki hak baca SPK.'); }
    41	$action = WhReq('action', 'list');
    42	
    43	try {
    44	    switch ($action) {
    45	        case 'list':             SpkList(); break;
    46	        case 'detail':           SpkDetail(); break;
    47	        case 'lookup_projects':  SpkLookupProjects(); break;
    48	        case 'lookup_tasks':     SpkLookupTasks(); break;
    49	        case 'lookup_subcontractors': SpkLookupSubcontractors(); break;
    50	        case 'lookup_materials': SpkLookupMaterials(); break;
    51	        case 'quota':            SpkQuotaInfo(); break;
    52	        case 'save':             SpkSave(); break;
    53	        case 'issue':            SpkIssue(); break;
    54	        case 'close':            SpkClose(); break;
    55	        case 'cancel':           SpkCancel(); break;
    56	        case 'delete':           SpkDelete(); break;
    57	        default: throw new WhException('Action tidak dikenali.');
    58	    }
    59	} catch (WhException $e) {
    60	    @pg_query($conn, 'ROLLBACK');
    61	    WhFail($e->getMessage());
    62	} catch (Exception $e) {
    63	    @pg_query($conn, 'ROLLBACK');
    64	    WhLog('spkCRD: ' . $e->getMessage());
    65	    WhFail('Terjadi kesalahan pada server. Hubungi administrator.');
    66	}

    69	function SpkNeed($level, $msg) {
    70	    $a = isset($_SESSION['FormAccess']) ? (int)$_SESSION['FormAccess'] : 0;
    71	    if ($a < $level) { throw new WhException($msg); }
    72	}

    74	// Apakah database ini sudah menjalankan DDL SPK subkon (kolom execution_subcontractor_id ada)?
    75	function SpkHasSubconSchema() {
    76	    static $ok = null;
    77	    if ($ok === null) {
    78	        $r = WhRow("SELECT 1 AS x FROM information_schema.columns
    79	                    WHERE table_schema='public' AND table_name='spk' AND column_name='execution_subcontractor_id' LIMIT 1");
    80	        $ok = $r ? true : false;
    81	    }
    82	    return $ok;
    83	}
    84	
    85	function SpkGet($id, $lock = false) {
    86	    $s = WhRow('SELECT * FROM spk WHERE id=$1' . ($lock ? ' FOR UPDATE' : ''), array((int)$id));
    87	    if (!$s) { throw new WhException('SPK tidak ditemukan.'); }
    88	    return $s;
    89	}
    90	
    91	// jumlah baris issue (posted) yang memakai SPK
    92	function SpkIssueCount($spkId) {
    93	    $r = WhRow("SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM material_out_header WHERE spk_id=$1 AND status='posted'", array((int)$spkId));
    94	    return (int)$r['n'];
    95	}
    96	
    97	// item SPK per material + terpakai + sisa
    98	function SpkItemsWithUsage($spkId) {
    99	    $rows = WhRows("SELECT si.material_id, m.material_code, m.material_name, COALESCE(NULLIF(si.unit,''), m.unit, '') AS unit,
   100	                           SUM(si.qty) AS qty, MAX(si.notes) AS notes
   101	                    FROM spk_items si JOIN materials m ON m.id=si.material_id
   102	                    WHERE si.spk_id=$1 GROUP BY si.material_id, m.material_code, m.material_name, COALESCE(NULLIF(si.unit,''), m.unit, '')
   103	                    ORDER BY m.material_name", array((int)$spkId));
   104	    foreach ($rows as &$r) {
   105	        $u = WhRow("SELECT COALESCE(SUM(i.qty_out),0) AS q FROM material_out_items i JOIN material_out_header h ON h.id=i.material_out_id
   106	                    WHERE h.spk_id=$1 AND i.material_id=$2 AND h.status='posted'", array((int)$spkId, (int)$r['material_id']));
   107	        $r['qty'] = round((float)$r['qty'], 4);
   108	        $r['used'] = round((float)$u['q'], 4);
   109	        $r['remaining'] = max(0, round($r['qty'] - $r['used'], 4));
   110	    }
   111	    unset($r);
   112	    return $rows;
   113	}
   114	
   115	function SpkList() {
   116	    list($start, $limit) = WhPage();
   117	    $q = WhReq('query');
   118	    $params = array(); $where = '1=1';
   119	    if ($q !== '') {
   120	        $params[] = '%' . $q . '%';
   121	        $where .= ' AND (s.spk_no ILIKE $1 OR p.name ILIKE $1 OR s.foreman ILIKE $1 OR s.description ILIKE $1)';
   122	    }
   123	    $c = WhRow("SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM spk s JOIN projects p ON p.id=s.project_id WHERE $where", $params);
   124	    $params[] = $limit;  $pl = '$' . count($params);
   125	    $params[] = $start;  $ps = '$' . count($params);
   126	    $ready = SpkHasSubconSchema();
   127	    $sx = $ready
   128	        ? ", s.spk_type, s.execution_subcontractor_id, sb.name AS subcontractor_name, sc.id AS contract_id, sc.spk_no AS contract_no, sc.status AS contract_status"
   129	        : ", 'INTERNAL'::text AS spk_type, NULL::int AS execution_subcontractor_id, NULL::text AS subcontractor_name, NULL::int AS contract_id, NULL::text AS contract_no, NULL::text AS contract_status";
   130	    $jx = $ready
   131	        ? " LEFT JOIN subcontractors sb ON sb.id=s.execution_subcontractor_id LEFT JOIN subcon_contracts sc ON sc.spk_id=s.id"
   132	        : "";
   133	    $rows = WhRows("SELECT s.id, s.spk_no, s.spk_date, s.project_id, p.name AS project_name, s.foreman, s.description, s.status, s.remark $sx,
   134	                           (SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT x.material_id) FROM spk_items x WHERE x.spk_id=s.id) AS item_count
   135	                    FROM spk s JOIN projects p ON p.id=s.project_id $jx
   136	                    WHERE $where ORDER BY s.spk_date DESC, s.id DESC LIMIT $pl OFFSET $ps", $params);
   137	    // persen terpakai = rata-rata (issue / qty SPK, maks 100%) per material
   138	    foreach ($rows as &$r) {
   139	        $items = SpkItemsWithUsage($r['id']);
   140	        $sum = 0.0;
   141	        foreach ($items as $it) { $sum += ($it['qty'] > 0) ? min(1.0, $it['used'] / $it['qty']) : 0.0; }
   142	        $r['used_pct'] = count($items) ? round($sum / count($items) * 100, 1) : 0;
   143	    }
   144	    unset($r);
   145	    WhJson(array('success' => true, 'totalCount' => (int)$c['n'], 'data' => $rows));
   146	}
   147	
   148	function SpkDetail() {
   149	    $id = (int)WhReq('id');
   150	    $s = SpkGet($id);
   151	    $p = WhRow('SELECT p.name, c.name AS client_name FROM projects p LEFT JOIN clients c ON c.id=p.client_id WHERE p.id=$1', array((int)$s['project_id']));
   152	    $pay = SpkProjectPaid($s['project_id']);
   153	    $tasks = WhRows("SELECT t.id, t.task_name, COALESCE(t.description,'') AS description, t.start_date, t.end_date, COALESCE(t.status,'') AS status
   154	                     FROM spk_tasks st JOIN public.project_tasks t ON t.id=st.task_id WHERE st.spk_id=$1 ORDER BY t.start_date NULLS LAST, t.id", array($id));
   155	    $sub = null; $contract = null;
   156	    if (SpkHasSubconSchema()) {
   157	        if (!empty($s['execution_subcontractor_id'])) {
   158	            $sub = WhRow("SELECT id, name, COALESCE(code,'') AS code FROM subcontractors WHERE id=$1", array((int)$s['execution_subcontractor_id']));
   159	        }
   160	        $contract = WhRow("SELECT id, spk_no AS contract_no, title, contract_value, status FROM subcon_contracts WHERE spk_id=$1", array($id));
   161	    }
   162	    WhJson(array('success' => true, 'header' => array_merge($s, array('project_name' => $p['name'], 'client_name' => $p['client_name'])),
   163	                 'pay' => $pay, 'items' => SpkItemsWithUsage($id), 'tasks' => $tasks, 'issue_count' => SpkIssueCount($id),
   164	                 'subcontractor' => $sub ? $sub : null, 'contract' => $contract ? $contract : null));
   165	}
   166	
   167	function SpkLookupProjects() {
   168	    $q = WhReq('query');
   169	    $rows = WhRows("SELECT p.id, p.name FROM projects p WHERE p.name ILIKE $1 ORDER BY p.name LIMIT 100", array('%' . $q . '%'));
   170	    WhJson(array('success' => true, 'totalCount' => count($rows), 'data' => $rows));
   171	}
   172	
   173	function SpkLookupSubcontractors() {
   174	    if (!SpkHasSubconSchema()) { WhJson(array('success' => true, 'totalCount' => 0, 'data' => array())); return; }
   175	    $q = WhReq('query');
   176	    $rows = WhRows("SELECT id, name, COALESCE(code,'') AS code FROM subcontractors
   177	                    WHERE active AND (name ILIKE $1 OR COALESCE(code,'') ILIKE $1) ORDER BY name LIMIT 100", array('%' . $q . '%'));
   178	    WhJson(array('success' => true, 'totalCount' => count($rows), 'data' => $rows));
   179	}

===== 7. SUBCON ENDPOINT =====
     1	<?php
     2	/**
     3	 * subconCRD.php
     4	 * ============================================================
     5	 * Backend Modul Subkontraktor (js/subcon.js, views/subcon.php). Pola sama dengan poCRD.php: PDO (class crud),
     6	 * dispatch lewat $_REQUEST['task'], respons JSON {success, message, data, totalCount}. Migrasi: sql/2026-10-30.subcon.sql
     7	 *
     8	 * Rantai dokumen (semua bisa ditelusuri, tidak ada yang dihapus -- hanya Dibatalkan dengan alasan):
     9	 *   Proyek -> (Task proyek) -> SPK (subcon_contracts) -> Prestasi (subcon_progress) -> Pembayaran (subcon_payments) -> Jurnal
    10	 *
    11	 * SPK     : Draft -> Diajukan -> Disetujui -> Selesai ; Dibatalkan dari Draft/Diajukan/Disetujui selama belum ada prestasi/pembayaran.
    12	 *           Nilai SPK (DPP) dibagi ke task proyek (project_tasks); total pembagian wajib = nilai kontrak. Opsional terikat satu baris RAB:
    13	 *           total SPK pada baris RAB itu tidak boleh melebihi nilai anggaran baris (qty_plan x unit_price_plan).
    14	 * Prestasi: per periode, progres kumulatif % per task (tidak boleh turun, maks 100). Nilai periode = nilai task x (kum - sebelumnya)/100.
    15	 *           PPN = nilai x tarif SPK ; PPh final dipotong = nilai x tarif ; retensi = nilai x % ; potongan uang muka proporsional
    16	 *           (nilai x dp% , maks sisa uang muka; di progres 100% sisa uang muka dipotong semua) ;
    17	 *           bayar = nilai + PPN - PPh - retensi - potongan uang muka.
    18	 *           Hanya satu prestasi Draft per SPK. Disetujui = jurnal (Dr WIP 1700 / PPN Masukan 2400 ; Cr Hutang Subkon 2110 ;
    19	 *           potongan: Dr 2110 / Cr Hutang PPh 2130, Hutang Retensi 2120, Uang Muka Subkon 1410). Batal hanya prestasi Disetujui TERAKHIR
    20	 *           dan belum ada pembayaran.
    21	 * Bayar   : DP (Dr 1410 / Cr kas-bank, <= uang muka SPK), PROGRESS (Dr 2110 / Cr kas-bank, <= sisa prestasi), RETENSI
    22	 *           (Dr 2120 / Cr kas-bank, <= retensi ditahan, hanya setelah SPK Selesai). Batal = reverse jurnal.
    23	 * Kontrol : SC_REQUIRE_OTHER_APPROVER=true -> penyetuju SPK / prestasi harus user lain selain pembuatnya.
    24	 * Task    : list_project_options | list_subcontractors | save_subcontractor | list_project_tasks | list_budget_items | list_accounts |
    25	 *           list_contracts | load_contract | save_contract | change_status | delete_contract | progress_prepare | save_progress |
    26	 *           approve_progress | cancel_progress | save_payment | cancel_payment
    27	 * ============================================================
    28	 */
    29	ini_set('display_errors', '0');
    30	ob_start();
    31	
    32	require_once __DIR__ . '/../php-func/user.valid.php';
    33	require_once __DIR__ . '/../php-func/function.all.php';
    34	require_once __DIR__ . '/../php-class/crudClass.php';
    35	
    36	if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_NONE) {
    37	    session_start();
    38	}
    39	header('Content-Type: application/json');
    40	
    41	$db = new crud();
    42	
    43	const SC_EPS = 0.01;
    44	const SC_REQUIRE_OTHER_APPROVER = true;
    45	const SC_REF_PROGRESS = 'subcon_progress';
    46	const SC_REF_PAYMENT  = 'subcon_payment';
    47	const SC_TRANSITIONS = [
    48	    'Draft'      => ['Diajukan', 'Dibatalkan'],
    49	    'Diajukan'   => ['Disetujui', 'Draft', 'Dibatalkan'],
    50	    'Disetujui'  => ['Selesai', 'Dibatalkan'],
    51	    'Selesai'    => [],
    52	    'Dibatalkan' => [],
    53	];
    54	
    55	class ScError extends Exception {}
    56	
    57	function sc_send($success, $data = [], $message = '', $extra = []) {
    58	    $resp = array_merge(['success' => $success, 'message' => $message], $extra);
    59	    if ($success) {
    60	        $resp['data'] = $data;
    61	        if (!isset($resp['totalCount'])) $resp['totalCount'] = is_array($data) ? count($data) : 0;    // totalCount dari $extra (paging) diutamakan
    62	    }
    63	    if (ob_get_length()) { ob_clean(); }
    64	    echo json_encode($resp, JSON_INVALID_UTF8_SUBSTITUTE);
    65	    exit;
    66	}
    67	
    68	function sc_f($v): float { return round((float)$v, 2); }
    69	function sc_uid() { return isset($_SESSION['user_id']) ? (int)$_SESSION['user_id'] : null; }
    70	function sc_date(string $d, string $label): string {
    71	    $x = DateTime::createFromFormat('Y-m-d', $d);
    72	    if (!$x || $x->format('Y-m-d') !== $d) throw new ScError($label . ' tidak valid (YYYY-MM-DD).');
    73	    return $d;
    74	}
    75	function sc_pct($v, string $label): float {
    76	    $p = round((float)$v, 2);
    77	    if ($p < 0 || $p > 100) throw new ScError($label . ' harus antara 0 - 100.');
    78	    return $p;
    79	}

    81	/** Pastikan enum reference_type jurnal memuat 'subcon_progress' & 'subcon_payment' (bila kolom berupa enum). */
    82	function sc_assert_ref_types($db): void {
    83	    $st = $db->query("SELECT t.typtype, t.oid FROM pg_attribute a JOIN pg_class c ON c.oid = a.attrelid JOIN pg_type t ON t.oid = a.atttypid
    84	                       WHERE c.relname = 'journal_entries' AND a.attname = 'reference_type' AND NOT a.attisdropped LIMIT 1");
    85	    $r = $st ? $st->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC) : false;
    86	    if (!$r || $r['typtype'] !== 'e') return;
    87	    $st = $db->prepare("SELECT enumlabel FROM pg_enum WHERE enumtypid = :o");
    88	    $st->execute([':o' => $r['oid']]);
    89	    $have = $st->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_COLUMN);
    90	    foreach ([SC_REF_PROGRESS, SC_REF_PAYMENT] as $need) {
    91	        if (!in_array($need, $have, true)) {
    92	            throw new ScError("Tipe jurnal '{$need}' belum terdaftar di database. Jalankan ALTER TYPE public.reference_type ADD VALUE untuk nilai itu (lihat spk.subcon.step1.sql) terlebih dahulu.");
    93	        }
    94	    }
    95	}
    96	/** Apakah kolom subcon_contracts.spk_id (tautan ke SPK subkon) sudah ada di database ini? */
    97	function sc_has_spk_link($db): bool {
    98	    static $ok = null;
    99	    if ($ok === null) {
   100	        $ok = (bool)$db->query("SELECT 1 FROM information_schema.columns
   101	                                 WHERE table_schema = 'public' AND table_name = 'subcon_contracts' AND column_name = 'spk_id' LIMIT 1")->fetchColumn();
   102	    }
   103	    return $ok;
   104	}
   105	function sc_has_journal($db, string $ref, int $id): bool {
   106	    $st = $db->prepare("SELECT 1 FROM journal_entries WHERE reference_type::text = :t AND reference_id = :id LIMIT 1");
   107	    $st->execute([':t' => $ref, ':id' => $id]);
   108	    return (bool)$st->fetchColumn();
   109	}
   110	function sc_post($db, string $rule, string $ref, int $id, string $date, float $amount, string $desc, ?int $creditAccountId = null): void {
   111	    if ($amount <= 0.004) return;
   112	    $p = ['rule_code' => $rule, 'reference_type' => $ref, 'reference_id' => $id, 'journal_date' => $date, 'amount' => $amount, 'description' => $desc];
   113	    if ($creditAccountId) $p['credit_account_id'] = $creditAccountId;
   114	    $r = AutoPostJournal($db, $p);
   115	    if (!$r['success']) throw new ScError('Gagal posting jurnal ' . $rule . ': ' . $r['message']);
   116	}

   118	function sc_contract($db, int $id, bool $lock = false): array {
   119	    $st = $db->prepare("SELECT * FROM subcon_contracts WHERE id = :id" . ($lock ? ' FOR UPDATE' : ''));
   120	    $st->execute([':id' => $id]);
   121	    $c = $st->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
   122	    if (!$c) throw new ScError('SPK tidak ditemukan.');
   123	    foreach (['contract_value', 'ppn_rate', 'pph_rate', 'retention_pct', 'dp_pct'] as $k) $c[$k] = (float)$c[$k];
   124	    return $c;
   125	}

   127	/** Total prestasi disetujui & pembayaran posted untuk satu SPK (dasar sisa uang muka, retensi, hutang). */
   128	function sc_totals($db, int $cid): array {
   129	    $st = $db->prepare("SELECT COALESCE(SUM(gross_amount),0) g, COALESCE(SUM(ppn_amount),0) ppn, COALESCE(SUM(pph_amount),0) pph,
   130	                               COALESCE(SUM(retention_amount),0) ret, COALESCE(SUM(dp_recovery),0) dprec, COALESCE(SUM(net_payable),0) net
   131	                          FROM subcon_progress WHERE contract_id = :c AND status = 'Disetujui'");
   132	    $st->execute([':c' => $cid]);
   133	    $t = $st->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
   134	    $st = $db->prepare("SELECT COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN pay_type='DP' THEN amount END),0) dp, COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN pay_type='PROGRESS' THEN amount END),0) pr,
   135	                               COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN pay_type='RETENSI' THEN amount END),0) rt
   136	                          FROM subcon_payments WHERE contract_id = :c AND status = 'Posted'");
   137	    $st->execute([':c' => $cid]);
   138	    $p = $st->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
   139	    $o = ['gross' => sc_f($t['g']), 'ppn' => sc_f($t['ppn']), 'pph' => sc_f($t['pph']), 'retention' => sc_f($t['ret']),
   140	          'dp_recovered' => sc_f($t['dprec']), 'net' => sc_f($t['net']),
   141	          'dp_paid' => sc_f($p['dp']), 'progress_paid' => sc_f($p['pr']), 'retention_paid' => sc_f($p['rt'])];
   142	    $o['dp_balance']        = sc_f($o['dp_paid'] - $o['dp_recovered']);        // uang muka yang belum dipotong
   143	    $o['retention_balance'] = sc_f($o['retention'] - $o['retention_paid']);    // retensi ditahan, belum dilepas
   144	    $o['payable_balance']   = sc_f($o['net'] - $o['progress_paid']);           // hutang prestasi belum dibayar
   145	    return $o;
   146	}
   147	
   148	/** Progres kumulatif sebelumnya (disetujui) per baris task SPK. */
   149	function sc_prev_map($db, int $cid, int $exclProgressId = 0): array {
   150	    $st = $db->prepare("SELECT d.contract_task_id, MAX(d.cum_pct) AS m FROM subcon_progress_dtl d JOIN subcon_progress p ON p.id = d.progress_id
   151	                         WHERE p.contract_id = :c AND p.status = 'Disetujui' AND p.id <> :x GROUP BY d.contract_task_id");
   152	    $st->execute([':c' => $cid, ':x' => $exclProgressId]);
   153	    $m = [];
   154	    foreach ($st->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC) as $r) $m[(int)$r['contract_task_id']] = (float)$r['m'];
   155	    return $m;
   156	}
   157	
   158	/** Hitung PPN, PPh, retensi, potongan uang muka dan nilai bayar satu prestasi. */
   159	function sc_calc(array $c, float $gross, float $cumPct, float $dpBalance): array {
   160	    $ppn = round($gross * $c['ppn_rate'] / 100, 2);
   161	    $pph = round($gross * $c['pph_rate'] / 100, 2);
   162	    $ret = round($gross * $c['retention_pct'] / 100, 2);
   163	    $plan = round($gross * $c['dp_pct'] / 100, 2);
   164	    $dpRec = ($cumPct >= 99.999) ? $dpBalance : min($dpBalance, $plan);       // progres 100% -> sisa uang muka dipotong semua
   165	    $dpRec = max(0.0, round($dpRec, 2));
   166	    $net = round($gross + $ppn - $pph - $ret - $dpRec, 2);
   167	    if ($net < 0) { $dpRec = max(0.0, round($dpRec + $net, 2)); $net = round($gross + $ppn - $pph - $ret - $dpRec, 2); }
   168	    return ['ppn' => $ppn, 'pph' => $pph, 'retention' => $ret, 'dp_recovery' => $dpRec, 'net' => $net];
   169	}

    83	    function app_require_active_user(): void {
    84	        global $conn;
    85	        $userId = (int)($_SESSION['user_id'] ?? 0);
    86	        if ($userId <= 0) {
    87	            http_response_code(401);
    88	            exit('Sesi berakhir. Silakan login kembali.');
    89	        }
    90	        $result = @pg_query_params(
    91	            $conn,
    92	            "SELECT 1 FROM m_user_system WHERE id = $1 AND active IS TRUE LIMIT 1",
    93	            array($userId)
    94	        );
    95	        if (!$result) {
    96	            error_log('[SECURITY] Could not verify active user session: user_id=' . $userId . '; ' . pg_last_error($conn));
    97	            http_response_code(503);
    98	            exit('Status akun tidak dapat diverifikasi. Silakan coba kembali.');
    99	        }
   100	        if (pg_num_rows($result) < 1) {
   101	            error_log('[SECURITY] Blocked inactive or missing user session: user_id=' . $userId);
   102	            $_SESSION = array();
   103	            if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_ACTIVE) {
   104	                session_regenerate_id(true);
   105	            }
   106	            http_response_code(401);
   107	            $isJson = (!empty($_SERVER['HTTP_ACCEPT']) && strpos($_SERVER['HTTP_ACCEPT'], 'application/json') !== false)
   108	                || (!empty($_SERVER['HTTP_X_REQUESTED_WITH']) && strtolower($_SERVER['HTTP_X_REQUESTED_WITH']) === 'xmlhttprequest');
   109	            if ($isJson) {
   110	                header('Content-Type: application/json; charset=utf-8');
   111	                echo json_encode(array('success' => false, 'message' => 'Akun tidak aktif atau sesi tidak berlaku. Silakan login kembali.'));
   112	            } else {
   113	                echo 'Akun tidak aktif atau sesi tidak berlaku. Silakan login kembali.';
   114	            }
   115	            exit;

   346	function rAmountReport($number, $round) {
   347	    if ($number > 0) {
   348	        return number_format(floor($number / $round) * $round, 0, '.', ',');
   349	    } elseif ($number < 0) {
   350	        return number_format(floor((($number * -1) / $round) * $round) * -1, 0, '.', ',');
   351	    } else {
   352	        return '-';
   353	    }

   356	function GetFormId($formURl) {
   357	    global $conn;
   358	    $formName = explode("/", $formURl);
   359	    $getFormName = end($formName);
   360	    $sql = "select * from m_form_name where php_url='" . pg_escape_string($conn, $getFormName) . "'";
   361	    $output = pg_query($conn, $sql) or die('Connection failed: ' . pg_last_error($conn));
   362	    $formId = null;
   363	    if (pg_num_rows($output) > 0) {
   364	        while ($objid = pg_fetch_array($output, null, PGSQL_ASSOC)) {
   365	            $formId = $objid["id"];
   366	        }
   367	    }
   368	    return $formId;
   369	}

===== 6. PEMERIKSAAN AKSES SETIAP TASK SUBCON =====
   165	    $dpRec = max(0.0, round($dpRec, 2));
   166	    $net = round($gross + $ppn - $pph - $ret - $dpRec, 2);
   167	    if ($net < 0) { $dpRec = max(0.0, round($dpRec + $net, 2)); $net = round($gross + $ppn - $pph - $ret - $dpRec, 2); }
   168	    return ['ppn' => $ppn, 'pph' => $pph, 'retention' => $ret, 'dp_recovery' => $dpRec, 'net' => $net];
   169	}

   171	if (empty($_SESSION['user_id'])) {
   172	    http_response_code(401);
   173	    sc_send(false, [], 'Sesi berakhir, silakan login ulang.');
   174	}

   176	
   177	$task = $_POST['task'] ?? $_GET['task'] ?? '';
   178	
   179	$scMutationTasks = [
   180	    'save_subcontractor', 'save_contract', 'change_status', 'delete_contract',
   181	    'save_progress', 'approve_progress', 'cancel_progress', 'save_payment', 'cancel_payment'
   182	];
   183	if (in_array($task, $scMutationTasks, true)
   184	    && strtoupper($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] ?? 'GET') !== 'POST') {
   185	    http_response_code(405);
   186	    header('Allow: GET, POST');
   187	    sc_send(false, [], 'Operasi perubahan data subkon wajib menggunakan POST.');
   188	}
   189	
   190	// Otorisasi operasi berdasarkan FormAccess dari router.
   191	// Level: 1 baca, 2 tambah, 3 ubah/approval, 4 pembatalan finansial/penghapusan.
   192	if (empty($_SESSION['user_id']) || !is_numeric($_SESSION['user_id']) || (int)$_SESSION['user_id'] <= 0) {
   193	    http_response_code(401);
   194	    sc_send(false, [], 'Sesi berakhir, silakan login ulang.');
   195	}
   196	$scAccess = (int)($_SESSION['FormAccess'] ?? 0);
   197	$scRequired = 1;
   198	if ($task === 'save_subcontractor') {
   199	    $scRequired = 2;
   200	} elseif ($task === 'save_contract') {
   201	    $scRequired = 2;
   202	} elseif ($task === 'save_progress') {
   203	    $scRequired = 2;
   204	} elseif ($task === 'save_payment') {
   205	    $scRequired = 2;
   206	} elseif (in_array($task, ['change_status', 'approve_progress'], true)) {
   207	    $scRequired = 3;
   208	} elseif ($task === 'cancel_progress') {
   209	    $scRequired = 4;
   210	} elseif ($task === 'cancel_payment') {
   211	    $scRequired = 4;
   212	} elseif ($task === 'delete_contract') {
   213	    $scRequired = 4;
   214	}
   215	if ($scAccess < $scRequired) {
   216	    http_response_code(403);
   217	    sc_send(false, [], 'Hak akses tidak mencukupi untuk operasi subkontraktor ini.');
   218	}
   219	
   220	try {
   221	    switch ($task) {
   222	
   223	        /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ master untuk combo */
   224	        case 'list_project_options':
   225	            $rows = $db->query("SELECT id, name FROM public.projects ORDER BY name ASC")->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
   226	            foreach ($rows as &$r) $r['id'] = (int)$r['id'];
   227	            unset($r);
   228	            sc_send(true, $rows);
   229	            break;
   230	
   231	        case 'list_subcontractors':
   232	            $rows = $db->query("SELECT id, name, npwp_no, is_pkp, pph_rate FROM public.subcontractors WHERE active ORDER BY name ASC")->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
   233	            foreach ($rows as &$r) { $r['id'] = (int)$r['id']; $r['pph_rate'] = (float)$r['pph_rate']; }
   234	            unset($r);
   235	            sc_send(true, $rows);
   236	            break;
   237	
   238	        case 'save_subcontractor':
   239	            $id = (int)($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
   240	            $name = trim((string)($_POST['name'] ?? ''));
   241	            if ($name === '') throw new ScError('Nama subkontraktor wajib diisi.');
   242	            $pph = sc_pct($_POST['pph_rate'] ?? 0, 'Tarif PPh');
   243	            $v = [':name' => $name, ':npwp' => trim((string)($_POST['npwp_no'] ?? '')) ?: null,
   244	                  ':pkp' => (($_POST['is_pkp'] ?? '') === '1' || ($_POST['is_pkp'] ?? '') === 'true') ? 't' : 'f',
   245	                  ':cp' => trim((string)($_POST['contact_person'] ?? '')) ?: null, ':ph' => trim((string)($_POST['phone'] ?? '')) ?: null,
   246	                  ':ad' => trim((string)($_POST['address'] ?? '')) ?: null, ':bn' => trim((string)($_POST['bank_name'] ?? '')) ?: null,
   247	                  ':ba' => trim((string)($_POST['bank_account_no'] ?? '')) ?: null, ':bh' => trim((string)($_POST['bank_account_name'] ?? '')) ?: null,
   248	                  ':pph' => $pph];
   249	            if ($id > 0) {
   250	                $v[':id'] = $id;
   251	                $db->prepare("UPDATE subcontractors SET name=:name, npwp_no=:npwp, is_pkp=:pkp, contact_person=:cp, phone=:ph, address=:ad,
   252	                                     bank_name=:bn, bank_account_no=:ba, bank_account_name=:bh, pph_rate=:pph WHERE id=:id")->execute($v);
   253	            } else {
   254	                $st = $db->prepare("INSERT INTO subcontractors (name, npwp_no, is_pkp, contact_person, phone, address, bank_name, bank_account_no, bank_account_name, pph_rate)
   255	                                    VALUES (:name,:npwp,:pkp,:cp,:ph,:ad,:bn,:ba,:bh,:pph) RETURNING id");
   256	                $st->execute($v);
   257	                $id = (int)$st->fetchColumn();
   258	            }
   259	            sc_send(true, ['id' => $id], 'Subkontraktor tersimpan.');
   260	            break;

===== B. IMPLEMENTASI SPK ACCESS =====
     1	<?php
     2	/**
     3	 * spkCRD.php — SPK (Surat Perintah Kerja)
     4	 *
     5	 * SPK = izin mengeluarkan material gudang untuk satu proyek. Hanya bisa DITERBITKAN setelah proyek menerima pembayaran
     6	 * termin, dan qty per material dibatasi: kuota kumulatif = qty RAB x (% kontrak yang sudah dibayar x 2), maks 100%
     7	 * (lihat php-func/function.spk.php). Warehouse Issuing proyek wajib memilih SPK dan qty issue dibatasi sisa jatah SPK.
     8	 *
     9	 * Status : Draft -> Diterbitkan -> Selesai ; Dibatalkan (dari Draft / Diterbitkan yang belum dipakai issue)
    10	 * Action : list | detail | lookup_projects | lookup_tasks | lookup_subcontractors | lookup_materials | quota | save | issue | close | cancel | delete
    11	 *
    12	 * [2026-11-11] SPK INTERNAL atau SUBCON (database/subcontract_management_ddl.sql, Langkah 1 SUBCONTRACT_MANAGEMENT_DESIGN.md)
    13	 *   - spk_type INTERNAL (default, perilaku lama tidak berubah) atau SUBCON (execution_subcontractor_id wajib).
    14	 *   - SPK SUBCON boleh tanpa baris material (pekerjaan murni). Kalau ada material, aturan kuota + stok tetap sama.
    15	 *   - Syarat 'harus ada pembayaran klien' tetap untuk semua SPK yang melepas material; SPK SUBCON tanpa material
    16	 *     tidak dikenai syarat itu (ubah lewat SPK_SUBCON_REQUIRE_CLIENT_PAYMENT).
    17	 *   - Tipe SPK tidak bisa diubah setelah disimpan. Kontrak subkon (subcon_contracts.spk_id) menempel ke SPK SUBCON.
    18	 *   - Kode tetap jalan di database yang belum menjalankan DDL tersebut (kolom baru dicek dulu).
    19	 * Akses  : buat / ubah >= 2 (ubah form tersimpan perlu 3) ; terbitkan / tutup / batalkan >= 3 ; hapus >= 4
    20	 */
    21	ob_start();
    22	ini_set('display_errors', '0');
    23	require_once __DIR__ . '/../php-func/user.valid.php';
    24	require_once '../php-func/function.all.php';
    25	require_once '../php-class/crudClass.php';
    26	require_once '../php-func/function.stock.php';
    27	require_once '../php-func/function.spk.php';
    28	
    29	define('SPK_ACCESS_EDIT', 2);
    30	define('SPK_ACCESS_ACTION', 3);
    31	define('SPK_ACCESS_DELETE', 4);
    32	// SPK SUBCON tanpa material gudang: apakah tetap wajib menunggu pembayaran klien sebelum diterbitkan?
    33	define('SPK_SUBCON_REQUIRE_CLIENT_PAYMENT', false);
    34	// SPK SUBCON hanya boleh diterbitkan bila kontrak subkon yang terhubung berstatus Disetujui (set false untuk mematikan aturan ini)
    35	define('SPK_SUBCON_REQUIRE_CONTRACT_ON_ISSUE', true);
    36	
    37	if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_NONE) { session_start(); }
    38	$spkUserId = (int)($_SESSION['user_id'] ?? 0);
    39	if ($spkUserId <= 0) { http_response_code(401); WhFail('Sesi berakhir, silakan login ulang.'); }
    40	if ((int)($_SESSION['FormAccess'] ?? 0) < 1) { http_response_code(403); WhFail('Anda tidak memiliki hak baca SPK.'); }
    41	$action = WhReq('action', 'list');
    42	
    43	try {
    44	    switch ($action) {
    45	        case 'list':             SpkList(); break;
    46	        case 'detail':           SpkDetail(); break;
    47	        case 'lookup_projects':  SpkLookupProjects(); break;
    48	        case 'lookup_tasks':     SpkLookupTasks(); break;
    49	        case 'lookup_subcontractors': SpkLookupSubcontractors(); break;
    50	        case 'lookup_materials': SpkLookupMaterials(); break;
    51	        case 'quota':            SpkQuotaInfo(); break;
    52	        case 'save':             SpkSave(); break;
    53	        case 'issue':            SpkIssue(); break;
    54	        case 'close':            SpkClose(); break;
    55	        case 'cancel':           SpkCancel(); break;
    56	        case 'delete':           SpkDelete(); break;
    57	        default: throw new WhException('Action tidak dikenali.');
    58	    }
    59	} catch (WhException $e) {
    60	    @pg_query($conn, 'ROLLBACK');
    61	    WhFail($e->getMessage());
    62	} catch (Exception $e) {
    63	    @pg_query($conn, 'ROLLBACK');
    64	    WhLog('spkCRD: ' . $e->getMessage());
    65	    WhFail('Terjadi kesalahan pada server. Hubungi administrator.');
    66	}
