    68	/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
    69	function SpkNeed($level, $msg) {
    70	    $a = isset($_SESSION['FormAccess']) ? (int)$_SESSION['FormAccess'] : 0;
    71	    if ($a < $level) { throw new WhException($msg); }
    72	}
    73	
    74	// Apakah database ini sudah menjalankan DDL SPK subkon (kolom execution_subcontractor_id ada)?
    75	function SpkHasSubconSchema() {
    76	    static $ok = null;
    77	    if ($ok === null) {
    78	        $r = WhRow("SELECT 1 AS x FROM information_schema.columns
    79	                    WHERE table_schema='public' AND table_name='spk' AND column_name='execution_subcontractor_id' LIMIT 1");
    80	        $ok = $r ? true : false;
    81	    }
    82	    return $ok;
    83	}
    84	
    85	function SpkGet($id, $lock = false) {
    86	    $s = WhRow('SELECT * FROM spk WHERE id=$1' . ($lock ? ' FOR UPDATE' : ''), array((int)$id));
    87	    if (!$s) { throw new WhException('SPK tidak ditemukan.'); }
    88	    return $s;
    89	}
    90	
    91	// jumlah baris issue (posted) yang memakai SPK
    92	function SpkIssueCount($spkId) {
    93	    $r = WhRow("SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM material_out_header WHERE spk_id=$1 AND status='posted'", array((int)$spkId));
    94	    return (int)$r['n'];
    95	}
    96	
    97	// item SPK per material + terpakai + sisa
    98	function SpkItemsWithUsage($spkId) {
    99	    $rows = WhRows("SELECT si.material_id, m.material_code, m.material_name, COALESCE(NULLIF(si.unit,''), m.unit, '') AS unit,
   100	                           SUM(si.qty) AS qty, MAX(si.notes) AS notes
   101	                    FROM spk_items si JOIN materials m ON m.id=si.material_id
   102	                    WHERE si.spk_id=$1 GROUP BY si.material_id, m.material_code, m.material_name, COALESCE(NULLIF(si.unit,''), m.unit, '')
   103	                    ORDER BY m.material_name", array((int)$spkId));
   104	    foreach ($rows as &$r) {
   105	        $u = WhRow("SELECT COALESCE(SUM(i.qty_out),0) AS q FROM material_out_items i JOIN material_out_header h ON h.id=i.material_out_id
   106	                    WHERE h.spk_id=$1 AND i.material_id=$2 AND h.status='posted'", array((int)$spkId, (int)$r['material_id']));
   107	        $r['qty'] = round((float)$r['qty'], 4);
   108	        $r['used'] = round((float)$u['q'], 4);
   109	        $r['remaining'] = max(0, round($r['qty'] - $r['used'], 4));
   110	    }
   111	    unset($r);
   112	    return $rows;
   113	}
   114	
   115	function SpkList() {
   200	    $rows = array();
   201	    foreach ($qt['materials'] as $m) {
   202	        if ($q !== '' && strpos(strtolower($m['material_name'] . ' ' . $m['material_code']), $q) === false) { continue; }
   203	        $rows[] = $m;
   204	    }
   205	    WhJson(array('success' => true, 'totalCount' => count($rows), 'data' => $rows, 'pay' => $qt['pay']));
   206	}
   207	
   208	function SpkSave() {
   209	    WhRequirePost();
   210	    SpkNeed(SPK_ACCESS_EDIT, 'Anda tidak memiliki hak akses untuk membuat / mengubah SPK.');
   211	    $id = (int)WhPost('id');
   212	    $projectId = (int)WhPost('project_id');
   213	    $date = WhDate(WhPost('spk_date'), 'Tanggal SPK');
   214	    $foreman = trim(WhPost('foreman')); $desc = trim(WhPost('description')); $remark = trim(WhPost('remark'));
   215	    $type = strtoupper(trim((string)WhPost('spk_type')));
   216	    if ($type === '') { $type = 'INTERNAL'; }
   217	    if ($type !== 'INTERNAL' && $type !== 'SUBCON') { throw new WhException('Tipe SPK tidak valid.'); }
   218	    $subId = (int)WhPost('execution_subcontractor_id');
   219	    $ws = trim((string)WhPost('work_start_date')); $we = trim((string)WhPost('work_end_date'));
   220	    $ws = ($ws === '') ? null : WhDate($ws, 'Tanggal mulai kerja');
   221	    $we = ($we === '') ? null : WhDate($we, 'Tanggal selesai kerja');
   222	    if ($ws !== null && $we !== null && $we < $ws) { throw new WhException('Tanggal selesai kerja tidak boleh lebih awal dari tanggal mulai.'); }
   223	    $subconReady = SpkHasSubconSchema();
   224	    if ($type === 'SUBCON') {
   225	        if (!$subconReady) { throw new WhException('SPK subkon belum aktif: jalankan database/subcontract_management_ddl.sql di database ini.'); }
   226	        if ($subId < 1 || !WhRow('SELECT 1 FROM subcontractors WHERE id=$1 AND active', array($subId))) {
   227	            throw new WhException('Pilih subkontraktor yang aktif untuk SPK subkon.');
   228	        }
   229	    } else { $subId = 0; }
   230	    $detailsRaw = isset($_POST['details']) ? $_POST['details'] : '[]';
   231	    $details = is_array($detailsRaw) ? $detailsRaw : json_decode((string)$detailsRaw, true);
   232	    if (!is_array($details)) { $details = array(); }
   233	    // SPK internal wajib punya material (izin issue gudang). SPK subkon boleh pekerjaan murni tanpa material.
   234	    if ($type === 'INTERNAL' && !count($details)) { throw new WhException('Tambahkan minimal satu material.'); }
   235	    $taskRaw = isset($_POST['task_details']) ? $_POST['task_details'] : '[]';
   236	    $taskDetails = is_array($taskRaw) ? $taskRaw : json_decode((string)$taskRaw, true);
   237	    if (!is_array($taskDetails) || !count($taskDetails)) { throw new WhException('Pilih minimal satu task proyek untuk SPK.'); }
   238	    $userId = WhUserId();
   239	    if ($projectId < 1) { throw new WhException('Proyek wajib dipilih.'); }
   240	    if (!WhRow('SELECT 1 FROM projects WHERE id=$1', array($projectId))) { throw new WhException('Proyek tidak ditemukan.'); }
   241	
   242	    // gabungkan baris duplikat per material; wajib material yang ada di RAB proyek
   243	    $lines = array();
   244	    foreach ($details as $d) {
   245	        $mid = isset($d['material_id']) ? (int)$d['material_id'] : 0;
   246	        $qty = isset($d['qty']) ? WhNum($d['qty']) : 0;
   247	        if ($mid < 1) { throw new WhException('Material pada detail tidak valid.'); }
   248	        if ($qty <= 0) { throw new WhException('Qty SPK harus lebih besar dari 0.'); }
   249	        if (!WhRow('SELECT 1 FROM budget_items WHERE project_id=$1 AND material_id=$2', array($projectId, $mid))) {
   250	            $m = WhRow('SELECT material_name FROM materials WHERE id=$1', array($mid));
   251	            throw new WhException('Material ' . ($m ? $m['material_name'] : $mid) . ' tidak ada di RAB proyek ini.');
   252	        }
   253	        if (!isset($lines[$mid])) { $lines[$mid] = array('qty' => 0, 'notes' => ''); }
   254	        $lines[$mid]['qty'] = round($lines[$mid]['qty'] + $qty, 4);
   255	        if (!empty($d['notes'])) { $lines[$mid]['notes'] = trim($d['notes']); }
   256	    }
   257	    if ($type === 'INTERNAL' && !count($lines)) { throw new WhException('Tambahkan minimal satu material.'); }
   258	
   259	    WhQ('BEGIN');
   260	    // Lock proyek agar kalkulasi kuota tidak berubah saat SPK disimpan.
   261	    WhLock(0, 900000 + $projectId);
   262	    $taskIds = array();
   263	    foreach ($taskDetails as $td) {
   264	        $tid = isset($td['task_id']) ? (int)$td['task_id'] : 0;
   265	        if ($tid < 1 || isset($taskIds[$tid])) { throw new WhException('Task SPK tidak valid atau dipilih lebih dari sekali.'); }
   266	        if (!WhRow('SELECT 1 FROM public.project_tasks WHERE id=$1 AND project_id=$2 FOR UPDATE', array($tid, $projectId))) {
   267	            throw new WhException('Task yang dipilih bukan bagian dari proyek ini.');
   268	        }
   269	        // Task dapat dibagi ke beberapa SPK; validasi kuantitas dilakukan pada alokasi Task.
   270	        $taskIds[$tid] = true;
   271	    }
   272	    $qt = SpkMaterialQuota($projectId, $id);
   273	    foreach ($lines as $mid => $ln) {
   274	        if (!isset($qt['materials'][$mid])) { throw new WhException('Material ID ' . $mid . ' tidak ditemukan dalam RAB proyek.'); }
   275	        $maxQty = (float)$qt['materials'][$mid]['available'];
   276	        if ($ln['qty'] > $maxQty + 0.00005) {
   277	            throw new WhException('Qty SPK ' . $qt['materials'][$mid]['material_name'] . ' (' . WhFmt($ln['qty']) . ') melebihi maksimum tersedia (' . WhFmt($maxQty) . ').');
   278	        }
   279	    }
   280	    if ($id > 0) {
   281	        $s = SpkGet($id, true);
   282	        if ($s['status'] !== 'Draft') { throw new WhException('Hanya SPK berstatus Draft yang dapat diubah.'); }
   283	        $oldType = isset($s['spk_type']) ? $s['spk_type'] : 'INTERNAL';
   284	        if ($oldType !== $type) { throw new WhException('Tipe SPK tidak dapat diubah. Buat SPK baru dengan tipe yang diinginkan.'); }
   285	        if ($subconReady) {
   286	            $lc = WhRow('SELECT 1 AS x FROM subcon_contracts WHERE spk_id=$1', array($id));
   287	            if ($lc && ((int)$s['project_id'] !== $projectId || (int)$s['execution_subcontractor_id'] !== $subId)) {
   288	                throw new WhException('SPK ini sudah terhubung ke kontrak subkon: proyek dan subkontraktor tidak dapat diubah.');
   289	            }
   290	            WhQ('UPDATE spk SET spk_date=$2, project_id=$3, foreman=$4, description=$5, remark=$6, execution_subcontractor_id=$7, work_start_date=$8, work_end_date=$9, updated_at=now() WHERE id=$1',
   291	                array($id, $date, $projectId, $foreman, $desc, $remark, $subId > 0 ? $subId : null, $ws, $we));
   292	        } else {
   293	            WhQ('UPDATE spk SET spk_date=$2, project_id=$3, foreman=$4, description=$5, remark=$6, updated_at=now() WHERE id=$1',
   294	                array($id, $date, $projectId, $foreman, $desc, $remark));
   295	        }
   296	        WhQ('DELETE FROM spk_items WHERE spk_id=$1', array($id));
   297	        WhQ('DELETE FROM spk_tasks WHERE spk_id=$1', array($id));
   298	        $spkNo = $s['spk_no'];
   299	    } else {
   300	        $spkNo = CreateRefNo('spk', 'spk_no', 'SPK');
   301	        if ($subconReady) {
   302	            $h = WhRow("INSERT INTO spk (spk_no, spk_date, project_id, foreman, description, status, remark, created_by, spk_type, execution_subcontractor_id, work_start_date, work_end_date)
   303	                        VALUES ($1,$2,$3,$4,$5,'Draft',$6,$7,$8,$9,$10,$11) RETURNING id",
   304	                       array($spkNo, $date, $projectId, $foreman, $desc, $remark, $userId, $type, $subId > 0 ? $subId : null, $ws, $we));
   305	        } else {
   306	            $h = WhRow("INSERT INTO spk (spk_no, spk_date, project_id, foreman, description, status, remark, created_by)
   307	                        VALUES ($1,$2,$3,$4,$5,'Draft',$6,$7) RETURNING id",
   308	                       array($spkNo, $date, $projectId, $foreman, $desc, $remark, $userId));
   309	        }
   310	        $id = (int)$h['id'];
   311	    }
   312	    foreach (array_keys($taskIds) as $tid) {
   313	        WhQ('INSERT INTO spk_tasks (spk_id, task_id) VALUES ($1,$2)', array($id, (int)$tid));
   314	    }
   315	    foreach ($lines as $mid => $ln) {
   316	        $m = WhRow('SELECT unit FROM materials WHERE id=$1', array($mid));
   317	        WhQ('INSERT INTO spk_items (spk_id, material_id, qty, unit, notes) VALUES ($1,$2,$3,$4,$5)',
   318	            array($id, $mid, $ln['qty'], $m ? $m['unit'] : '', $ln['notes']));
   319	    }
   320	    WhQ('COMMIT');
   321	    WhOk('SPK tersimpan (Draft). No: ' . $spkNo, array('id' => $id, 'spk_no' => $spkNo));
   322	}
   323	
   324	// Terbitkan: syarat sudah ada pembayaran termin + qty tiap material dalam kuota
   325	function SpkIssue() {
   326	    WhRequirePost();
   327	    SpkNeed(SPK_ACCESS_ACTION, 'Anda tidak memiliki hak akses untuk menerbitkan SPK.');
   328	    $id = (int)WhPost('id'); $userId = WhUserId();
   329	    WhQ('BEGIN');
   330	    $s = SpkGet($id, true);
   331	    if ($s['status'] !== 'Draft') { throw new WhException('Hanya SPK berstatus Draft yang dapat diterbitkan.'); }
   332	    $pid = (int)$s['project_id'];
   333	    WhLock(0, 900000 + $pid);      // kunci kuota proyek agar dua SPK tidak menghabiskan kuota yang sama
   334	
   335	    $isSubcon = (isset($s['spk_type']) && $s['spk_type'] === 'SUBCON');
   336	    $spkItems = SpkItemsWithUsage($id);
   337	    // SPK subkon tanpa material gudang tidak melepas stok, jadi tidak ikut syarat "harus ada pembayaran klien"
   338	    $needPay  = !$isSubcon || count($spkItems) > 0 || SPK_SUBCON_REQUIRE_CLIENT_PAYMENT;
   339	    if ($isSubcon && SPK_SUBCON_REQUIRE_CONTRACT_ON_ISSUE) {
   340	        $kc = WhRow('SELECT status FROM subcon_contracts WHERE spk_id=$1', array($id));
   341	        if (!$kc || $kc['status'] !== 'Disetujui') { throw new WhException('SPK subkon baru dapat diterbitkan setelah kontrak subkon yang terhubung berstatus Disetujui.'); }
   342	    }
   343	    $qt = SpkMaterialQuota($pid, $id);
   344	    $pay = $qt['pay'];
   345	    if ($needPay) {
   346	        if ($pay['paid_amount'] <= 0) { throw new WhException('SPK belum dapat diterbitkan: proyek ini belum menerima pembayaran termin.'); }
   347	        if ($pay['allowed_pct'] <= 0) { throw new WhException('SPK belum dapat diterbitkan: nilai kontrak proyek belum diisi atau pembayaran belum tercatat.'); }
   348	    }
   349	
   350	    foreach ($spkItems as $it) {
   351	        $mid = (int)$it['material_id'];
   352	        if (!isset($qt['materials'][$mid])) { throw new WhException($it['material_name'] . ' tidak ada di RAB proyek.'); }
   353	        $a = $qt['materials'][$mid]['available'];
   354	        if ($it['qty'] > $a + 0.00005) {
   355	            throw new WhException("Qty {$it['material_name']} di SPK (" . WhFmt($it['qty']) . ") melebihi kuota yang tersedia (" . WhFmt($a) .
   356	                "). Kuota = RAB " . WhFmt($qt['materials'][$mid]['rab_qty']) . ' x ' . WhFmt($pay['allowed_pct']) . '% (dibayar ' .
   357	                WhFmt($pay['paid_pct']) . '% x ' . SPK_PAID_MULTIPLIER . '), dikurangi SPK aktif dan pemakaian yang sudah ada.');
   358	        }
   359	    }
   360	    WhQ("UPDATE spk SET status='Diterbitkan', issued_at=now(), issued_by=$2, updated_at=now() WHERE id=$1", array($id, $userId));
   361	    WhQ('COMMIT');
   362	    WhOk('SPK ' . $s['spk_no'] . ' diterbitkan.');
   363	}
   364	
   365	function SpkClose() {
   366	    WhRequirePost();
   367	    SpkNeed(SPK_ACCESS_ACTION, 'Anda tidak memiliki hak akses untuk menutup SPK.');
   368	    $id = (int)WhPost('id'); $userId = WhUserId();
   369	    WhQ('BEGIN');
   370	    $s = SpkGet($id, true);
   371	    if ($s['status'] !== 'Diterbitkan') { throw new WhException('Hanya SPK berstatus Diterbitkan yang dapat ditutup.'); }
   372	    WhQ("UPDATE spk SET status='Selesai', closed_at=now(), closed_by=$2, updated_at=now() WHERE id=$1", array($id, $userId));
   373	    WhQ('COMMIT');
   374	    WhOk('SPK ' . $s['spk_no'] . ' ditutup. Sisa jatah yang tidak terpakai kembali menjadi kuota proyek.');
   375	}
   376	
   377	function SpkCancel() {
   378	    WhRequirePost();
   379	    SpkNeed(SPK_ACCESS_ACTION, 'Anda tidak memiliki hak akses untuk membatalkan SPK.');
   380	    $id = (int)WhPost('id');
   381	    WhQ('BEGIN');
   382	    $s = SpkGet($id, true);
   383	    if ($s['status'] !== 'Draft' && $s['status'] !== 'Diterbitkan') { throw new WhException('SPK berstatus ' . $s['status'] . ' tidak dapat dibatalkan.'); }
   384	    if (SpkIssueCount($id) > 0) { throw new WhException('SPK ini sudah dipakai issue material. Reverse issue terkait dulu, atau gunakan Tutup SPK.'); }
   385	    if (SpkHasSubconSchema()) {
   386	        $kc = WhRow('SELECT id FROM subcon_contracts WHERE spk_id=$1', array($id));
   387	        if ($kc) {
   388	            $u = WhRow("SELECT (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM subcon_progress WHERE contract_id=$1 AND status<>'Dibatalkan') AS prg,
   389	                               (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM subcon_payments WHERE contract_id=$1 AND status='Posted') AS pay", array((int)$kc['id']));
   390	            if ((int)$u['prg'] > 0 || (int)$u['pay'] > 0) {
   391	                throw new WhException('Kontrak subkon yang terhubung sudah memiliki prestasi atau pembayaran. Batalkan prestasi / pembayaran itu dulu.');
   392	            }
   393	        }
   394	    }
   395	    WhQ("UPDATE spk SET status='Dibatalkan', updated_at=now() WHERE id=$1", array($id));
   396	    WhQ('COMMIT');
   397	    WhOk('SPK ' . $s['spk_no'] . ' dibatalkan.');
   398	}
   399	
   400	function SpkDelete() {
   401	    WhRequirePost();
   402	    SpkNeed(SPK_ACCESS_DELETE, 'Anda tidak memiliki hak akses untuk menghapus SPK.');
   403	    $id = (int)WhPost('id');
   404	    WhQ('BEGIN');
   405	    $s = SpkGet($id, true);
   406	    if ($s['status'] !== 'Draft') { throw new WhException('Hanya SPK berstatus Draft yang dapat dihapus. Gunakan Batalkan untuk SPK lain.'); }
   407	    if (SpkHasSubconSchema() && WhRow('SELECT 1 AS x FROM subcon_contracts WHERE spk_id=$1', array($id))) {
   408	        throw new WhException('SPK ini sudah terhubung ke kontrak subkon. Putuskan atau hapus kontrak subkonnya dulu.');
   409	    }
   410	    WhQ('DELETE FROM spk WHERE id=$1', array($id));       // spk_items ikut terhapus (ON DELETE CASCADE)
   411	    WhQ('COMMIT');
   412	    WhOk('SPK ' . $s['spk_no'] . ' dihapus.');
   413	}

    94	$formId = 0;
    95	
    96	// Direct physical view requests must authorize by the requested filename,
    97	// never by a stale session menu ID or a caller-supplied form_id.
    98	// The legacy menu router is the only path allowed to resolve a requested menu ID.
    99	$isLegacyMenuRouter = (bool)preg_match('~(?:^|/)views/router/?$~i', $requestPath);
   100	$isDirectViewFile = (bool)preg_match('~(?:^|/)views/([^/]+\\.php)$~i', $requestPath)
   101	    && !$isLegacyMenuRouter

   103	
   104	// Hanya legacy menu router yang boleh menerima menu_id/form_id dari URL.
   105	// Endpoint CRUD harus selalu ditentukan oleh nama endpoint di bawah; caller tidak boleh
   106	// memilih form_id lain untuk meminjam FormAccess menu yang lebih tinggi.

   111	        $formId = intval($_REQUEST['form_id']);
   112	    } elseif (isset($_GET['id']) && ctype_digit((string)$_GET['id'])) {
   113	        // Legacy /views/router?id=<form_id>; do not confuse record ids with menu IDs.
   114	        $formId = intval($_GET['id']);
   115	    }
   116	}

   118	$formAccess = null;
   119	
   120	if ($formId > 0) {
   121	    $sql = 'SELECT access_level FROM m_user_access_menu WHERE user_id = $1 AND form_id = $2 LIMIT 1';
   122	    $result = pg_query_params($conn, $sql, array($userId, $formId));
   123	    if ($result && pg_num_rows($result) > 0) {
   124	        $row = pg_fetch_assoc($result);
   125	        $formAccess = $row['access_level'];
   126	    }
   127	}
   128	
   129	// Fallback untuk halaman lama yang dibuka berdasarkan nama file.
   130	// CRUD direct request tetap harus divalidasi oleh router/endpoint; jangan
   131	// mengandalkan fallback nama file ini sebagai otorisasi operasi.
   132	if ($formAccess === null) {
   133	    $cleanUrlFile = preg_replace('/CRD\.php$/i', '.php', $urlFile);
   134	    $cleanUrlFile = preg_replace('/\.pdf\.php$/i', '.php', $cleanUrlFile);
   135	
   136	    // Endpoint AJAX/CRUD harus memetakan dirinya ke menu induk, bukan memakai FormAccess
   137	    // dari halaman lain yang kebetulan terakhir dibuka pada sesi yang sama.
   138	    $endpointAliases = array(
   139	        'project.invoiceCRD.php' => array('project.invoice.php', 'projectinvoice.php'),
   140	        'projectprogressCRD.php' => array('project.progress.php', 'project.progress.m.php'),
   141	        'ppn.backfillCRD.php'    => array('ppn.report.php'),
   142	        'rab_input.php'             => array('rab.php'),

   144	        'supplier.payment.list.print.php' => array('supplier.payment.php'),
   145	        'supplier.payment.print.php'      => array('supplier.payment.php'),
   146	        'dashboardQ.php'                    => array('dashboard.php'),
   147	    );
   148	    $endpointCandidates = array_values(array_unique(array_merge(
   149	        array($urlFile, $cleanUrlFile),
   150	        isset($endpointAliases[$urlFile]) ? $endpointAliases[$urlFile] : array()
   151	    )));
   152	    $matchClauses = array();
   153	    $params = array($userId);
   154	    foreach ($endpointCandidates as $candidate) {
   155	        $params[] = $candidate;
   156	        $matchClauses[] = "LOWER(BTRIM(b.php_url)) = LOWER($" . count($params) . ")";
   157	    }
   158	
   159	    $sql = "
   160	        SELECT a.access_level, b.id AS real_form_id
   161	        FROM m_user_access_menu a
   162	        INNER JOIN m_form_name b ON a.form_id = b.id
   163	        WHERE a.user_id = $1
   164	          AND (" . implode(' OR ', $matchClauses) . ")
   165	          AND COALESCE(a.access_level, 0) > 0
   166	        ORDER BY a.access_level ASC, b.id ASC
   167	        LIMIT 1
   168	    ";
   169	    $result = pg_query_params($conn, $sql, $params);
   170	
   171	    if ($result && pg_num_rows($result) > 0) {
   172	        $row = pg_fetch_assoc($result);
   173	        $formAccess = $row['access_level'];
   174	        if ($formId <= 0) {
   175	            $formId = intval($row['real_form_id']);
   176	        }
   177	    }
   178	}
   179	
   180	if ($formAccess === null || intval($formAccess) <= 0 || $formId <= 0) {
   181	    if ($isAjax) {
   182	        http_response_code(403);
   183	        header('Content-Type: application/json; charset=utf-8');
   184	        echo json_encode(array('success' => false, 'message' => 'Akses ditolak: Anda tidak memiliki hak akses ke modul ini.'));
   185	        exit;
   186	    }
   187	    http_response_code(403);
   188	    exit('Akses ditolak: Anda tidak memiliki hak akses ke modul ini.');
   189	}

   115	function SpkList() {
   116	    list($start, $limit) = WhPage();
   117	    $q = WhReq('query');
   118	    $params = array(); $where = '1=1';
   119	    if ($q !== '') {
   120	        $params[] = '%' . $q . '%';
   121	        $where .= ' AND (s.spk_no ILIKE $1 OR p.name ILIKE $1 OR s.foreman ILIKE $1 OR s.description ILIKE $1)';
   122	    }
   123	    $c = WhRow("SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM spk s JOIN projects p ON p.id=s.project_id WHERE $where", $params);
   124	    $params[] = $limit;  $pl = '$' . count($params);
   125	    $params[] = $start;  $ps = '$' . count($params);
   126	    $ready = SpkHasSubconSchema();
   127	    $sx = $ready
   128	        ? ", s.spk_type, s.execution_subcontractor_id, sb.name AS subcontractor_name, sc.id AS contract_id, sc.spk_no AS contract_no, sc.status AS contract_status"
   129	        : ", 'INTERNAL'::text AS spk_type, NULL::int AS execution_subcontractor_id, NULL::text AS subcontractor_name, NULL::int AS contract_id, NULL::text AS contract_no, NULL::text AS contract_status";
   130	    $jx = $ready
   131	        ? " LEFT JOIN subcontractors sb ON sb.id=s.execution_subcontractor_id LEFT JOIN subcon_contracts sc ON sc.spk_id=s.id"
   132	        : "";
   133	    $rows = WhRows("SELECT s.id, s.spk_no, s.spk_date, s.project_id, p.name AS project_name, s.foreman, s.description, s.status, s.remark $sx,
   134	                           (SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT x.material_id) FROM spk_items x WHERE x.spk_id=s.id) AS item_count
   135	                    FROM spk s JOIN projects p ON p.id=s.project_id $jx
   136	                    WHERE $where ORDER BY s.spk_date DESC, s.id DESC LIMIT $pl OFFSET $ps", $params);
   137	    // persen terpakai = rata-rata (issue / qty SPK, maks 100%) per material
   138	    foreach ($rows as &$r) {
   139	        $items = SpkItemsWithUsage($r['id']);
   140	        $sum = 0.0;
   141	        foreach ($items as $it) { $sum += ($it['qty'] > 0) ? min(1.0, $it['used'] / $it['qty']) : 0.0; }
   142	        $r['used_pct'] = count($items) ? round($sum / count($items) * 100, 1) : 0;
   143	    }
   144	    unset($r);
   145	    WhJson(array('success' => true, 'totalCount' => (int)$c['n'], 'data' => $rows));
   146	}
   147	
   148	function SpkDetail() {
   149	    $id = (int)WhReq('id');
   150	    $s = SpkGet($id);
   151	    $p = WhRow('SELECT p.name, c.name AS client_name FROM projects p LEFT JOIN clients c ON c.id=p.client_id WHERE p.id=$1', array((int)$s['project_id']));
   152	    $pay = SpkProjectPaid($s['project_id']);
   153	    $tasks = WhRows("SELECT t.id, t.task_name, COALESCE(t.description,'') AS description, t.start_date, t.end_date, COALESCE(t.status,'') AS status
   154	                     FROM spk_tasks st JOIN public.project_tasks t ON t.id=st.task_id WHERE st.spk_id=$1 ORDER BY t.start_date NULLS LAST, t.id", array($id));
   155	    $sub = null; $contract = null;
   156	    if (SpkHasSubconSchema()) {
   157	        if (!empty($s['execution_subcontractor_id'])) {
   158	            $sub = WhRow("SELECT id, name, COALESCE(code,'') AS code FROM subcontractors WHERE id=$1", array((int)$s['execution_subcontractor_id']));
   159	        }
   160	        $contract = WhRow("SELECT id, spk_no AS contract_no, title, contract_value, status FROM subcon_contracts WHERE spk_id=$1", array($id));
   161	    }
   162	    WhJson(array('success' => true, 'header' => array_merge($s, array('project_name' => $p['name'], 'client_name' => $p['client_name'])),
   163	                 'pay' => $pay, 'items' => SpkItemsWithUsage($id), 'tasks' => $tasks, 'issue_count' => SpkIssueCount($id),
   164	                 'subcontractor' => $sub ? $sub : null, 'contract' => $contract ? $contract : null));
   165	}

   167	function SpkLookupProjects() {
   168	    $q = WhReq('query');
   169	    $rows = WhRows("SELECT p.id, p.name FROM projects p WHERE p.name ILIKE $1 ORDER BY p.name LIMIT 100", array('%' . $q . '%'));
   170	    WhJson(array('success' => true, 'totalCount' => count($rows), 'data' => $rows));
   171	}

   173	function SpkLookupSubcontractors() {
   174	    if (!SpkHasSubconSchema()) { WhJson(array('success' => true, 'totalCount' => 0, 'data' => array())); return; }
   175	    $q = WhReq('query');
   176	    $rows = WhRows("SELECT id, name, COALESCE(code,'') AS code FROM subcontractors
   177	                    WHERE active AND (name ILIKE $1 OR COALESCE(code,'') ILIKE $1) ORDER BY name LIMIT 100", array('%' . $q . '%'));
   178	    WhJson(array('success' => true, 'totalCount' => count($rows), 'data' => $rows));
   179	}

   181	function SpkLookupTasks() {
   182	    $pid = (int)WhReq('project_id'); $exclude = (int)WhReq('exclude_id');
   183	    if ($pid < 1 || !WhRow('SELECT 1 FROM projects WHERE id=$1', array($pid))) { throw new WhException('Pilih proyek yang valid.'); }
   184	    // Task boleh digunakan pada beberapa SPK. Kuantitas per Task dikontrol melalui tabel alokasi.
   185	    $rows = WhRows("SELECT t.id, t.task_name, COALESCE(t.description,'') AS description, t.start_date, t.end_date, COALESCE(t.status,'') AS status
   186	                    FROM public.project_tasks t WHERE t.project_id=$1
   187	                    ORDER BY t.start_date NULLS LAST, t.id", array($pid));
   188	    WhJson(array('success'=>true,'totalCount'=>count($rows),'data'=>$rows));
   189	}

   196	// Material RAB proyek + kuota tersedia untuk SPK baru
   197	function SpkLookupMaterials() {
   198	    $pid = (int)WhReq('project_id'); $excl = (int)WhReq('exclude_id'); $q = strtolower(WhReq('query'));
   199	    $qt = SpkMaterialQuota($pid, $excl);
   200	    $rows = array();
   201	    foreach ($qt['materials'] as $m) {
   202	        if ($q !== '' && strpos(strtolower($m['material_name'] . ' ' . $m['material_code']), $q) === false) { continue; }
   203	        $rows[] = $m;
   204	    }
   205	    WhJson(array('success' => true, 'totalCount' => count($rows), 'data' => $rows, 'pay' => $qt['pay']));
   206	}

===== B. SUBCON: HEADER DAN VALIDASI REQUEST =====
     1	<?php
     2	/**
     3	 * subconCRD.php
     4	 * ============================================================
     5	 * Backend Modul Subkontraktor (js/subcon.js, views/subcon.php). Pola sama dengan poCRD.php: PDO (class crud),
     6	 * dispatch lewat $_REQUEST['task'], respons JSON {success, message, data, totalCount}. Migrasi: sql/2026-10-30.subcon.sql
     7	 *
     8	 * Rantai dokumen (semua bisa ditelusuri, tidak ada yang dihapus -- hanya Dibatalkan dengan alasan):
     9	 *   Proyek -> (Task proyek) -> SPK (subcon_contracts) -> Prestasi (subcon_progress) -> Pembayaran (subcon_payments) -> Jurnal
    10	 *
    11	 * SPK     : Draft -> Diajukan -> Disetujui -> Selesai ; Dibatalkan dari Draft/Diajukan/Disetujui selama belum ada prestasi/pembayaran.
    12	 *           Nilai SPK (DPP) dibagi ke task proyek (project_tasks); total pembagian wajib = nilai kontrak. Opsional terikat satu baris RAB:
    13	 *           total SPK pada baris RAB itu tidak boleh melebihi nilai anggaran baris (qty_plan x unit_price_plan).
    14	 * Prestasi: per periode, progres kumulatif % per task (tidak boleh turun, maks 100). Nilai periode = nilai task x (kum - sebelumnya)/100.
    15	 *           PPN = nilai x tarif SPK ; PPh final dipotong = nilai x tarif ; retensi = nilai x % ; potongan uang muka proporsional
    16	 *           (nilai x dp% , maks sisa uang muka; di progres 100% sisa uang muka dipotong semua) ;
    17	 *           bayar = nilai + PPN - PPh - retensi - potongan uang muka.
    18	 *           Hanya satu prestasi Draft per SPK. Disetujui = jurnal (Dr WIP 1700 / PPN Masukan 2400 ; Cr Hutang Subkon 2110 ;
    19	 *           potongan: Dr 2110 / Cr Hutang PPh 2130, Hutang Retensi 2120, Uang Muka Subkon 1410). Batal hanya prestasi Disetujui TERAKHIR
    20	 *           dan belum ada pembayaran.
    21	 * Bayar   : DP (Dr 1410 / Cr kas-bank, <= uang muka SPK), PROGRESS (Dr 2110 / Cr kas-bank, <= sisa prestasi), RETENSI
    22	 *           (Dr 2120 / Cr kas-bank, <= retensi ditahan, hanya setelah SPK Selesai). Batal = reverse jurnal.
    23	 * Kontrol : SC_REQUIRE_OTHER_APPROVER=true -> penyetuju SPK / prestasi harus user lain selain pembuatnya.
    24	 * Task    : list_project_options | list_subcontractors | save_subcontractor | list_project_tasks | list_budget_items | list_accounts |
    25	 *           list_contracts | load_contract | save_contract | change_status | delete_contract | progress_prepare | save_progress |
    26	 *           approve_progress | cancel_progress | save_payment | cancel_payment
    27	 * ============================================================
    28	 */

    57	function sc_send($success, $data = [], $message = '', $extra = []) {
    58	    $resp = array_merge(['success' => $success, 'message' => $message], $extra);
    59	    if ($success) {
    60	        $resp['data'] = $data;
    61	        if (!isset($resp['totalCount'])) $resp['totalCount'] = is_array($data) ? count($data) : 0;    // totalCount dari $extra (paging) diutamakan
    62	    }
    63	    if (ob_get_length()) { ob_clean(); }
    64	    echo json_encode($resp, JSON_INVALID_UTF8_SUBSTITUTE);
    65	    exit;
    66	}

    68	function sc_f($v): float { return round((float)$v, 2); }
    69	function sc_uid() { return isset($_SESSION['user_id']) ? (int)$_SESSION['user_id'] : null; }
    70	function sc_date(string $d, string $label): string {
    71	    $x = DateTime::createFromFormat('Y-m-d', $d);
    72	    if (!$x || $x->format('Y-m-d') !== $d) throw new ScError($label . ' tidak valid (YYYY-MM-DD).');
    73	    return $d;
    74	}
    75	function sc_pct($v, string $label): float {
    76	    $p = round((float)$v, 2);
    77	    if ($p < 0 || $p > 100) throw new ScError($label . ' harus antara 0 - 100.');
    78	    return $p;
    79	}

    81	/** Pastikan enum reference_type jurnal memuat 'subcon_progress' & 'subcon_payment' (bila kolom berupa enum). */
    82	function sc_assert_ref_types($db): void {
    83	    $st = $db->query("SELECT t.typtype, t.oid FROM pg_attribute a JOIN pg_class c ON c.oid = a.attrelid JOIN pg_type t ON t.oid = a.atttypid
    84	                       WHERE c.relname = 'journal_entries' AND a.attname = 'reference_type' AND NOT a.attisdropped LIMIT 1");
    85	    $r = $st ? $st->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC) : false;
    86	    if (!$r || $r['typtype'] !== 'e') return;
    87	    $st = $db->prepare("SELECT enumlabel FROM pg_enum WHERE enumtypid = :o");
    88	    $st->execute([':o' => $r['oid']]);
    89	    $have = $st->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_COLUMN);
    90	    foreach ([SC_REF_PROGRESS, SC_REF_PAYMENT] as $need) {
    91	        if (!in_array($need, $have, true)) {
    92	            throw new ScError("Tipe jurnal '{$need}' belum terdaftar di database. Jalankan ALTER TYPE public.reference_type ADD VALUE untuk nilai itu (lihat spk.subcon.step1.sql) terlebih dahulu.");
    93	        }
    94	    }
    95	}
    96	/** Apakah kolom subcon_contracts.spk_id (tautan ke SPK subkon) sudah ada di database ini? */
    97	function sc_has_spk_link($db): bool {
    98	    static $ok = null;
    99	    if ($ok === null) {
   100	        $ok = (bool)$db->query("SELECT 1 FROM information_schema.columns
   101	                                 WHERE table_schema = 'public' AND table_name = 'subcon_contracts' AND column_name = 'spk_id' LIMIT 1")->fetchColumn();
   102	    }
   103	    return $ok;
   104	}
   105	function sc_has_journal($db, string $ref, int $id): bool {
   106	    $st = $db->prepare("SELECT 1 FROM journal_entries WHERE reference_type::text = :t AND reference_id = :id LIMIT 1");
   107	    $st->execute([':t' => $ref, ':id' => $id]);
   108	    return (bool)$st->fetchColumn();
   109	}
   110	function sc_post($db, string $rule, string $ref, int $id, string $date, float $amount, string $desc, ?int $creditAccountId = null): void {
   111	    if ($amount <= 0.004) return;
   112	    $p = ['rule_code' => $rule, 'reference_type' => $ref, 'reference_id' => $id, 'journal_date' => $date, 'amount' => $amount, 'description' => $desc];
   113	    if ($creditAccountId) $p['credit_account_id'] = $creditAccountId;
   114	    $r = AutoPostJournal($db, $p);
   115	    if (!$r['success']) throw new ScError('Gagal posting jurnal ' . $rule . ': ' . $r['message']);
   116	}

   118	function sc_contract($db, int $id, bool $lock = false): array {
   119	    $st = $db->prepare("SELECT * FROM subcon_contracts WHERE id = :id" . ($lock ? ' FOR UPDATE' : ''));
   120	    $st->execute([':id' => $id]);
   121	    $c = $st->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
   122	    if (!$c) throw new ScError('SPK tidak ditemukan.');
   123	    foreach (['contract_value', 'ppn_rate', 'pph_rate', 'retention_pct', 'dp_pct'] as $k) $c[$k] = (float)$c[$k];
   124	    return $c;
   125	}

   127	/** Total prestasi disetujui & pembayaran posted untuk satu SPK (dasar sisa uang muka, retensi, hutang). */
   128	function sc_totals($db, int $cid): array {
   129	    $st = $db->prepare("SELECT COALESCE(SUM(gross_amount),0) g, COALESCE(SUM(ppn_amount),0) ppn, COALESCE(SUM(pph_amount),0) pph,
   130	                               COALESCE(SUM(retention_amount),0) ret, COALESCE(SUM(dp_recovery),0) dprec, COALESCE(SUM(net_payable),0) net
   131	                          FROM subcon_progress WHERE contract_id = :c AND status = 'Disetujui'");
   132	    $st->execute([':c' => $cid]);
   133	    $t = $st->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
   134	    $st = $db->prepare("SELECT COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN pay_type='DP' THEN amount END),0) dp, COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN pay_type='PROGRESS' THEN amount END),0) pr,
   135	                               COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN pay_type='RETENSI' THEN amount END),0) rt
   136	                          FROM subcon_payments WHERE contract_id = :c AND status = 'Posted'");
   137	    $st->execute([':c' => $cid]);
   138	    $p = $st->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
   139	    $o = ['gross' => sc_f($t['g']), 'ppn' => sc_f($t['ppn']), 'pph' => sc_f($t['pph']), 'retention' => sc_f($t['ret']),
   140	          'dp_recovered' => sc_f($t['dprec']), 'net' => sc_f($t['net']),
   141	          'dp_paid' => sc_f($p['dp']), 'progress_paid' => sc_f($p['pr']), 'retention_paid' => sc_f($p['rt'])];
   142	    $o['dp_balance']        = sc_f($o['dp_paid'] - $o['dp_recovered']);        // uang muka yang belum dipotong
   143	    $o['retention_balance'] = sc_f($o['retention'] - $o['retention_paid']);    // retensi ditahan, belum dilepas
   144	    $o['payable_balance']   = sc_f($o['net'] - $o['progress_paid']);           // hutang prestasi belum dibayar
   145	    return $o;
   146	}
   147	
   148	/** Progres kumulatif sebelumnya (disetujui) per baris task SPK. */
   149	function sc_prev_map($db, int $cid, int $exclProgressId = 0): array {
   150	    $st = $db->prepare("SELECT d.contract_task_id, MAX(d.cum_pct) AS m FROM subcon_progress_dtl d JOIN subcon_progress p ON p.id = d.progress_id
   151	                         WHERE p.contract_id = :c AND p.status = 'Disetujui' AND p.id <> :x GROUP BY d.contract_task_id");
   152	    $st->execute([':c' => $cid, ':x' => $exclProgressId]);
   153	    $m = [];
   154	    foreach ($st->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC) as $r) $m[(int)$r['contract_task_id']] = (float)$r['m'];
   155	    return $m;
   156	}
   157	
   158	/** Hitung PPN, PPh, retensi, potongan uang muka dan nilai bayar satu prestasi. */
   159	function sc_calc(array $c, float $gross, float $cumPct, float $dpBalance): array {
   160	    $ppn = round($gross * $c['ppn_rate'] / 100, 2);
   161	    $pph = round($gross * $c['pph_rate'] / 100, 2);
   162	    $ret = round($gross * $c['retention_pct'] / 100, 2);
   163	    $plan = round($gross * $c['dp_pct'] / 100, 2);
   164	    $dpRec = ($cumPct >= 99.999) ? $dpBalance : min($dpBalance, $plan);       // progres 100% -> sisa uang muka dipotong semua
   165	    $dpRec = max(0.0, round($dpRec, 2));
   166	    $net = round($gross + $ppn - $pph - $ret - $dpRec, 2);
   167	    if ($net < 0) { $dpRec = max(0.0, round($dpRec + $net, 2)); $net = round($gross + $ppn - $pph - $ret - $dpRec, 2); }
   168	    return ['ppn' => $ppn, 'pph' => $pph, 'retention' => $ret, 'dp_recovery' => $dpRec, 'net' => $net];
   169	}

   193	    http_response_code(401);
   194	    sc_send(false, [], 'Sesi berakhir, silakan login ulang.');
   195	}
   196	$scAccess = (int)($_SESSION['FormAccess'] ?? 0);
   197	$scRequired = 1;
   198	if ($task === 'save_subcontractor') {
   199	    $scRequired = 2;
   200	} elseif ($task === 'save_contract') {
   201	    $scRequired = 2;
   202	} elseif ($task === 'save_progress') {
   203	    $scRequired = 2;
   204	} elseif ($task === 'save_payment') {
   205	    $scRequired = 2;
   206	} elseif (in_array($task, ['change_status', 'approve_progress'], true)) {
   207	    $scRequired = 3;
   208	} elseif ($task === 'cancel_progress') {
   209	    $scRequired = 4;
   210	} elseif ($task === 'cancel_payment') {
   211	    $scRequired = 4;
   212	} elseif ($task === 'delete_contract') {
   213	    $scRequired = 4;
   214	}
   215	if ($scAccess < $scRequired) {
   216	    http_response_code(403);
   217	    sc_send(false, [], 'Hak akses tidak mencukupi untuk operasi subkontraktor ini.');
   218	}
   219	
   220	try {
   221	    switch ($task) {
   222	
   223	        /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ master untuk combo */
   224	        case 'list_project_options':
   225	            $rows = $db->query("SELECT id, name FROM public.projects ORDER BY name ASC")->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);

===== C. SUBCON: LOAD DAN SAVE CONTRACT =====
   317	        case 'list_contracts':
   318	            $start = (int)($_REQUEST['start'] ?? 0);
   319	            $limit = (int)($_REQUEST['limit'] ?? 250);
   320	            $query = trim($_REQUEST['query'] ?? '');
   321	            $where = ''; $params = [];
   322	            if ($query !== '') {
   323	                $where = "WHERE (c.spk_no ILIKE :q OR c.title ILIKE :q OR p.name ILIKE :q OR s.name ILIKE :q)";
   324	                $params[':q'] = '%' . $query . '%';
   325	            }
   326	            $hasLink = sc_has_spk_link($db);
   327	            $lx = $hasLink ? "c.spk_id, ls.spk_no AS linked_spk_no, ls.status AS linked_spk_status,"
   328	                           : "NULL::int AS spk_id, NULL::text AS linked_spk_no, NULL::text AS linked_spk_status,";
   329	            $jx = $hasLink ? "LEFT JOIN spk ls ON ls.id = c.spk_id" : "";
   330	            $st = $db->prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM subcon_contracts c JOIN public.projects p ON p.id = c.project_id
   331	                                  JOIN subcontractors s ON s.id = c.subcontractor_id {$where}");
   332	            $st->execute($params);
   333	            $total = (int)$st->fetchColumn();
   334	
   335	            $st = $db->prepare("
   336	                SELECT c.id, c.spk_no, c.project_id, p.name AS project_name, c.subcontractor_id, s.name AS subcontractor_name, c.title,
   337	                       c.contract_value, c.ppn_rate, c.pph_rate, c.retention_pct, c.dp_pct, c.start_date, c.end_date, c.status,
   338	                       {$lx}
   339	                       COALESCE(pr.gross,0) AS billed_gross, COALESCE(pr.net,0) AS billed_net, COALESCE(pr.ret,0) AS retention_held,
   340	                       COALESCE(pay.dp,0) AS dp_paid, COALESCE(pay.pr,0) AS progress_paid, COALESCE(pay.rt,0) AS retention_paid
   341	                  FROM subcon_contracts c
   342	                  JOIN public.projects p ON p.id = c.project_id
   343	                  JOIN subcontractors s ON s.id = c.subcontractor_id
   344	                  {$jx}
   345	                  LEFT JOIN (SELECT contract_id, SUM(gross_amount) gross, SUM(net_payable) net, SUM(retention_amount) ret
   346	                               FROM subcon_progress WHERE status = 'Disetujui' GROUP BY contract_id) pr ON pr.contract_id = c.id
   347	                  LEFT JOIN (SELECT contract_id, SUM(CASE WHEN pay_type='DP' THEN amount END) dp, SUM(CASE WHEN pay_type='PROGRESS' THEN amount END) pr,
   348	                                    SUM(CASE WHEN pay_type='RETENSI' THEN amount END) rt
   349	                               FROM subcon_payments WHERE status = 'Posted' GROUP BY contract_id) pay ON pay.contract_id = c.id
   350	                  {$where}
   351	                 ORDER BY c.id DESC LIMIT :limit OFFSET :start");
   352	            foreach ($params as $k => $v) $st->bindValue($k, $v);
   353	            $st->bindValue(':limit', $limit, PDO::PARAM_INT);
   354	            $st->bindValue(':start', $start, PDO::PARAM_INT);
   355	            $st->execute();
   356	            $rows = $st->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
   357	            foreach ($rows as &$r) {
   358	                $r['id'] = (int)$r['id']; $r['project_id'] = (int)$r['project_id']; $r['subcontractor_id'] = (int)$r['subcontractor_id'];
   359	                foreach (['contract_value', 'ppn_rate', 'pph_rate', 'retention_pct', 'dp_pct', 'billed_gross', 'billed_net', 'retention_held', 'dp_paid', 'progress_paid', 'retention_paid'] as $k) $r[$k] = (float)$r[$k];
   360	                $r['progress_pct'] = $r['contract_value'] > 0 ? round($r['billed_gross'] / $r['contract_value'] * 100, 2) : 0;
   361	                $r['payable_balance'] = sc_f($r['billed_net'] - $r['progress_paid']);
   362	            }
   363	            unset($r);
   364	            sc_send(true, $rows, '', ['totalCount' => $total]);
   365	            break;
   366	
   367	        /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ detail SPK (panel detail) */
   368	        case 'load_contract':
   369	            $id = (int)($_REQUEST['id'] ?? 0);
   370	            $c = sc_contract($db, $id);
   371	            $st = $db->prepare("SELECT c.*, p.name AS project_name, s.name AS subcontractor_name, s.npwp_no, s.is_pkp,
   372	                                       bi.item_name AS budget_item_name, cu.login_name AS created_by_name, au.login_name AS approved_by_name,
   373	                                       xu.login_name AS cancelled_by_name, zu.login_name AS closed_by_name
   374	                                  FROM subcon_contracts c JOIN public.projects p ON p.id = c.project_id JOIN subcontractors s ON s.id = c.subcontractor_id
   375	                                  LEFT JOIN public.budget_items bi ON bi.id = c.budget_item_id
   376	                                  LEFT JOIN public.m_user_system cu ON cu.id = c.created_by LEFT JOIN public.m_user_system au ON au.id = c.approved_by
   377	                                  LEFT JOIN public.m_user_system xu ON xu.id = c.cancelled_by LEFT JOIN public.m_user_system zu ON zu.id = c.closed_by
   378	                                 WHERE c.id = :id");
   379	            $st->execute([':id' => $id]);
   380	            $hdr = $st->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
   381	            $hdr['linked_spk_no'] = null; $hdr['linked_spk_status'] = null;
   382	            if (!empty($hdr['spk_id'])) {
   383	                $st = $db->prepare("SELECT spk_no, status FROM spk WHERE id = :i");
   384	                $st->execute([':i' => (int)$hdr['spk_id']]);
   385	                $ls = $st->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
   386	                if ($ls) { $hdr['linked_spk_no'] = $ls['spk_no']; $hdr['linked_spk_status'] = $ls['status']; }
   387	            }
   388	
   389	            $prev = sc_prev_map($db, $id);
   390	            $st = $db->prepare("SELECT t.id, t.task_id, t.task_name, t.task_value, t.notes, pt.start_date, pt.end_date
   391	                                  FROM subcon_contract_tasks t LEFT JOIN public.project_tasks pt ON pt.id = t.task_id
   392	                                 WHERE t.contract_id = :id ORDER BY pt.start_date NULLS LAST, t.id");
   393	            $st->execute([':id' => $id]);
   394	            $tasks = $st->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
   395	            foreach ($tasks as &$t) {
   396	                $t['id'] = (int)$t['id']; $t['task_id'] = $t['task_id'] !== null ? (int)$t['task_id'] : null; $t['task_value'] = (float)$t['task_value'];
   397	                $t['cum_pct'] = isset($prev[$t['id']]) ? $prev[$t['id']] : 0.0;
   398	                $t['billed_amount'] = sc_f($t['task_value'] * $t['cum_pct'] / 100);
   399	            }
   400	            unset($t);
   401	
   402	            $st = $db->prepare("SELECT p.*, cu.login_name AS created_by_name, au.login_name AS approved_by_name,
   403	                                       COALESCE((SELECT SUM(y.amount) FROM subcon_payments y WHERE y.progress_id = p.id AND y.status = 'Posted'), 0) AS paid_amount
   404	                                  FROM subcon_progress p LEFT JOIN public.m_user_system cu ON cu.id = p.created_by LEFT JOIN public.m_user_system au ON au.id = p.approved_by
   405	                                 WHERE p.contract_id = :id ORDER BY p.id");
   406	            $st->execute([':id' => $id]);
   407	            $progress = $st->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
   408	            foreach ($progress as &$p) {
   409	                $p['id'] = (int)$p['id'];
   410	                foreach (['gross_amount', 'ppn_amount', 'pph_amount', 'retention_amount', 'dp_recovery', 'net_payable', 'cum_pct', 'paid_amount'] as $k) $p[$k] = (float)$p[$k];
   411	            }
   412	            unset($p);
   413	
   414	            $st = $db->prepare("SELECT y.*, a.account_code, a.account_name, pr.ref_no AS progress_ref, cu.login_name AS created_by_name, xu.login_name AS cancelled_by_name
   415	                                  FROM subcon_payments y JOIN public.chart_of_accounts a ON a.id = y.account_id
   416	                                  LEFT JOIN subcon_progress pr ON pr.id = y.progress_id
   417	                                  LEFT JOIN public.m_user_system cu ON cu.id = y.created_by LEFT JOIN public.m_user_system xu ON xu.id = y.cancelled_by
   418	                                 WHERE y.contract_id = :id ORDER BY y.id");
   419	            $st->execute([':id' => $id]);
   420	            $payments = $st->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
   421	            foreach ($payments as &$y) { $y['id'] = (int)$y['id']; $y['amount'] = (float)$y['amount']; }

   424	            $st = $db->prepare("SELECT j.ref_no, j.journal_date, j.reference_type::text AS reference_type, j.reference_id
   425	                                  FROM journal_entries j
   426	                                 WHERE (j.reference_type::text = :rp AND j.reference_id IN (SELECT id FROM subcon_progress WHERE contract_id = :c))
   427	                                    OR (j.reference_type::text = :ry AND j.reference_id IN (SELECT id FROM subcon_payments WHERE contract_id = :c))
   428	                                 ORDER BY j.id");
   429	            $st->execute([':rp' => SC_REF_PROGRESS, ':ry' => SC_REF_PAYMENT, ':c' => $id]);

   431	
   432	            $tot = sc_totals($db, $id);
   433	            $tot['billed_pct'] = $c['contract_value'] > 0 ? round($tot['gross'] / $c['contract_value'] * 100, 2) : 0;
   434	            $tot['dp_planned'] = sc_f($c['contract_value'] * $c['dp_pct'] / 100);
   435	
   436	            $allowed = SC_TRANSITIONS[$hdr['status']] ?? [];
   437	            if ($tot['gross'] > 0 || $tot['dp_paid'] > 0) $allowed = array_values(array_diff($allowed, ['Dibatalkan', 'Draft']));
   438	            sc_send(true, ['header' => $hdr, 'tasks' => $tasks, 'progress' => $progress, 'payments' => $payments, 'journals' => $journals,
   439	                           'totals' => $tot, 'allowed_actions' => $allowed]);
