   442	        /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ simpan SPK (baru / ubah Draft) */
   443	        case 'save_contract':
   444	            $id   = (int)($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
   445	            $prj  = (int)($_POST['project_id'] ?? 0);
   446	            $sub  = (int)($_POST['subcontractor_id'] ?? 0);
   447	            $title = trim((string)($_POST['title'] ?? ''));
   448	            $val  = sc_f($_POST['contract_value'] ?? 0);
   449	            $ppn  = sc_pct($_POST['ppn_rate'] ?? 0, 'Tarif PPN');
   450	            $pph  = sc_pct($_POST['pph_rate'] ?? 0, 'Tarif PPh');
   451	            $ret  = sc_pct($_POST['retention_pct'] ?? 0, 'Retensi');
   452	            $dp   = sc_pct($_POST['dp_pct'] ?? 0, 'Uang muka');
   453	            $bid  = (int)($_POST['budget_item_id'] ?? 0) ?: null;
   454	            $sd   = trim((string)($_POST['start_date'] ?? '')); $ed = trim((string)($_POST['end_date'] ?? ''));
   455	            $scope = trim((string)($_POST['scope'] ?? '')) ?: null; $notes = trim((string)($_POST['notes'] ?? '')) ?: null;
   456	            $rowsIn = json_decode($_POST['tasks'] ?? '[]', true);
   457	            $spkId = (int)($_POST['spk_id'] ?? 0) ?: null;      // tautan ke SPK bertipe SUBCON (opsional)
   458	            if ($prj <= 0) throw new ScError('Proyek wajib dipilih.');
   459	            if ($sub <= 0) throw new ScError('Subkontraktor wajib dipilih.');
   460	            if ($title === '') throw new ScError('Judul / lingkup pekerjaan wajib diisi.');
   461	            if ($val <= 0) throw new ScError('Nilai kontrak harus lebih besar dari 0.');
   462	            if ($sd !== '') sc_date($sd, 'Tanggal mulai'); if ($ed !== '') sc_date($ed, 'Tanggal selesai');
   463	            if ($sd !== '' && $ed !== '' && $ed < $sd) throw new ScError('Tanggal selesai tidak boleh lebih awal dari tanggal mulai.');
   464	            if (!is_array($rowsIn)) $rowsIn = [];
   465	
   466	            $db->beginTransaction();
   467	            try {
   468	                $st = $db->prepare("SELECT 1 FROM public.projects WHERE id = :id"); $st->execute([':id' => $prj]);
   469	                if (!$st->fetchColumn()) throw new ScError('Proyek tidak ditemukan.');
   470	                $st = $db->prepare("SELECT 1 FROM subcontractors WHERE id = :id AND active"); $st->execute([':id' => $sub]);
   471	                if (!$st->fetchColumn()) throw new ScError('Subkontraktor tidak ditemukan / tidak aktif.');
   472	
   473	                // tautan ke SPK subkon: tipe SUBCON, proyek & subkontraktor sama, masih aktif, belum dipakai kontrak lain
   474	                if ($spkId) {
   475	                    if (!sc_has_spk_link($db)) throw new ScError('Tautan ke SPK belum aktif: jalankan database/subcontract_management_ddl.sql di database ini.');
   476	                    $st = $db->prepare("SELECT s.spk_no, s.spk_type, s.project_id, s.execution_subcontractor_id, s.status,
   477	                                               (SELECT c.id FROM subcon_contracts c WHERE c.spk_id = s.id AND c.id <> :x LIMIT 1) AS other_contract
   478	                                          FROM spk s WHERE s.id = :i");
   479	                    $st->execute([':i' => $spkId, ':x' => $id]);
   480	                    $sp = $st->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
   481	                    if (!$sp) throw new ScError('SPK yang dipilih tidak ditemukan.');
   482	                    if ($sp['spk_type'] !== 'SUBCON') throw new ScError('Kontrak subkon hanya dapat ditautkan ke SPK bertipe Subkon.');
   483	                    if ((int)$sp['project_id'] !== $prj || (int)$sp['execution_subcontractor_id'] !== $sub) {
   484	                        throw new ScError("SPK {$sp['spk_no']} dibuat untuk proyek / subkontraktor yang berbeda dari kontrak ini.");
   485	                    }
   486	                    if (!in_array($sp['status'], ['Draft', 'Diterbitkan'], true)) throw new ScError("SPK {$sp['spk_no']} berstatus {$sp['status']}, tidak dapat ditautkan.");
   487	                    if ($sp['other_contract']) throw new ScError("SPK {$sp['spk_no']} sudah terhubung ke kontrak subkon lain.");
   488	                }
   489	
   490	                // pembagian ke task proyek (kosong = seluruh pekerjaan satu baris)
   491	                $clean = []; $seen = []; $sum = 0.0;
   492	                if (!count($rowsIn)) $rowsIn = [['task_id' => 0, 'task_name' => $title, 'task_value' => $val]];
   493	                $stT = $db->prepare("SELECT task_name FROM public.project_tasks WHERE id = :t AND project_id = :p");
   494	                foreach ($rowsIn as $n => $r) {
   495	                    $tid = (int)($r['task_id'] ?? 0) ?: null;
   496	                    $tv  = sc_f($r['task_value'] ?? 0);
   497	                    $no  = 'Baris task ' . ($n + 1);
   498	                    if ($tv <= 0) throw new ScError("{$no}: nilai harus lebih besar dari 0.");
   499	                    if ($tid) {
   500	                        if (isset($seen[$tid])) throw new ScError("{$no}: task yang sama dipilih dua kali.");
   501	                        $seen[$tid] = true;
   502	                        $stT->execute([':t' => $tid, ':p' => $prj]);
   503	                        $tn = $stT->fetchColumn();
   504	                        if ($tn === false) throw new ScError("{$no}: task bukan milik proyek ini.");
   505	                    } else {
   506	                        $tn = trim((string)($r['task_name'] ?? '')) ?: $title;
   507	                    }
   508	                    $sum += $tv;
   509	                    $clean[] = ['tid' => $tid, 'name' => $tn, 'val' => $tv, 'notes' => trim((string)($r['notes'] ?? '')) ?: null];
   510	                }
   511	                if (abs($sum - $val) > SC_EPS) {
   512	                    throw new ScError('Total pembagian task (' . number_format($sum, 2) . ') harus sama dengan nilai kontrak (' . number_format($val, 2) . ').');
   513	                }
   514	
   515	                // batas anggaran RAB (bila SPK diikat ke baris RAB)
   516	                if ($bid) {
   517	                    $st = $db->prepare("SELECT project_id, COALESCE(qty_plan,0) * COALESCE(unit_price_plan,0) AS bv, item_name FROM public.budget_items WHERE id = :id");
   518	                    $st->execute([':id' => $bid]);
   519	                    $bi = $st->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
   520	                    if (!$bi || (int)$bi['project_id'] !== $prj) throw new ScError('Baris RAB tidak ditemukan pada proyek ini.');
   521	                    $st = $db->prepare("SELECT COALESCE(SUM(contract_value),0) FROM subcon_contracts WHERE budget_item_id = :b AND status <> 'Dibatalkan' AND id <> :x");
   522	                    $st->execute([':b' => $bid, ':x' => $id]);
   523	                    $committed = (float)$st->fetchColumn();
   524	                    if ($committed + $val > (float)$bi['bv'] + SC_EPS) {
   525	                        throw new ScError('Nilai SPK melebihi sisa anggaran RAB "' . $bi['item_name'] . '" (anggaran ' . number_format((float)$bi['bv'], 2) .
   526	                                          ', sudah dikomitmen SPK lain ' . number_format($committed, 2) . ').');
   527	                    }
   528	                }
   529	
   530	                $v = [':p' => $prj, ':s' => $sub, ':t' => $title, ':sc' => $scope, ':b' => $bid, ':val' => $val, ':ppn' => $ppn, ':pph' => $pph,
   531	                      ':ret' => $ret, ':dp' => $dp, ':sd' => $sd !== '' ? $sd : null, ':ed' => $ed !== '' ? $ed : null, ':n' => $notes];
   532	                if ($id > 0) {
   533	                    $c0 = sc_contract($db, $id, true);
   534	                    if ($c0['status'] !== 'Draft') throw new ScError('Hanya SPK berstatus Draft yang dapat diubah.');
   535	                    $v[':id'] = $id;
   536	                    $db->prepare("UPDATE subcon_contracts SET project_id=:p, subcontractor_id=:s, title=:t, scope=:sc, budget_item_id=:b, contract_value=:val,
   537	                                         ppn_rate=:ppn, pph_rate=:pph, retention_pct=:ret, dp_pct=:dp, start_date=CAST(:sd AS date), end_date=CAST(:ed AS date),
   538	                                         notes=:n, updated_at=NOW() WHERE id=:id")->execute($v);
   539	                    $db->prepare("DELETE FROM subcon_contract_tasks WHERE contract_id = :id")->execute([':id' => $id]);
   540	                    $spk = $c0['spk_no'];
   541	                } else {
   542	                    if (!function_exists('CreateRefNo')) throw new Exception('CreateRefNo() tidak tersedia.');
   543	                    $spk = CreateRefNo('subcon_contracts', 'spk_no', 'SPK');
   544	                    $v[':no'] = $spk; $v[':cb'] = sc_uid();
   545	                    $st = $db->prepare("INSERT INTO subcon_contracts (spk_no, project_id, subcontractor_id, title, scope, budget_item_id, contract_value, ppn_rate, pph_rate,
   546	                                               retention_pct, dp_pct, start_date, end_date, notes, status, created_by)
   547	                                        VALUES (:no,:p,:s,:t,:sc,:b,:val,:ppn,:pph,:ret,:dp,CAST(:sd AS date),CAST(:ed AS date),:n,'Draft',:cb) RETURNING id");
   548	                    $st->execute($v);
   549	                    $id = (int)$st->fetchColumn();
   550	                }
   551	                if (sc_has_spk_link($db)) {
   552	                    $db->prepare("UPDATE subcon_contracts SET spk_id = CAST(:spk AS integer) WHERE id = :id")->execute([':spk' => $spkId, ':id' => $id]);
   553	                }
   554	                $stI = $db->prepare("INSERT INTO subcon_contract_tasks (contract_id, task_id, task_name, task_value, notes) VALUES (:c,:t,:n,:v,:no)");
   555	                foreach ($clean as $r) $stI->execute([':c' => $id, ':t' => $r['tid'], ':n' => $r['name'], ':v' => $r['val'], ':no' => $r['notes']]);
   556	                $db->commit();
   557	                sc_send(true, ['id' => $id, 'spk_no' => $spk], "SPK {$spk} berhasil disimpan.");
   558	            } catch (Throwable $e) { if ($db->inTransaction()) $db->rollBack(); throw $e; }
   559	            break;
   560	
   561	        /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ ubah status SPK */
   562	        case 'change_status':
   563	            $id = (int)($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
   564	            $to = (string)($_POST['to'] ?? '');
   565	            $reason = trim((string)($_POST['reason'] ?? ''));
   566	            $db->beginTransaction();
   567	            try {
   568	                $c = sc_contract($db, $id, true);
   569	                if (!in_array($to, SC_TRANSITIONS[$c['status']] ?? [], true)) throw new ScError("Status {$c['status']} tidak dapat diubah ke {$to}.");
   570	                $tot = sc_totals($db, $id);
   571	                $st = $db->prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM subcon_progress WHERE contract_id = :c AND status <> 'Dibatalkan'");
   572	                $st->execute([':c' => $id]);
   573	                $nProgress = (int)$st->fetchColumn();
   574	                $st = $db->prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM subcon_payments WHERE contract_id = :c AND status = 'Posted'");
   575	                $st->execute([':c' => $id]);
   576	                $nPay = (int)$st->fetchColumn();
   577	
   578	                if (($to === 'Dibatalkan' || $to === 'Draft') && ($nProgress > 0 || $nPay > 0)) {
   579	                    throw new ScError('SPK ini sudah punya prestasi / pembayaran, tidak dapat dibatalkan / dikembalikan ke Draft. Batalkan dokumen turunannya dulu.');
   580	                }
   581	                $set = "status = :s, updated_at = NOW()"; $p = [':s' => $to, ':id' => $id];
   582	                if ($to === 'Dibatalkan') {
   583	                    if (strlen($reason) < 5) throw new ScError('Alasan pembatalan wajib diisi (minimal 5 karakter).');
   584	                    $set .= ", cancelled_by = :u, cancelled_at = NOW(), cancel_reason = :r"; $p[':u'] = sc_uid(); $p[':r'] = $reason;
   585	                    // lepas tautan SPK (indeks unik uq_subcon_contract_spk) agar SPK itu bisa dipakai kontrak lain; jejaknya masuk catatan
   586	                    if (!empty($c['spk_id'])) {
   587	                        $st = $db->prepare("SELECT spk_no FROM spk WHERE id = :i"); $st->execute([':i' => (int)$c['spk_id']]);
   588	                        $oldSpk = (string)$st->fetchColumn();
   589	                        $set .= ", spk_id = NULL, notes = TRIM(BOTH E'\\n' FROM COALESCE(notes, '') || E'\\n' || :ln)";
   590	                        $p[':ln'] = 'Tautan ke SPK ' . $oldSpk . ' dilepas saat kontrak dibatalkan.';
   591	                    }
   592	                } elseif ($to === 'Disetujui') {
   593	                    if (SC_REQUIRE_OTHER_APPROVER && (int)$c['created_by'] === (int)sc_uid()) throw new ScError('Penyetuju harus user lain selain pembuat SPK.');
   594	                    $set .= ", approved_by = :u, approved_at = NOW()"; $p[':u'] = sc_uid();
   595	                } elseif ($to === 'Selesai') {
   596	                    $st = $db->prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM subcon_progress WHERE contract_id = :c AND status = 'Draft'");
   597	                    $st->execute([':c' => $id]);
   598	                    if ((int)$st->fetchColumn() > 0) throw new ScError('Masih ada prestasi berstatus Draft. Setujui atau batalkan dulu.');
   599	                    if ($tot['gross'] < $c['contract_value'] - SC_EPS) throw new ScError('Progres SPK belum 100% (tertagih ' . number_format($tot['gross'], 2) . ' dari ' . number_format($c['contract_value'], 2) . ').');
   600	                    if ($tot['dp_balance'] > SC_EPS) throw new ScError('Uang muka subkon belum seluruhnya dipotong (sisa ' . number_format($tot['dp_balance'], 2) . ').');
   601	                    $set .= ", closed_by = :u, closed_at = NOW()"; $p[':u'] = sc_uid();
   602	                } elseif ($to === 'Diajukan') {
   603	                    $st = $db->prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM subcon_contract_tasks WHERE contract_id = :c");
   604	                    $st->execute([':c' => $id]);
   605	                    if ((int)$st->fetchColumn() === 0) throw new ScError('SPK belum memiliki pembagian task.');
   606	                }
   607	                $db->prepare("UPDATE subcon_contracts SET {$set} WHERE id = :id")->execute($p);
   608	                $db->commit();
   609	                sc_send(true, [], "SPK {$c['spk_no']} sekarang berstatus {$to}.");
   610	            } catch (Throwable $e) { if ($db->inTransaction()) $db->rollBack(); throw $e; }
   611	            break;
   612	
   613	        case 'delete_contract':
   614	            $id = (int)($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
   615	            $db->beginTransaction();
   616	            try {
   617	                $c = sc_contract($db, $id, true);
   618	                if ($c['status'] !== 'Draft') throw new ScError('Hanya SPK berstatus Draft yang dapat dihapus. Gunakan Batalkan untuk SPK lain.');
   619	                $st = $db->prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM subcon_progress WHERE contract_id = :c"); $st->execute([':c' => $id]);
   620	                if ((int)$st->fetchColumn() > 0) throw new ScError('SPK sudah memiliki prestasi, tidak dapat dihapus.');
   621	                $db->prepare("DELETE FROM subcon_contract_tasks WHERE contract_id = :id")->execute([':id' => $id]);
   622	                $db->prepare("DELETE FROM subcon_contracts WHERE id = :id")->execute([':id' => $id]);
   623	                $db->commit();
   624	                sc_send(true, [], "SPK {$c['spk_no']} dihapus.");
   625	            } catch (Throwable $e) { if ($db->inTransaction()) $db->rollBack(); throw $e; }
   626	            break;
   627	
   628	        /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ prestasi */
   629	        // Data awal dialog prestasi: baris task + progres sebelumnya + parameter potongan
   630	        case 'progress_prepare':

===== D. SUBCON: PROGRESS DAN PAYMENT =====
   630	        case 'progress_prepare':
   631	            $cid = (int)($_REQUEST['contract_id'] ?? 0);
   632	            $pid = (int)($_REQUEST['progress_id'] ?? 0);
   633	            $c = sc_contract($db, $cid);
   634	            $prev = sc_prev_map($db, $cid, $pid);
   635	            $st = $db->prepare("SELECT id, task_name, task_value FROM subcon_contract_tasks WHERE contract_id = :c ORDER BY id");
   636	            $st->execute([':c' => $cid]);
   637	            $rows = $st->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
   638	            $cur = [];
   639	            if ($pid > 0) {
   640	                $st = $db->prepare("SELECT contract_task_id, cum_pct FROM subcon_progress_dtl WHERE progress_id = :p");
   641	                $st->execute([':p' => $pid]);
   642	                foreach ($st->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC) as $r) $cur[(int)$r['contract_task_id']] = (float)$r['cum_pct'];
   643	            }
   644	            foreach ($rows as &$r) {
   645	                $r['id'] = (int)$r['id']; $r['task_value'] = (float)$r['task_value'];
   646	                $r['prev_pct'] = isset($prev[$r['id']]) ? $prev[$r['id']] : 0.0;
   647	                $r['cum_pct'] = isset($cur[$r['id']]) ? $cur[$r['id']] : $r['prev_pct'];
   648	            }
   649	            unset($r);
   650	            $tot = sc_totals($db, $cid);
   651	            sc_send(true, $rows, '', ['params' => ['contract_value' => $c['contract_value'], 'ppn_rate' => $c['ppn_rate'], 'pph_rate' => $c['pph_rate'],
   652	                       'retention_pct' => $c['retention_pct'], 'dp_pct' => $c['dp_pct'], 'dp_balance' => $tot['dp_balance'], 'status' => $c['status']]]);
   653	            break;
   654	
   655	        case 'save_progress':
   656	            $id   = (int)($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
   657	            $cid  = (int)($_POST['contract_id'] ?? 0);
   658	            $date = sc_date((string)($_POST['progress_date'] ?? ''), 'Tanggal prestasi');
   659	            $evid = trim((string)($_POST['evidence_ref'] ?? '')) ?: null;
   660	            $remark = trim((string)($_POST['remark'] ?? '')) ?: null;
   661	            $rowsIn = json_decode($_POST['details'] ?? '[]', true);
   662	            if (!is_array($rowsIn) || !count($rowsIn)) throw new ScError('Isi progres minimal satu task.');
   663	            $db->beginTransaction();
   664	            try {
   665	                $c = sc_contract($db, $cid, true);
   666	                if ($c['status'] !== 'Disetujui') throw new ScError('Prestasi hanya dapat dibuat untuk SPK berstatus Disetujui.');
   667	                $st = $db->prepare("SELECT id FROM subcon_progress WHERE contract_id = :c AND status = 'Draft' AND id <> :x LIMIT 1");
   668	                $st->execute([':c' => $cid, ':x' => $id]);
   669	                if ($st->fetchColumn()) throw new ScError('Masih ada prestasi Draft pada SPK ini. Setujui atau batalkan dulu.');
   670	                if ($id > 0) {
   671	                    $st = $db->prepare("SELECT status, ref_no, contract_id FROM subcon_progress WHERE id = :id FOR UPDATE");
   672	                    $st->execute([':id' => $id]);
   673	                    $p0 = $st->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
   674	                    if (!$p0 || (int)$p0['contract_id'] !== $cid) throw new ScError('Prestasi tidak ditemukan.');
   675	                    if ($p0['status'] !== 'Draft') throw new ScError('Hanya prestasi berstatus Draft yang dapat diubah.');
   676	                }
   677	                $prev = sc_prev_map($db, $cid, $id);
   678	                $st = $db->prepare("SELECT id, task_value FROM subcon_contract_tasks WHERE contract_id = :c");
   679	                $st->execute([':c' => $cid]);
   680	                $taskVal = []; foreach ($st->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC) as $t) $taskVal[(int)$t['id']] = (float)$t['task_value'];
   681	
   682	                $dtl = []; $gross = 0.0; $weighted = 0.0; $changed = false;
   683	                foreach ($rowsIn as $r) {
   684	                    $tid = (int)($r['contract_task_id'] ?? 0);
   685	                    if (!isset($taskVal[$tid])) throw new ScError('Task prestasi tidak termasuk dalam SPK ini.');
   686	                    $cum = round((float)($r['cum_pct'] ?? 0), 3);
   687	                    $pv  = isset($prev[$tid]) ? $prev[$tid] : 0.0;
   688	                    if ($cum > 100 + 0.0005) throw new ScError('Progres kumulatif tidak boleh lebih dari 100%.');
   689	                    if ($cum < $pv - 0.0005) throw new ScError('Progres kumulatif tidak boleh lebih kecil dari progres sebelumnya (' . $pv . '%).');
   690	                    $amt = round($taskVal[$tid] * ($cum - $pv) / 100, 2);
   691	                    if ($cum > $pv + 0.0005) $changed = true;
   692	                    $gross += $amt;
   693	                    $dtl[$tid] = ['prev' => $pv, 'cum' => $cum, 'amt' => $amt];
   694	                }
   695	                // task yang tidak dikirim tetap pada progres sebelumnya
   696	                foreach ($taskVal as $tid => $tv) {
   697	                    if (!isset($dtl[$tid])) { $pv = isset($prev[$tid]) ? $prev[$tid] : 0.0; $dtl[$tid] = ['prev' => $pv, 'cum' => $pv, 'amt' => 0.0]; }
   698	                    $weighted += $tv * $dtl[$tid]['cum'] / 100;
   699	                }
   700	                if (!$changed || $gross <= 0) throw new ScError('Tidak ada kenaikan progres pada prestasi ini.');
   701	                $gross = sc_f($gross);
   702	                $cumPct = $c['contract_value'] > 0 ? round($weighted / $c['contract_value'] * 100, 3) : 0;
   703	                $tot = sc_totals($db, $cid);
   704	                $calc = sc_calc($c, $gross, $cumPct, $tot['dp_balance']);
   705	
   706	                if ($id > 0) {
   707	                    $db->prepare("UPDATE subcon_progress SET progress_date = CAST(:d AS date), gross_amount=:g, ppn_amount=:ppn, pph_amount=:pph, retention_amount=:ret,
   708	                                         dp_recovery=:dp, net_payable=:net, cum_pct=:cp, evidence_ref=:e, remark=:r, updated_at=NOW() WHERE id=:id")
   709	                       ->execute([':d' => $date, ':g' => $gross, ':ppn' => $calc['ppn'], ':pph' => $calc['pph'], ':ret' => $calc['retention'], ':dp' => $calc['dp_recovery'],
   710	                                  ':net' => $calc['net'], ':cp' => $cumPct, ':e' => $evid, ':r' => $remark, ':id' => $id]);
   711	                    $db->prepare("DELETE FROM subcon_progress_dtl WHERE progress_id = :id")->execute([':id' => $id]);
   712	                    $ref = $p0['ref_no'];
   713	                } else {
   714	                    $ref = CreateRefNo('subcon_progress', 'ref_no', 'SKP');
   715	                    $st = $db->prepare("INSERT INTO subcon_progress (ref_no, contract_id, progress_date, status, gross_amount, ppn_amount, pph_amount, retention_amount,
   716	                                               dp_recovery, net_payable, cum_pct, evidence_ref, remark, created_by)
   717	                                        VALUES (:no,:c,CAST(:d AS date),'Draft',:g,:ppn,:pph,:ret,:dp,:net,:cp,:e,:r,:u) RETURNING id");
   718	                    $st->execute([':no' => $ref, ':c' => $cid, ':d' => $date, ':g' => $gross, ':ppn' => $calc['ppn'], ':pph' => $calc['pph'], ':ret' => $calc['retention'],
   719	                                  ':dp' => $calc['dp_recovery'], ':net' => $calc['net'], ':cp' => $cumPct, ':e' => $evid, ':r' => $remark, ':u' => sc_uid()]);
   720	                    $id = (int)$st->fetchColumn();
   721	                }
   722	                $stI = $db->prepare("INSERT INTO subcon_progress_dtl (progress_id, contract_task_id, prev_pct, cum_pct, amount) VALUES (:p,:t,:pv,:c,:a)");
   723	                foreach ($dtl as $tid => $d) $stI->execute([':p' => $id, ':t' => $tid, ':pv' => $d['prev'], ':c' => $d['cum'], ':a' => $d['amt']]);
   724	                $db->commit();
   725	                sc_send(true, ['id' => $id, 'ref_no' => $ref, 'calc' => $calc, 'gross' => $gross], "Prestasi {$ref} tersimpan (Draft).");
   726	            } catch (Throwable $e) { if ($db->inTransaction()) $db->rollBack(); throw $e; }
   727	            break;
   728	
   729	        case 'approve_progress':
   730	            $id = (int)($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
   731	            $db->beginTransaction();
   732	            try {
   733	                sc_assert_ref_types($db);
   734	                $st = $db->prepare("SELECT * FROM subcon_progress WHERE id = :id FOR UPDATE");
   735	                $st->execute([':id' => $id]);
   736	                $p = $st->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
   737	                if (!$p) throw new ScError('Prestasi tidak ditemukan.');
   738	                if ($p['status'] !== 'Draft') throw new ScError('Hanya prestasi berstatus Draft yang dapat disetujui.');
   739	                $c = sc_contract($db, (int)$p['contract_id'], true);
   740	                if ($c['status'] !== 'Disetujui') throw new ScError('SPK tidak berstatus Disetujui.');
   741	                if (SC_REQUIRE_OTHER_APPROVER && (int)$p['created_by'] === (int)sc_uid()) throw new ScError('Penyetuju harus user lain selain pembuat prestasi.');
   742	
   743	                // progres sebelumnya harus masih sama dengan saat prestasi dibuat; hitung ulang potongan dengan sisa uang muka terkini
   744	                $prev = sc_prev_map($db, (int)$c['id'], $id);
   745	                $st = $db->prepare("SELECT d.contract_task_id, d.prev_pct, d.cum_pct, d.amount, t.task_name FROM subcon_progress_dtl d
   746	                                      JOIN subcon_contract_tasks t ON t.id = d.contract_task_id WHERE d.progress_id = :p");
   747	                $st->execute([':p' => $id]);
   748	                foreach ($st->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC) as $d) {
   749	                    $pv = isset($prev[(int)$d['contract_task_id']]) ? $prev[(int)$d['contract_task_id']] : 0.0;
   750	                    if (abs($pv - (float)$d['prev_pct']) > 0.0005) throw new ScError('Progres sebelumnya untuk "' . $d['task_name'] . '" berubah sejak prestasi dibuat. Edit ulang prestasi ini.');
   751	                }
   752	                $tot = sc_totals($db, (int)$c['id']);
   753	                $calc = sc_calc($c, (float)$p['gross_amount'], (float)$p['cum_pct'], $tot['dp_balance']);
   754	                $db->prepare("UPDATE subcon_progress SET ppn_amount=:ppn, pph_amount=:pph, retention_amount=:ret, dp_recovery=:dp, net_payable=:net,
   755	                                     status='Disetujui', approved_by=:u, approved_at=NOW() WHERE id=:id")
   756	                   ->execute([':ppn' => $calc['ppn'], ':pph' => $calc['pph'], ':ret' => $calc['retention'], ':dp' => $calc['dp_recovery'], ':net' => $calc['net'], ':u' => sc_uid(), ':id' => $id]);
   757	
   758	                $date = $p['progress_date']; $d0 = " {$p['ref_no']} - {$c['spk_no']}";
   759	                sc_post($db, 'SUBCON_WORK',        SC_REF_PROGRESS, $id, $date, (float)$p['gross_amount'], 'Prestasi subkon' . $d0);
   760	                sc_post($db, 'SUBCON_PPN',         SC_REF_PROGRESS, $id, $date, $calc['ppn'],         'PPN Masukan prestasi' . $d0);
   761	                sc_post($db, 'SUBCON_PPH',         SC_REF_PROGRESS, $id, $date, $calc['pph'],         'PPh final dipotong' . $d0);
   762	                sc_post($db, 'SUBCON_RETENTION',   SC_REF_PROGRESS, $id, $date, $calc['retention'],   'Retensi ditahan' . $d0);
   763	                sc_post($db, 'SUBCON_DP_RECOVERY', SC_REF_PROGRESS, $id, $date, $calc['dp_recovery'], 'Potongan uang muka' . $d0);
   764	                $db->commit();
   765	                sc_send(true, ['calc' => $calc], "Prestasi {$p['ref_no']} disetujui & dijurnal. Nilai bayar " . number_format($calc['net'], 2) . '.');
   766	            } catch (Throwable $e) { if ($db->inTransaction()) $db->rollBack(); throw $e; }
   767	            break;
   768	
   769	        case 'cancel_progress':
   770	            $id = (int)($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
   771	            $reason = trim((string)($_POST['reason'] ?? ''));
   772	            if (strlen($reason) < 5) throw new ScError('Alasan pembatalan wajib diisi (minimal 5 karakter).');
   773	            $db->beginTransaction();
   774	            try {
   775	                $st = $db->prepare("SELECT * FROM subcon_progress WHERE id = :id FOR UPDATE");
   776	                $st->execute([':id' => $id]);
   777	                $p = $st->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
   778	                if (!$p) throw new ScError('Prestasi tidak ditemukan.');
   779	                if ($p['status'] === 'Dibatalkan') throw new ScError('Prestasi sudah dibatalkan.');
   780	                $c = sc_contract($db, (int)$p['contract_id'], true);
   781	                if ($p['status'] === 'Disetujui') {
   782	                    if ($c['status'] === 'Selesai') throw new ScError('SPK sudah Selesai, prestasi tidak dapat dibatalkan.');
   783	                    $st = $db->prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM subcon_payments WHERE progress_id = :p AND status = 'Posted'");
   784	                    $st->execute([':p' => $id]);
   785	                    if ((int)$st->fetchColumn() > 0) throw new ScError('Prestasi sudah ada pembayaran. Batalkan pembayarannya dulu.');
   786	                    $st = $db->prepare("SELECT MAX(id) FROM subcon_progress WHERE contract_id = :c AND status = 'Disetujui'");
   787	                    $st->execute([':c' => (int)$c['id']]);
   788	                    if ((int)$st->fetchColumn() !== $id) throw new ScError('Hanya prestasi disetujui yang TERAKHIR yang dapat dibatalkan (progres periode berikutnya bergantung padanya).');
   789	                    if (sc_has_journal($db, SC_REF_PROGRESS, $id)) ReverseJournalByReference($db, SC_REF_PROGRESS, $id);
   790	                }
   791	                $db->prepare("UPDATE subcon_progress SET status='Dibatalkan', cancelled_by=:u, cancelled_at=NOW(), cancel_reason=:r WHERE id=:id")
   792	                   ->execute([':u' => sc_uid(), ':r' => $reason, ':id' => $id]);
   793	                $db->commit();
   794	                sc_send(true, [], "Prestasi {$p['ref_no']} dibatalkan.");
   795	            } catch (Throwable $e) { if ($db->inTransaction()) $db->rollBack(); throw $e; }
   796	            break;
   797	
   798	        /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ pembayaran */
   799	        case 'save_payment':
   800	            $cid  = (int)($_POST['contract_id'] ?? 0);
   801	            $type = (string)($_POST['pay_type'] ?? '');
   802	            $pid  = (int)($_POST['progress_id'] ?? 0);
   803	            $date = sc_date((string)($_POST['payment_date'] ?? ''), 'Tanggal bayar');
   804	            $acc  = (int)($_POST['account_id'] ?? 0);
   805	            $amt  = sc_f($_POST['amount'] ?? 0);
   806	            $ref  = trim((string)($_POST['reference_no'] ?? '')) ?: null;
   807	            $notes = trim((string)($_POST['notes'] ?? '')) ?: null;
   808	            if (!in_array($type, ['DP', 'PROGRESS', 'RETENSI'], true)) throw new ScError('Jenis pembayaran tidak valid.');
   809	            if ($amt <= 0) throw new ScError('Jumlah bayar harus lebih besar dari 0.');
   810	            $db->beginTransaction();
   811	            try {
   812	                sc_assert_ref_types($db);
   813	                $c = sc_contract($db, $cid, true);
   814	                $st = $db->prepare("SELECT account_code FROM public.chart_of_accounts WHERE id = :a AND is_active AND account_type::text IN ('cash','bank')");
   815	                $st->execute([':a' => $acc]);
   816	                if ($st->fetchColumn() === false) throw new ScError('Akun kas / bank sumber pembayaran wajib dipilih.');
   817	                $tot = sc_totals($db, $cid);
   818	
   819	                if ($type === 'DP') {
   820	                    if ($c['status'] !== 'Disetujui') throw new ScError('Uang muka hanya untuk SPK berstatus Disetujui.');
   821	                    $plan = sc_f($c['contract_value'] * $c['dp_pct'] / 100);
   822	                    if ($plan <= 0) throw new ScError('SPK ini tidak memiliki uang muka (dp% = 0).');
   823	                    if ($tot['dp_paid'] + $amt > $plan + SC_EPS) throw new ScError('Uang muka melebihi ketentuan SPK (' . number_format($plan, 2) . ', sudah dibayar ' . number_format($tot['dp_paid'], 2) . ').');
   824	                    $pid = 0; $rule = 'SUBCON_PAY_DP';
   825	                } elseif ($type === 'PROGRESS') {
   826	                    if (!in_array($c['status'], ['Disetujui', 'Selesai'], true)) throw new ScError('SPK tidak dalam status yang dapat dibayar.');
   827	                    $st = $db->prepare("SELECT net_payable, status, contract_id, ref_no FROM subcon_progress WHERE id = :p");
   828	                    $st->execute([':p' => $pid]);
   829	                    $pr = $st->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
   830	                    if (!$pr || (int)$pr['contract_id'] !== $cid || $pr['status'] !== 'Disetujui') throw new ScError('Pilih prestasi yang sudah disetujui pada SPK ini.');
   831	                    $st = $db->prepare("SELECT COALESCE(SUM(amount),0) FROM subcon_payments WHERE progress_id = :p AND status = 'Posted'");
   832	                    $st->execute([':p' => $pid]);
   833	                    $out = sc_f((float)$pr['net_payable'] - (float)$st->fetchColumn());
   834	                    if ($amt > $out + SC_EPS) throw new ScError('Pembayaran melebihi sisa hutang prestasi ' . $pr['ref_no'] . ' (' . number_format($out, 2) . ').');
   835	                    $rule = 'SUBCON_PAY';
   836	                } else {
   837	                    if ($c['status'] !== 'Selesai') throw new ScError('Retensi hanya dapat dilepas setelah SPK berstatus Selesai.');
   838	                    if ($amt > $tot['retention_balance'] + SC_EPS) throw new ScError('Pelepasan retensi melebihi retensi yang ditahan (' . number_format($tot['retention_balance'], 2) . ').');
   839	                    $pid = 0; $rule = 'SUBCON_PAY_RET';
   840	                }
   841	                $payNo = CreateRefNo('subcon_payments', 'payment_no', 'SPY');
   842	                $st = $db->prepare("INSERT INTO subcon_payments (payment_no, contract_id, pay_type, progress_id, payment_date, account_id, amount, reference_no, notes, status, created_by)
   843	                                    VALUES (:no,:c,:t,:p,CAST(:d AS date),:a,:amt,:r,:n,'Posted',:u) RETURNING id");
   844	                $st->execute([':no' => $payNo, ':c' => $cid, ':t' => $type, ':p' => $pid ?: null, ':d' => $date, ':a' => $acc, ':amt' => $amt, ':r' => $ref, ':n' => $notes, ':u' => sc_uid()]);
   845	                $payId = (int)$st->fetchColumn();
   846	                sc_post($db, $rule, SC_REF_PAYMENT, $payId, $date, $amt, "Pembayaran subkon {$payNo} - {$c['spk_no']}", $acc);
   847	                $db->commit();
   848	                sc_send(true, ['id' => $payId, 'payment_no' => $payNo], "Pembayaran {$payNo} tersimpan & dijurnal.");
   849	            } catch (Throwable $e) { if ($db->inTransaction()) $db->rollBack(); throw $e; }
   850	            break;
   851	
   852	        case 'cancel_payment':
   853	            $id = (int)($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
   854	            $reason = trim((string)($_POST['reason'] ?? ''));
   855	            if (strlen($reason) < 5) throw new ScError('Alasan pembatalan wajib diisi (minimal 5 karakter).');
   856	            $db->beginTransaction();
   857	            try {
   858	                $st = $db->prepare("SELECT * FROM subcon_payments WHERE id = :id FOR UPDATE");
   859	                $st->execute([':id' => $id]);
   860	                $y = $st->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
   861	                if (!$y) throw new ScError('Pembayaran tidak ditemukan.');
   862	                if ($y['status'] !== 'Posted') throw new ScError('Pembayaran sudah dibatalkan.');
   863	                $c = sc_contract($db, (int)$y['contract_id'], true);
   864	                $tot = sc_totals($db, (int)$c['id']);
   865	                if ($y['pay_type'] === 'DP' && ($tot['dp_paid'] - (float)$y['amount']) < $tot['dp_recovered'] - SC_EPS) {
   866	                    throw new ScError('Uang muka ini sudah dipotong dari prestasi. Batalkan prestasi terkait dulu.');
   867	                }
   868	                if ($y['pay_type'] === 'RETENSI' && $c['status'] === 'Selesai') { /* boleh: retensi kembali ditahan */ }
   869	                if (sc_has_journal($db, SC_REF_PAYMENT, $id)) ReverseJournalByReference($db, SC_REF_PAYMENT, $id);
   870	                $db->prepare("UPDATE subcon_payments SET status='Dibatalkan', cancelled_by=:u, cancelled_at=NOW(), cancel_reason=:r WHERE id=:id")
   871	                   ->execute([':u' => sc_uid(), ':r' => $reason, ':id' => $id]);
   872	                $db->commit();
   873	                sc_send(true, [], "Pembayaran {$y['payment_no']} dibatalkan.");
   874	            } catch (Throwable $e) { if ($db->inTransaction()) $db->rollBack(); throw $e; }
   875	            break;
   876	
   877	        default:
   878	            sc_send(false, [], 'Aksi/Task tidak dikenal.');
   879	            break;
   880	    }

   893	            $result = [];
   894	            if ($hasAnalysis && !empty($row['analysis'])) {
   895	                $decoded = json_decode((string)$row['analysis'], true);
   896	                if (is_array($decoded)) $result = $decoded;
   897	            }
   898	            if (!$result) {
   899	                $projectStmt = $db->prepare("SELECT name FROM projects WHERE id = :pid");
   900	                $projectStmt->execute([':pid' => $pid]);
   901	                $projectName = (string)($projectStmt->fetchColumn() ?: 'Proyek');
   902	                $actions = json_decode((string)($row['recommendations'] ?? '[]'), true);
   903	                if (!is_array($actions)) $actions = [];
   904	                $result = [
   905	                    'project_id' => $pid, 'project_name' => $projectName,
   906	                    'risk' => strtoupper((string)($row['risk_level'] ?? 'LOW')),
   907	                    'headline' => (string)($row['ai_summary'] ?? 'Hasil analisis tersimpan'),
   908	                    'summary' => (string)($row['ai_summary'] ?? ''),
   909	                    'actions' => $actions, 'questions' => [], 'findings' => [],
   910	                    'ai_used' => false, 'ai_note' => 'Menampilkan ringkasan tersimpan dari format log lama.',
   911	                ];
   912	            }
   913	            $result['log_id'] = (int)$row['id'];
   914	            $result['generated_at'] = $row['created_at'];
   915	            $result['cached'] = true;
   916	            $result['can_refresh'] = ($aiAccessLevel >= 3);
   917	            out(['success' => true, 'data' => $result]);
   918	        }
   919	
   920	        $result = dash_run_analysis($db, $pid, true, 'dashboard', (int)($_SESSION['user_id'] ?? 0) ?: null);
   921	        $result['can_refresh'] = true;
   922	        out(['success' => true, 'data' => $result]);
   923	    } catch (Exception $e) {
   924	        error_log('[dashboardCRD] project_ai: ' . $e->getMessage());
   925	        out(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Analisis proyek gagal: ' . $e->getMessage()]);
   926	    }
   927	    break;
   928	
   929	// ============================================================
   930	default:
   931	    out(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Aksi tidak ditemukan']);
   932	}

===== 3. EXPENSE: SELURUH FILE =====
     1	<?php
     2	/*
     3	Document      : m.expensesCRD.php
     4	Description   : Backend CRUD Master Expense (sama pola dengan m.materialsCRD.php)
     5	                - list: grid utama dengan paging & search
     6	                - read_edit: load data untuk form edit
     7	                - save: create / update
     8	                - delete: soft-delete (set active=false)
     9	Backend class : crud (PDO wrapper)
    10	*/
    11	// Gunakan pemeriksaan login dan hak akses menu yang sama dengan modul lain.
    12	require_once __DIR__ . '/../php-func/user.valid.php';
    13	require_once __DIR__ . '/../php-class/crudClass.php';
    14	
    15	if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_NONE) {
    16	    session_start();
    17	}
    18	header('Content-Type: application/json');
    19	
    20	$access = (int)($_SESSION['FormAccess'] ?? 0);
    21	
    22	function exp_require_access($minimum, $message) {
    23	    $current = (int)($_SESSION['FormAccess'] ?? 0);
    24	    if ($current < $minimum) {
    25	        exp_send(false, [], $message);
    26	    }
    27	}
    28	
    29	$task = $_REQUEST['task'] ?? '';
    30	$db = new crud();
    31	
    32	function exp_send($success, $data = [], $message = '', $extra = []) {
    33	    $response = array_merge(['success' => $success, 'message' => $message], $extra);
    34	    if ($success) {
    35	        if (is_array($data) && isset($data['data'])) {
    36	            $response['data'] = $data['data'];
    37	            if (isset($data['totalCount'])) $response['totalCount'] = $data['totalCount'];
    38	        } else {
    39	            $response['data'] = $data;
    40	        }
    41	    }
    42	    echo json_encode($response, JSON_INVALID_UTF8_SUBSTITUTE);
    43	    exit;
    44	}
    45	
    46	try {
    47	    switch ($task) {
    48	        /* --------------------------------------------------------------
    49	         * LIST - Grid utama
    50	         * -------------------------------------------------------------- */
    51	        case 'list':
    52	            $start = (int)($_REQUEST['start'] ?? 0);
    53	            $limit = (int)($_REQUEST['limit'] ?? 250);
    54	            $query = trim($_REQUEST['query'] ?? '');
    55	
    56	            $where = '';
    57	            $params = [];
    58	            if ($query !== '') {
    59	                $where = "WHERE (expense_code ILIKE :q OR expense_name ILIKE :q OR category ILIKE :q)";
    60	                $params[':q'] = '%' . $query . '%';
    61	            }
    62	
    63	            $stmtCount = $db->prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM expense_master {$where}");
    64	            $stmtCount->execute($params);
    65	            $totalCount = (int)$stmtCount->fetchColumn();
    66	
    67	            $stmt = $db->prepare("
    68	                SELECT id, expense_code, expense_name, category, unit, default_price, active,
    69	                       created_by, created_at, updated_by, updated_at
    70	                FROM expense_master
    71	                {$where}
    72	                ORDER BY category, expense_name
    73	                LIMIT :limit OFFSET :start
    74	            ");
    75	            foreach ($params as $k => $v) $stmt->bindValue($k, $v);
    76	            $stmt->bindValue(':limit', $limit, PDO::PARAM_INT);
    77	            $stmt->bindValue(':start', $start, PDO::PARAM_INT);
    78	            $stmt->execute();
    79	
    80	            $rows = $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
    81	            foreach ($rows as &$r) {
    82	                $r['id'] = (int)$r['id'];
    83	                $r['default_price'] = (float)$r['default_price'];
    84	                $r['active'] = ($r['active'] === true || $r['active'] === 't' || $r['active'] === '1');
    85	                $r['created_by'] = $r['created_by'] ? (int)$r['created_by'] : null;
    86	                $r['updated_by'] = $r['updated_by'] ? (int)$r['updated_by'] : null;
    87	            }
    88	            unset($r);
    89	
    90	            exp_send(true, ['data' => $rows, 'totalCount' => $totalCount]);
    91	            break;
    92	
    93	        /* --------------------------------------------------------------
    94	         * READ_EDIT - Load data untuk form edit
    95	         * -------------------------------------------------------------- */
    96	        case 'read_edit':
    97	            $id = (int)($_REQUEST['id'] ?? 0);
    98	            if ($id <= 0) exp_send(false, [], 'ID expense tidak valid.');
    99	
   100	            $stmt = $db->prepare("
   101	                SELECT id, expense_code, expense_name, category, unit, default_price, active
   102	                FROM expense_master
   103	                WHERE id = :id
   104	            ");
   105	            $stmt->execute([':id' => $id]);
   106	            $row = $stmt->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
   107	
   108	            if (!$row) exp_send(false, [], 'Expense tidak ditemukan.');
   109	
   110	            $row['id'] = (int)$row['id'];
   111	            $row['default_price'] = (float)$row['default_price'];
   112	
   113	            exp_send(true, $row);
   114	            break;
   115	
   116	        /* --------------------------------------------------------------
   117	         * SAVE - Create / Update
   118	         * -------------------------------------------------------------- */
   119	        case 'save':
   120	        case 'create':
   121	            $id = (int)($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
   122	            exp_require_access($id > 0 ? 3 : 2, $id > 0
   123	                ? 'Anda tidak memiliki hak akses untuk mengubah Master Expense.'
   124	                : 'Anda tidak memiliki hak akses untuk menambah Master Expense.');
   125	            $code = trim((string)($_POST['expense_code'] ?? ''));
   126	            $name = trim((string)($_POST['expense_name'] ?? ''));
   127	            $category = trim((string)($_POST['category'] ?? ''));
   128	            $unit = trim((string)($_POST['unit'] ?? ''));
   129	            $price = round((float)($_POST['default_price'] ?? 0), 2);
   130	            $rawActive = $_POST['is_active'] ?? 'false';
   131	            $active = in_array((string)$rawActive, ['true', '1', 't', 'on'], true);
   132	
   133	            // Validasi
   134	            if ($code === '') exp_send(false, [], 'Kode Expense wajib diisi.');
   135	            if (strlen($code) > 30) exp_send(false, [], 'Kode Expense maksimal 30 karakter.');
   136	            if ($name === '') exp_send(false, [], 'Nama Expense wajib diisi.');
   137	            if (strlen($name) > 150) exp_send(false, [], 'Nama Expense maksimal 150 karakter.');
   138	            if ($category === '') exp_send(false, [], 'Kategori wajib dipilih.');
   139	            if ($unit === '') exp_send(false, [], 'Satuan wajib diisi.');
   140	            if ($price < 0) exp_send(false, [], 'Harga Satuan tidak boleh negatif.');
   141	
   142	            // Cek duplikasi kode (kecuali saat edit record yang sama)
   143	            $stmtChk = $db->prepare("SELECT id FROM expense_master WHERE expense_code = :code AND id <> :id");
   144	            $stmtChk->execute([':code' => $code, ':id' => $id]);
   145	            if ($stmtChk->fetchColumn()) {
   146	                exp_send(false, [], 'Kode Expense "' . $code . '" sudah digunakan.');
   147	            }
   148	
   149	            $userId = (int)($_SESSION['user_id'] ?? 0);
   150	
   151	            if ($id > 0) {
   152	                // UPDATE
   153	                $stmt = $db->prepare("
   154	                    UPDATE expense_master
   155	                    SET expense_code = :code,
   156	                        expense_name = :name,
   157	                        category = :category,
   158	                        unit = :unit,
   159	                        default_price = :price,
   160	                        active = :active,
   161	                        updated_by = :uid,
   162	                        updated_at = NOW()
   163	                    WHERE id = :id
   164	                ");
   165	                $stmt->execute([
   166	                    ':code' => $code, ':name' => $name, ':category' => $category,
   167	                    ':unit' => $unit, ':price' => $price, ':active' => $active ? 't' : 'f',
   168	                    ':uid' => $userId, ':id' => $id
   169	                ]);
   170	                exp_send(true, ['id' => $id, 'expense_code' => $code], 'Expense berhasil diupdate.');
   171	            } else {
   172	                // CREATE
   173	                $stmt = $db->prepare("
   174	                    INSERT INTO expense_master
   175	                    (expense_code, expense_name, category, unit, default_price, active, created_by)
   176	                    VALUES (:code, :name, :category, :unit, :price, :active, :uid)
   177	                    RETURNING id
   178	                ");
   179	                $stmt->execute([
   180	                    ':code' => $code, ':name' => $name, ':category' => $category,
   181	                    ':unit' => $unit, ':price' => $price, ':active' => $active ? 't' : 'f',
   182	                    ':uid' => $userId
   183	                ]);
   184	                $newId = (int)$stmt->fetchColumn();
   185	                exp_send(true, ['id' => $newId, 'expense_code' => $code], 'Expense baru berhasil disimpan.');
   186	            }
   187	            break;
   188	
   189	        /* --------------------------------------------------------------
   190	         * DELETE - Soft delete (set active = false)
   191	         * -------------------------------------------------------------- */
   192	        case 'delete':
   193	            exp_require_access(4, 'Anda tidak memiliki hak akses untuk menonaktifkan Master Expense.');
   194	            $id = (int)($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
   195	            if ($id <= 0) exp_send(false, [], 'ID expense tidak valid.');
   196	
   197	            $userId = (int)($_SESSION['user_id'] ?? 0);
   198	
   199	            $stmt = $db->prepare("
   200	                UPDATE expense_master
   201	                SET active = false, updated_by = :uid, updated_at = NOW()
   202	                WHERE id = :id
   203	            ");
   204	            $stmt->execute([':uid' => $userId, ':id' => $id]);
   205	
   206	            exp_send(true, [], 'Expense berhasil dinonaktifkan.');
   207	            break;
   208	
   209	        /* --------------------------------------------------------------
   210	         * LIST_OPTIONS - Untuk combo di RAB / modul lain
   211	         * -------------------------------------------------------------- */
   212	        case 'list_options':
   213	            $category = trim($_REQUEST['category'] ?? '');
   214	            $where = "WHERE active = true";
   215	            $params = [];
   216	            if ($category !== '' && $category !== 'All') {
   217	                $where .= " AND category = :cat";
   218	                $params[':cat'] = $category;
   219	            }
   220	            $stmt = $db->prepare("
   221	                SELECT id, expense_code, expense_name, category, unit, default_price
   222	                FROM expense_master
   223	                {$where}
   224	                ORDER BY category, expense_name
   225	            ");
   226	            $stmt->execute($params);
   227	            $rows = $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
   228	            foreach ($rows as &$r) {
   229	                $r['id'] = (int)$r['id'];
   230	                $r['default_price'] = (float)$r['default_price'];
   231	                $r['label'] = $r['expense_name'] . ' (' . $r['unit'] . ')';
   232	            }
   233	            unset($r);
   234	            exp_send(true, $rows);
   235	            break;
   236	
   237	        default:
   238	            exp_send(false, [], 'Task tidak dikenali: ' . htmlspecialchars($task));
   239	    }
   240	} catch (PDOException $e) {
   241	    error_log('[m.expensesCRD] PDO: ' . $e->getMessage());
   242	    exp_send(false, [], 'Database Error: ' . $e->getMessage());
   243	} catch (Throwable $e) {
   244	    error_log('[m.expensesCRD] ' . $e->getMessage());
   245	    exp_send(false, [], 'System Error: ' . $e->getMessage());
   246	}

===== 4. TAX RATE: SELURUH FILE =====
     1	<?php
     2	/**
     3	 * Document      : m.taxexchangerateCRD.php
     4	 * Description   : CRUD Backend Controller for Master Kurs Pajak Kemenkeu
     5	 */
     6	set_time_limit(0);
     7	if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_NONE) {
     8	    session_start();
     9	}
    10	date_default_timezone_set('Asia/Jakarta');
    11	
    12	header('Content-Type: application/json; charset=utf-8');
    13	
    14	// Validasi login dan hak akses form EXCHANGE RATE.
    15	require_once __DIR__ . '/../php-func/user.valid.php';
    16	
    17	// PDO untuk operasi data kurs.
    18	require_once __DIR__ . '/db_connection.PHP';
    19	
    20	$formAccess = (int)($_SESSION['FormAccess'] ?? 0);
    21	
    22	function taxExchangeRequireAccess($minimum, $message)
    23	{
    24	    $currentAccess = (int)($_SESSION['FormAccess'] ?? 0);
    25	
    26	    if ($currentAccess < $minimum) {
    27	        http_response_code(403);
    28	        echo json_encode([
    29	            'success' => false,
    30	            'message' => $message
    31	        ]);
    32	        exit;
    33	    }
    34	}
    35	
    36	$task   = $_POST['task'] ?? $_GET['task'] ?? 'read';
    37	if (in_array($task, ['fetch_now', 'sync', 'delete'], true)
    38	    && strtoupper($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] ?? 'GET') !== 'POST') {
    39	    http_response_code(405);
    40	    header('Allow: GET, POST');
    41	    echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Sinkronisasi dan penghapusan wajib menggunakan POST.']);
    42	    exit;
    43	}
    44	$start  = intval($_POST['start'] ?? $_REQUEST['start'] ?? 0);
    45	$limit  = intval($_POST['limit'] ?? $_REQUEST['limit'] ?? 250);
    46	$id     = intval($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
    47	
    48	switch ($task) {
    49	
    50	    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    51	    // 1. READ HEADER GRID (Daftar Periode KMK)
    52	    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    53	    case 'read':
    54	        try {
    55	            $stmtCount = $pdo->query("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tax_exchange_rate_hdr");
    56	            $totalCount = $stmtCount->fetchColumn();
    57	
    58	            $stmt = $pdo->prepare("
    59	                SELECT id, kmk_no, start_date, end_date, file_url, remark, created_at
    60	                FROM tax_exchange_rate_hdr
    61	                ORDER BY start_date DESC, id DESC
    62	                LIMIT :limit OFFSET :offset
    63	            ");
    64	            $stmt->bindValue(':limit', $limit, PDO::PARAM_INT);
    65	            $stmt->bindValue(':offset', $start, PDO::PARAM_INT);
    66	            $stmt->execute();
    67	            $data = $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
    68	
    69	            echo json_encode([
    70	                'success' => true,
    71	                'totalRecord' => $totalCount,
    72	                'data' => $data
    73	            ]);
    74	        } catch (Exception $e) {
    75	            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => $e->getMessage()]);
    76	        }
    77	        break;
    78	
    79	    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    80	    // 2. READ DETAIL GRID (Rincian Kurs per Mata Uang)
    81	    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    82	    case 'read_detail':
    83	        try {
    84	            $hdrId = intval($_POST['hdr_id'] ?? $_REQUEST['hdr_id'] ?? 0);
    85	            if ($hdrId <= 0) {
    86	                // Default ke header terbaru jika tidak spesifik
    87	                $hdrId = $pdo->query("SELECT id FROM tax_exchange_rate_hdr ORDER BY start_date DESC LIMIT 1")->fetchColumn();
    88	            }
    89	
    90	            if (!$hdrId) {
    91	                echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'data' => []]);
    92	                exit;
    93	            }
    94	
    95	            $stmt = $pdo->prepare("
    96	                SELECT 
    97	                    d.id, d.hdr_id, d.currency_id, d.currency_code, d.currency_name, 
    98	                    d.unit_multiplier, d.rate AS rate_kmk, d.rate_per_unit AS rate_effective,
    99	                    d.prev_rate, d.change_amount, d.change_direction
   100	                FROM tax_exchange_rate_dtl d
   101	                WHERE d.hdr_id = :hdr_id
   102	                ORDER BY d.currency_code ASC
   103	            ");
   104	            $stmt->execute(['hdr_id' => $hdrId]);
   105	            $data = $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
   106	
   107	            echo json_encode([
   108	                'success' => true,
   109	                'hdr_id' => $hdrId,
   110	                'data' => $data
   111	            ]);
   112	        } catch (Exception $e) {
   113	            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => $e->getMessage()]);
   114	        }
   115	        break;
   116	
   117	    // ------------------------------------------------------------
   118	    // 3. FETCH NOW (Tarik Otomatis dari Kemenkeu)
   119	    // ------------------------------------------------------------
   120	    case 'fetch_now':
   121	    case 'sync':
   122	        taxExchangeRequireAccess(
   123	            3,
   124	            'Anda tidak memiliki hak untuk memperbarui Kurs Pajak.'
   125	        );
   126	        try {
   127	            require_once __DIR__ . '/../cron/fetch_kurs_pajak.php';
   128	            $res = fetchAndStoreTaxExchangeRates($pdo);
   129	
   130	            if ($res['success']) {
   131	                echo json_encode([
   132	                    'success' => true,
   133	                    'message' => "Berhasil memperbarui Kurs Pajak! KMK: {$res['kmk_no']} ({$res['period']}) - Total {$res['items_count']} mata uang."
   134	                ]);
   135	            } else {
   136	                echo json_encode([
   137	                    'success' => false,
   138	                    'message' => $res['message']
   139	                ]);
   140	            }
   141	        } catch (Exception $e) {
   142	            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => "Error sync: " . $e->getMessage()]);
   143	        }
   144	        break;
   145	
   146	    // ------------------------------------------------------------
   147	    // 4. DELETE RECORD
   148	    // ------------------------------------------------------------
   149	    case 'delete':
   150	        taxExchangeRequireAccess(
   151	            4,
   152	            'Anda tidak memiliki hak untuk menghapus Kurs Pajak.'
   153	        );
   154	        try {
   155	            if ($id <= 0) {
   156	                echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'ID data tidak valid.']);
   157	                exit;
   158	            }
   159	            $stmt = $pdo->prepare("DELETE FROM tax_exchange_rate_hdr WHERE id = :id");
   160	            $stmt->execute(['id' => $id]);
   161	
   162	            echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'message' => 'Data Kurs Pajak berhasil dihapus.']);
   163	        } catch (Exception $e) {
   164	            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => $e->getMessage()]);
   165	        }
   166	        break;
   167	
   168	    default:
   169	        echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Task tidak dikenali.']);
   170	        break;
   171	}

===== 5. USER VALID: AUTENTIKASI DAN SAME-ORIGIN =====
     1	<?php
     2	/**
     3	 * Hardened legacy menu validation.
     4	 * Keeps the existing PostgreSQL access lookup and sidebar generation.
     5	 */
     6	if (!defined('BASE_PATH')) {
     7	    define('BASE_PATH', dirname(__DIR__));
     8	}
     9	require_once __DIR__ . '/app.url.php';
    10	
    11	if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_NONE) {
    12	    session_start();
    13	}
    14	
    15	include_once __DIR__ . '/function.all.php';
    16	include_once __DIR__ . '/../php-class/crudClass.php';
    17	
    18	$requestPath = parse_url(isset($_SERVER['REQUEST_URI']) ? $_SERVER['REQUEST_URI'] : '/', PHP_URL_PATH);
    19	$urlFile = basename($requestPath);
    20	
    21	global $conn, $dbconn;
    22	if (empty($conn)) {
    23	    if (!empty($dbconn)) {
    24	        $conn = $dbconn;
    25	    } elseif (!empty($GLOBALS['dbconn'])) {
    26	        $conn = $GLOBALS['dbconn'];
    27	    } elseif (!empty($GLOBALS['conn'])) {
    28	        $conn = $GLOBALS['conn'];
    29	    }
    30	}
    31	
    32	$isAjax = (
    33	    (!empty($_SERVER['HTTP_X_REQUESTED_WITH']) &&
    34	     strtolower($_SERVER['HTTP_X_REQUESTED_WITH']) === 'xmlhttprequest')
    35	    || (!empty($_SERVER['HTTP_ACCEPT']) &&
    36	        strpos($_SERVER['HTTP_ACCEPT'], 'application/json') !== false)
    37	);
    38	/*
    39	if (empty($_SESSION['user_id']) || !is_numeric($_SESSION['user_id'])
    40	    || intval($_SESSION['user_id']) <= 0) {
    41	    if ($isAjax) {
    42	        http_response_code(401);
    43	        header('Content-Type: application/json; charset=utf-8');
    44	        echo json_encode(array('success' => false, 'message' => 'Session telah berakhir. Silakan login kembali.'));
    45	        exit;
    46	    }
    47	    header('Location: ' . rtrim(BASE_URL, '/') . '/index.php');
    48	    exit;
    49	}*/
    50	
    51	if (empty($_SESSION['user_id']) || !is_numeric($_SESSION['user_id'])
    52	|| intval($_SESSION['user_id']) <= 0) {
    53	    
    54	    // Deteksi request type
    55	    $isJsonRequest = (!empty($_SERVER['HTTP_ACCEPT']) && strpos($_SERVER['HTTP_ACCEPT'], 'application/json') !== false);
    56	    $isFetchRequest = (!empty($_SERVER['HTTP_X_REQUESTED_WITH']) && strtolower($_SERVER['HTTP_X_REQUESTED_WITH']) === 'xmlhttprequest');
    57	    $hasActionParam = (isset($_GET['action']) || isset($_POST['action']) || isset($_POST['task']));
    58	    
    59	    // Untuk AJAX/Fetch/API request -> return JSON

     7	date_default_timezone_set('Asia/Jakarta');
     8	
     9	include_once "../php-class/crudClass.php";
    10	include_once "../php-func/function.all.php";
    11	
    12	// Tangkap task sebelum pemeriksaan izin operasi.
    13	$task = $_POST['task'] ?? $_GET['task'] ?? '';
    14	
    15	if (in_array($task, ['create', 'add', 'update', 'edit', 'delete'], true)
    16	    && strtoupper($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] ?? 'GET') !== 'POST') {
    17	    http_response_code(405);
    18	    header('Allow: GET, POST');
    19	    echo json_encode(["success" => false, "status" => "Error", "message" => "Operasi perubahan akun wajib menggunakan POST."]);
    20	    exit;
    21	}
    22	
    23	// Defense-in-depth: endpoint must have a valid login and a menu permission.
    24	// Levels follow the application convention: 1=read, 2=create, 3=update, 4=delete/admin.
    25	if (empty($_SESSION['user_id']) || !is_numeric($_SESSION['user_id']) || (int)$_SESSION['user_id'] <= 0) {
    26	    http_response_code(401);
    27	    echo json_encode(["success" => false, "status" => "Error", "message" => "Sesi berakhir. Silakan login kembali."]);
    28	    exit;
    29	}
    30	$userAccess = (int)($_SESSION['FormAccess'] ?? 0);
    31	$requiredAccess = 1;
    32	if (in_array($task ?? '', ['create', 'add'], true)) $requiredAccess = 2;
    33	if (in_array($task ?? '', ['update', 'edit'], true)) $requiredAccess = 3;
    34	if (($task ?? '') === 'delete') $requiredAccess = 4;
    35	
    36	// Assigning or changing other users' menu permissions is an administrator operation.
    37	$permissionPayload = $_POST['form_access'] ?? $_POST['arrForm'] ?? null;
    38	if (in_array($task ?? '', ['create', 'add', 'update', 'edit'], true) && !empty($permissionPayload)) {
    39	    $requiredAccess = 4;
    40	}
    41	if ($userAccess < $requiredAccess) {
    42	    http_response_code(403);
    43	    echo json_encode(["success" => false, "status" => "Error", "message" => "Hak akses tidak mencukupi untuk operasi ini."]);
    44	    exit;
    45	}
    46

    48	$offset      = isset($_POST["start"]) ? intval($_POST["start"]) : 0;
    49	$limit       = isset($_POST["limit"]) ? intval($_POST["limit"]) : 250;
    50

    52	$dataArray     = json2Array($itemInfoParam);
    53	$CheckItemNum  = (empty($dataArray)) ? "[0]" : "[" . $dataArray . "]";
    54	$CheckItemChar = (empty($dataArray)) ? "['0']" : "[" . $dataArray . "]";
    55	
    56	$db   = new crud();
    57	
    58	switch ($task) {
    59	
    60	    // ==========================================
    61	    // 1. READ MAIN GRID (Daftar Master User System)
    62	    // ==========================================
    63	    case ('read'):
    64	        $sResult   = searchData($searchField, array("s.login_name", "e.employee_name"), 250);
    65	        $limit     = $sResult["limit"];
    66	        $searchSQL = $sResult["searchSQL"];
    67

    69	            s.id, s.login_name, s.employee_id, s.active,
    70	            '' as password, '' as confirm_password,
    71	            e.employee_name, e.gender, e.from_date,

    74	        ";
    75	
    76	        $table = "
    77	            m_user_system s
    78	            left join m_employee e on e.id = s.employee_id
    79	            left join view_employee_depart_status d on coalesce(d.id, 0) = coalesce(e.id, 0)
    80	        ";
    81	
    82	        $where = "1=1 " . $searchSQL;
    83	        $order = "e.employee_name ASC";
    84	
    85	        $db->select($table, $fields, $where, $order, $limit, $offset);
    86	        echo $db->getResult();
    87	        break;
    88	
    89	    // ==========================================
    90	    // 2. READ DETAIL GRID (Rincian Hak Akses Menu User)
    91	    // ==========================================
    92	    case ("read_detail"):
    93	        $id = intval($_POST['id'] ?? $_POST['user_id'] ?? $_REQUEST['id'] ?? $_REQUEST['user_id'] ?? 0);
    94	
    95	        $fields = "
    96	            u.id, u.form_id, u.access_level, u.user_id,
    97	            fr.group_id, fr.seq, coalesce(fr.title,'') form_name, coalesce(fr.remark,'') remark,
    98	            g.name as group_name, g.seq as group_seq, 'true' as active
    99	        ";
   100	
   101	        $table = "
   102	            m_user_access_menu u
   103	            left join m_form_name fr on fr.id = u.form_id
   104	            left join (
   105	                select id, name, coalesce(remark,'') remark, seq
   106	                from (

   108	                    from m_form_group d
   109	                    left join (
   110	                        select name, seq from m_form_group where substring(seq,3,2)='00'
   111	                    ) h on substring(h.seq,1,2)=substring(d.seq,1,2)
   112	                    where substring(d.seq,3,2)<>'00' and substring(d.seq,1,2)<>'00')
   113	                    union
   114	                    (select h.id, h.name, h.remark, seq
   115	                    from m_form_group h
   116	                    where substring(seq,3,2)='00' and substring(seq,1,2)<>'00')
   117	                ) data
   118	                order by seq
   119	            ) g on g.id = fr.group_id
   120	        ";
   121	
   122	        $where = "u.user_id = " . $id;
   123	        $order = "g.seq ASC, fr.seq ASC";
   124	
   125	        $db->select($table, $fields, $where, $order);
   126	        echo $db->getResult();
   127	        break;
   128

   130	    // 3. READ EDIT (Load Single Record untuk Dialog Edit)
   131	    // ==========================================
   132	    case ("read_edit"):
   133	        $id = intval($_POST['id'] ?? $_REQUEST['id'] ?? 0);
   134	
   135	        $fields = "
   136	            s.id, s.login_name, s.employee_id, s.employee_id as name, s.active,
   137	            '' as password, '' as confirm_password,
   138	            e.employee_name, e.gender, e.from_date, 
   139	            d.department_name, d.status_name
   140	        ";
   141	
   142	        $table = "
   143	            m_user_system s
   144	            left join m_employee e on e.id = s.employee_id
   145	            left join view_employee_depart_status d on coalesce(d.id, 0) = coalesce(e.id, 0)
   146	        ";
   147	
   148	        $where = "s.id = " . $id;
   149	
   150	        $db->select($table, $fields, $where);
   151	        echo $db->getResult();
   152	        break;
   153	
   154	    // ==========================================
   155	    // 4. ADD FORM (Load Daftar Form Pilihan)
   156	    // ==========================================
   157	    case ('addform'):
   158	        $field = "f.id form_id, g.name as group_name, f.title form_name, f.remark, 1 as access_level, 'true' as active";
   159	        $cleanItemNum = str_replace("'", "", $CheckItemNum);
   160	
   161	        $table = "
   162	            m_form_name f
   163	            left join (
   164	                select id, name, coalesce(remark,'') remark, seq
   165	                from (
   166	                    (select d.id, h.name||' -- '||d.name as name, d.remark, d.seq
   167	                    from m_form_group d
   168	                    left join (
   169	                        select name, seq from m_form_group where substring(seq,3,2)='00'
   170	                    ) h on substring(h.seq,1,2)=substring(d.seq,1,2)
   171	                    where substring(d.seq,3,2)<>'00' and substring(d.seq,1,2)<>'00')
   172	                    union
   173	                    (select h.id, h.name, h.remark, seq
   174	                    from m_form_group h
   175	                    where substring(seq,3,2)='00' and substring(seq,1,2)<>'00')
   176	                ) data
   177	            ) g on g.id = f.group_id
   178	        ";
   179	
   180	        $where = "f.id <> all (array" . $cleanItemNum . ")";
   181	        // Search text is a value, never a client-supplied SQL fragment.
   182	        $formSearch = searchData($searchField, ["f.title", "f.remark", "g.name"], 250);
   183	        if (!empty($formSearch["searchSQL"])) {
   184	            $where .= $formSearch["searchSQL"];
   185	        }
   186

   188	
   189	        $db->select($table, $field, $where, $orderBy);
   190	        $result = $db->getResult();
   191	        
   192	        if (empty($result) || trim($result) == "") {
   193	            echo json_encode(array("totalRecord" => 0, "data" => array()));
   194	        } else {
   195	            echo $result;
   196	        }
   197	        break;
   198

   200	    // 5. CREATE (Tambah User Baru)
   201	    // ==========================================
   202	    case ('create'):
   203	    case ('add'):
   204	        $employeeId = intval($_POST["employee_id"] ?? $_POST["name"] ?? 0);
   205	
   206	        if ($employeeId <= 0) {
   207	            echo json_encode([
   208	                "success" => "false", 
   209	                "status"  => "Error", 
   210	                "message" => "Gagal: Pilih Karyawan (Name) yang valid terlebih dahulu!"
   211	            ]);
   212	            exit;
   213	        }
   214	
   215	        $rawPassword = trim($_POST["password"] ?? '');
   216	        $hashedPassword = !empty($rawPassword) ? password_hash($rawPassword, PASSWORD_DEFAULT, ['cost' => 12]) : '';
   217	
   218	        $queryC = '';
   219	        $table  = "m_user_system";
   220	        $data   = array(
   221	            "login_name"  => checksinglequote($_POST["login_name"] ?? ''),
   222	            "employee_id" => $employeeId,
   223	            "password"    => $hashedPassword,
   224	            "active"      => ($_POST["u_active"] === 'true' || $_POST["active"] === 'true') ? 'true' : 'false'
   225	        );
   226	        $queryC .= insertQ($table, $data) . ";";
   227	
   228	        $table = "m_user_login_status";
   229	        $data  = array("user_id" => "currval('m_user_system_id_seq'::regclass)");
   230	        $queryC .= insertQ($table, $data) . ";";
   231	
   232	        $formAccessPayload = $_POST['form_access'] ?? $_POST['arrForm'] ?? null;
   233	
   234	        if (!empty($formAccessPayload)) {
   235	            $cleanJson = stripslashes($formAccessPayload);
   236	            $dataForm  = json_decode($cleanJson, true);
   237	
   238	            if (is_array($dataForm) && count($dataForm) > 0) {
   239	                foreach ($dataForm as $row) {
   240	                    $formId    = intval($row['form_id'] ?? 0);
   241	                    $accessLvl = intval($row['access_level'] ?? 1);
   242	                    if ($accessLvl < 0 || $accessLvl > 4) {
   243	                        http_response_code(400);
   244	                        echo json_encode(["success" => false, "status" => "Error", "message" => "Tingkat hak akses harus 0 sampai 4."]);
   245	                        exit;
   246	                    }
   247	
   248	                    if ($formId > 0) {
   249	                        $tableD = "m_user_access_menu";
   250	                        $dataD  = array(
   251	                            "user_id"      => "currval('m_user_system_id_seq'::regclass)",
   252	                            "form_id"      => $formId,
   253	                            "access_level" => $accessLvl
   254	                        );
   255	                        $queryC .= insertQ($tableD, $dataD) . ";";
   256	                    }
   257	                }
   258	            }
   259	        }
   260	
   261	        $db->cmdQ($queryC);
   262	        echo $db->getResult();
   263	        break;
   264

   267	    // ==========================================

   269	    case ('edit'):
   270	        $queryC = '';
   271	        $userId = intval($_POST['user_id'] ?? $_POST['id'] ?? 0);
   272	        $employeeId = intval($_POST["employee_id"] ?? $_POST["name"] ?? 0);
   273	
   274	        if ($userId > 0) {
   275	            if ($employeeId <= 0) {
   276	                $checkStmt = $db->prepare("SELECT employee_id FROM m_user_system WHERE id = :id");
   277	                $checkStmt->execute(['id' => $userId]);
   278	                $existRow = $checkStmt->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
   279	                if ($existRow && !empty($existRow['employee_id'])) {
   280	                    $employeeId = intval($existRow['employee_id']);
   281	                }
   282	            }
   283	
   284	            if ($employeeId <= 0) {
   285	                echo json_encode([
   286	                    "success" => "false",

   289	                ]);
   290	                exit;
   291	            }
   292	
   293	            $table = "m_user_system";
   294	            $data  = array(
   295	                "login_name"  => checksinglequote($_POST["login_name"] ?? ''),
   296	                "employee_id" => $employeeId,
   297	                "active"      => ($_POST["u_active"] === 'true' || $_POST["u_active"] === true || $_POST["active"] === 'true') ? 'true' : 'false'
   298	            );
   299

    12

    17	    $offset     = (empty($startParam)) ? 0 : intval($startParam);
    18	
    19	    $queryParam  = $_POST["query"] ?? '';
    20	    $sResult     = searchData($queryParam, array("dt.title", "dt.php_url", "dt.js_url", "g.name"), 100);
    21	    $limit       = isset($sResult["limit"]) ? intval($sResult["limit"]) : 100;
    22	    $searchField = isset($sResult["searchSQL"]) ? $sResult["searchSQL"] : '';
    23

    26	    $dataArray = '';
    27	    if (!empty($itemInfoParam)) {
    28	        $decodedInfo = json_decode($itemInfoParam, true);
    29	        if (is_array($decodedInfo)) {

    31	        }

    67	                "remark"        => checksinglequote($_POST["remark"] ?? ''),
    68	                "active"           => $_POST["_active"] ?? null,
    69	
    70	            );
    71	            $queryC .= insertQ($table, $data) . ";";
    72	
    73	            if (is_array($dataForm)) {
    74	                foreach ($dataForm as $form) {

    81	                }

    86	            echo $db->getResult();

    98	                        coalesce(fr.php_url,'') php_url, coalesce(fr.js_url,'') js_url,
    99	                        coalesce(fr.remark,'') remark, tna, active
   100	                    from m_form_name fr

   102	                left join (

   104	                    from (
