   153	                        (select d.id, h.name||' -- '||d.name as name, d.remark, d.seq
   154	                        from m_form_group d
   155	                        left join (
   156	                            select name, seq from m_form_group where substring(seq,3,2)='00'
   157	                        ) h on substring(h.seq,1,2)=substring(d.seq,1,2)
   158	                        where substring(d.seq,3,2)<>'00' and substring(d.seq,1,2)<>'00')
   159	                        union
   160	                        (select h.id, h.name, h.remark, seq
   161	                        from m_form_group h
   162	                        where substring(seq,3,2)='00' and substring(seq,1,2)<>'00')
   163	                    ) data
   164	                ) g on g.id = f.group_id
   165	            ";
   166	            $where = "l.form_id = " . $formId;
   167	            $order = "g.name ASC, f.seq ASC";
   168	
   169	            $db->select($table, $field, $where, $order);
   170	            echo $db->getResult();
   171	            break;
   172	
   173	        case 'addform':
   174	            $db = new crud();
   175	            $field = "f.id link_form_id, g.name as group_name, f.title, f.remark";
   176	            $table = "
   177	                m_form_name f
   178	                left join (
   179	                    select id, name, coalesce(remark,'') remark, seq
   180	                    from (
   181	                        (select d.id, h.name||' -- '||d.name as name, d.remark, d.seq
   182	                        from m_form_group d
   183	                        left join (
   184	                            select name, seq from m_form_group where substring(seq,3,2)='00'
   185	                        ) h on substring(h.seq,1,2)=substring(d.seq,1,2)
   186	                        where substring(d.seq,3,2)<>'00' and substring(d.seq,1,2)<>'00')
   187	                        union
   188	                        (select h.id, h.name, h.remark, seq
   189	                        from m_form_group h
   190	                        where substring(seq,3,2)='00' and substring(seq,1,2)<>'00')
   191	                    ) data
   192	                ) g on g.id = f.group_id
   193	            ";
   194	            
   195	            $where = "f.id NOT IN (" . $cleanItemNum . ")";
   196	            if (!empty($searchField) && trim($searchField) != "") {
   197	                $where .= " " . $searchField;
   198	            }
   199	            $order = "g.name ASC, f.seq ASC";
   200	
   201	            $db->select($table, $field, $where, $order);
   202	            echo $db->getResult();
   203	            break;
   204	
   205	        case 'HistoryData':
   206	            $db = new crud();
   207	            $id = intval($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
   208	            $table = "view_single_table_log";
   209	            $field = "
   210	                tx_id, date, count,
   211	                case amend_name
   212	                    when 'group_id' then '[EDIT] - Group'
   213	                    when 'seq' then '[EDIT] - Seq'
   214	                    when 'title' then '[EDIT] - Title'
   215	                    when 'php_url' then '[EDIT] - PHP URL'
   216	                    when 'js_url' then '[EDIT] - JS URL'
   217	                    when 'remark' then '[EDIT] - Remark'
   218	                    else amend_name
   219	                end ::character varying(200) AS amend_name,
   220	                CASE
   221	                    WHEN amend_name = 'group_id'
   222	                        then (select name from view_form_group where id::int = before_amendment::int)
   223	                    else before_amendment
   224	                end as before_amendment,
   225	                CASE
   226	                    WHEN amend_name = 'group_id'
   227	                        then (select name from view_form_group where id::int = after_amendment::int)
   228	                    else after_amendment
   229	                end as after_amendment,
   230	                user_name, user_time
   231	            ";
   232	            $where = "tx_id::int = " . $id . " and table_name = 'm_form_name'";
   233	            $orderBy = "count desc";
   234	
   235	            $db->select('history', $table, $field, $where, $orderBy);
   236	            echo $db->getResult();
   237	            break;
   238	
   239	        case 'update':
   240	            $queryC = '';
   241	            $formId = intval($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
   242	            $rawForm = $_POST["arrForm"] ?? '[]';
   243	            $dataForm = !empty($rawForm) ? json_decode($rawForm) : [];
   244	
   245	            // Memeriksa dan membersihkan relasi form_links lama
   246	            $dbCheck = new crud();
   247	            $dbCheck->select('m_form_links', 'id, form_id', "form_id = " . $formId, "id ASC");
   248	            $resCheckJSON = $dbCheck->getResult();
   249	            $resCheckArr = json_decode($resCheckJSON, true);
   250	            $dataCheck = $resCheckArr["data"] ?? [];
   251	
   252	            for ($c = 0; $c < count($dataCheck); $c++) {
   253	                $dataFound = 0;
   254	                $cId = $dataCheck[$c]['id'];
   255	                for ($a = 0; $a < count($dataForm); $a++) {
   256	                    if (isset($dataForm[$a]->link_id) && $dataForm[$a]->link_id == $cId) {
   257	                        $dataFound = 1;
   258	                        break;
   259	                    }
   260	                }

===== 8. FORM GROUP: IMPLEMENTASI =====
     1	<?php
     2	require_once __DIR__ . '/../php-func/user.valid.php';
     3	if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_NONE) {
     4	    session_start();
     5	}
     6	date_default_timezone_set('Asia/Jakarta');
     7	
     8	include_once "../php-class/crudClass.php";
     9	include_once "../php-func/function.all.php";
    10	
    11	// 1. Ambil parameter pencarian & paging
    12	$startParam  = $_POST["start"] ?? 0;
    13	$offset      = ($startParam == null) ? 0 : intval($startParam);
    14	
    15	$queryParam  = $_POST["query"] ?? '';
    16	$sResult     = searchData($queryParam, array("seq", "name", "remark"), 100);
    17	$limit       = $sResult["limit"] ?? 100;
    18	$searchField = $sResult["searchSQL"] ?? '';
    19	
    20	$task  = $_POST['task'] ?? '';
    21	if (empty($_SESSION['user_id']) || !is_numeric($_SESSION['user_id']) || (int)$_SESSION['user_id'] <= 0) {
    22	    http_response_code(401);
    23	    echo json_encode(["success" => false, "message" => "Sesi berakhir. Silakan login kembali."]);
    24	    exit;
    25	}
    26	$formAdminAccess = (int)($_SESSION['FormAccess'] ?? 0);
    27	$formRequiredAccess = 1;
    28	if (in_array($task, ['create', 'add', 'update', 'edit', 'delete'], true)) $formRequiredAccess = 4;
    29	if ($formAdminAccess < $formRequiredAccess) {
    30	    http_response_code(403);
    31	    echo json_encode(["success" => false, "message" => "Hak akses tidak mencukupi untuk pengelolaan menu."]);
    32	    exit;
    33	}
    34	
    35	$db    = new crud();
    36	$table = "m_form_group";
    37	
    38	switch ($task) {
    39	
    40	    // ==========================================
    41	    // 1. READ (Ambil Data untuk Grid / List)
    42	    // ==========================================
    43	    case ('read'):
    44	        $sResult   = searchData($queryParam, array("seq", "name", "remark"), 250);
    45	        $limit     = $sResult["limit"];
    46	        $searchSQL = $sResult["searchSQL"];
    47	
    48	        $fields = "id, seq, name as grp_name, coalesce(remark,'') remark, coalesce(active, true) as active";
    49	        $where  = "1=1 " . $searchSQL;
    50	        $order  = "seq ASC, name ASC";
    51	
    52	        // Pemanggilan yang BENAR: (tabel, fields, where, order, limit, offset)
    53	        $db->select($table, $fields, $where, $order, $limit, $offset);
    54	        echo $db->getResult();
    55	    break;
    56	
    57	    // ==========================================
    58	    // 2. READ EDIT (Ambil Data Berdasarkan ID)
    59	    // ==========================================
    60	    case ('read_edit'):
    61	        $id     = intval($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
    62	        $fields = "id, seq, name as grp_name, coalesce(remark,'') remark, coalesce(active, true) as active";
    63	        $where  = "id = " . $id;
    64	
    65	        $db->select($table, $fields, $where);
    66	        echo $db->getResult();
    67	    break;
    68	
    69	    // ==========================================
    70	    // 3. HISTORY DATA (Log Perubahan)
    71	    // ==========================================
    72	    case ('HistoryData'):
    73	        $id     = intval($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
    74	        $fields = "
    75	            tx_id, date, count,
    76	            case amend_name
    77	                when 'seq' then '[EDIT] - Sequence'
    78	                when 'name' then '[EDIT] - Name'
    79	                when 'remark' then '[EDIT] - Remark'
    80	                else amend_name
    81	            end ::character varying(200) AS amend_name,
    82	            before_amendment,
    83	            after_amendment,
    84	            user_name, user_time
    85	        ";
    86	        $where   = "tx_id::int = " . $id . " and table_name = '" . $table . "'";
    87	        $orderBy = "count desc";
    88	
    89	        $db->select("view_single_table_log", $fields, $where, $orderBy);
    90	        echo $db->getResult();
    91	    break;
    92	
    93	    // ==========================================
    94	    // 4. CREATE / ADD
    95	    // ==========================================
    96	    case ('create'):
    97	    case ('add'):
    98	        $seq    = $_POST['seq'] ?? '';
    99	        $name   = $_POST['grp_name'] ?? $_POST['name'] ?? '';
   100	        $remark = $_POST['remark'] ?? '';
   101	        // PDO pgsql tidak bisa bind boolean PHP native -- wajib string 't'/'f'.
   102	        $active = ($_POST['active'] ?? 'true') === 'true' ? 't' : 'f';
   103	
   104	        if (!empty($name)) {
   105	            $data = array(
   106	                "seq"    => checksinglequote($seq),
   107	                "name"   => checksinglequote($name),
   108	                "remark" => checksinglequote($remark),
   109	                "active" => $active
   110	            );
   111	
   112	            if ($db->insert($table, $data)) {
   113	                echo $db->getResult();
   114	            } else {
   115	                echo $db->getResult();
   116	            }
   117	        } else {
   118	            echo json_encode(array(
   119	                "success" => "false",
   120	                "status"  => "Error",
   121	                "message" => "Nama Group wajib diisi."
   122	            ));
   123	        }
   124	    break;
   125	
   126	    // ==========================================
   127	    // 5. UPDATE / EDIT
   128	    // ==========================================
   129	    case ('update'):
   130	    case ('edit'):
   131	        $formId = intval($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
   132	        $seq    = $_POST['seq'] ?? '';
   133	        $name   = $_POST['grp_name'] ?? $_POST['name'] ?? '';
   134	        $remark = $_POST['remark'] ?? '';
   135	        // PDO pgsql tidak bisa bind boolean PHP native -- wajib string 't'/'f'.
   136	        $active = ($_POST['active'] ?? 'true') === 'true' ? 't' : 'f';
   137	
   138	        if ($formId > 0) {
   139	            $data = array(
   140	                "seq"    => checksinglequote($seq),
   141	                "name"   => checksinglequote($name),
   142	                "remark" => checksinglequote($remark),
   143	                "active" => $active
   144	            );
   145	            $where = "id = " . $formId;
   146	
   147	            if ($db->update($table, $data, $where)) {
   148	                echo $db->getResult();
   149	            } else {
   150	                echo $db->getResult();
   151	            }
   152	        } else {
   153	            echo json_encode(array(
   154	                "success" => "false",
   155	                "status"  => "Error",
   156	                "message" => "ID tidak valid untuk proses update."
   157	            ));
   158	        }
   159	    break;
   160	
   161	    // ==========================================
   162	    // 6. DELETE
   163	    // ==========================================
   164	    case ('delete'):
   165	        $deleteId = intval($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
   166	
   167	        if ($deleteId > 0) {
   168	            $where  = "id = " . $deleteId;
   169	            $queryC = deleteQ($table, $where) . ";";
   170	
   171	            $db->cmdQ($queryC);
   172	            echo $db->getResult();
   173	        } else {
   174	            echo json_encode(array(
   175	                "success" => "false",
   176	                "status"  => "Error",
   177	                "message" => "ID tidak valid untuk proses penghapusan."
   178	            ));
   179	        }
   180	    break;
   181	
   182	    default:
   183	        echo json_encode(array(
   184	            "success" => "false",
   185	            "status"  => "Error",
   186	            "message" => "Task atau perintah tidak dikenali."
   187	        ));
   188	    break;
   189	}
   190	?>
